### modules/services/src/main/scala/scholarchain/services/Composition.scala [CHANGED BY repair-01 Part A]
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.core.config.{ConfigLoader, ScholarChainConfig}
     4	import scholarchain.rag.live.BedrockHaikuClient
     5	import scholarchain.rag.runtime.{
     6	  PromptSet,
     7	  RuntimeMode,
     8	  RuntimePorts,
     9	  RuntimeSetup,
    10	  WindowSummarySource,
    11	  Workflow
    12	}
    13	import scholarchain.rag.{EmbeddingModels, Templates}
    14	import scholarchain.storage.cli.{Args, Cli}
    15	import scholarchain.storage.live.{
    16	  BedrockTitanEmbedder,
    17	  DynamoEvidenceLookup,
    18	  DynamoServiceStateTable,
    19	  DynamoStore,
    20	  LiveAws,
    21	  SqsJobQueue,
    22	  LiveTarget,
    23	  NeptuneGraphReader,
    24	  S3ObjectStore,
    25	  S3VectorStore
    26	}
    27	import scholarchain.storage.{
    28	  EvidenceLookupPort,
    29	  FileObjectStore,
    30	  FileRuntimeRecords,
    31	  FileVectorStore,
    32	  GraphReadLimits,
    33	  HashingEmbedder,
    34	  IdempotencyStore,
    35	  InMemoryGraph,
    36	  InMemoryJobQueue,
    37	  ItemFileReader,
    38	  JobQueuePort,
    39	  JobStore,
    40	  LocalServiceStateTable,
    41	  ObjectReadPort,
    42	  RetryPolicy,
    43	  S3Layout,
    44	  Throttle,
    45	  WindowSummaries
    46	}
    47	
    48	import java.nio.file.{Path, Paths}
    49	import java.time.Instant
    50	
    51	/** Everything a service needs from rag and storage, wired once per process. jobs and queue carry
    52	  * the async path (ruling 7): query-api creates jobs and sends messages, the worker runs them.
    53	  */
    54	final case class Backend(
    55	    setup: RuntimeSetup,
    56	    ports: RuntimePorts,
    57	    evidence: EvidenceLookupPort,
    58	    idempotency: IdempotencyStore,
    59	    jobs: JobStore,
    60	    queue: JobQueuePort
    61	)
    62	
    63	/** The local store files of fake mode, in the AskCli layout (graph and items under
    64	  * snapshot=<date>/run=<runId>/); outputs (audits, runtime records, service state) go under out.
    65	  * windowSummaries is the object store root holding the corpus window summaries (read only;
    66	  * default: objects).
    67	  */
    68	final case class FakeStores(
    69	    graph: Path,
    70	    items: Path,
    71	    objects: Path,
    72	    vectors: Path,
    73	    out: Path,
    74	    windowSummaries: Option[Path] = None
    75	)
    76	
    77	/** The served runs of live mode (inputs of the runtime run ID, RUNTIME.md section 14). */
    78	final case class LiveRun(
    79	    target: LiveTarget,
    80	    snapshot: String,
    81	    graphRun: String,
    82	    itemsRun: String,
    83	    vectorsRun: String
    84	)
    85	
    86	/** The runtime composition root (ruling 13). Fake mode: the Phase 7 local stores, the rule planner
    87	  * and template synthesizer, the hashing embedder; no AWS call. Live mode (never started by an
    88	  * agent): Neptune, DynamoDB, S3, and S3 Vectors read through the read-only port sides (Phase 7
    89	  * M6), Titan for the query embedding, and Haiku only when services.stub-llm is false.
    90	  */
    91	object Composition:
    92	  private val monotonic: () => Long = () => Throttle.monotonicMs()
    93	
    94	  /** answers false (graph-evidence-api) never opens the window-summary object (repair-01 m3). */
    95	  def fake(
    96	      cfg: ScholarChainConfig,
    97	      s: FakeStores,
    98	      out: String => Unit,
    99	      answers: Boolean = true
   100	  ): Either[String, Backend] =
   101	    for
   102	      model <- EmbeddingModels.forId(cfg.models.embeddingModel)
   103	      prompts <- loadPrompts(cfg)
   104	      graphSeg <- Cli.runSegments(s.graph).toRight(s"${s.graph} has no snapshot=/run= segments")
   105	      itemsSeg <- Cli.runSegments(s.items).toRight(s"${s.items} has no snapshot=/run= segments")
   106	      summaries <- windowSummaries(
   107	        cfg,
   108	        graphSeg._1,
   109	        FileObjectStore(s.windowSummaries.getOrElse(s.objects)).reader,
   110	        out,
   111	        answers
   112	      )
   113	      graph = InMemoryGraph()
   114	      objects = FileObjectStore(s.objects)
   115	      _ <- graph.loader
   116	        .load(s.graph, cfg.graph.windows, Some(objects.reader))
   117	        .left
   118	        .map(vs => s"the graph files failed validation: ${vs.take(3).mkString("; ")}")
   119	      kv <- ItemFileReader
   120	        .open(s.items, cfg.graph.windows)
   121	        .left
   122	        .map(vs => s"the item files failed validation: ${vs.take(3).mkString("; ")}")
   123	    yield
   124	      val (snapshot, graphRun) = graphSeg
   125	      val version = EmbeddingModels.version(model, Templates.Version)
   126	      val vectorFiles = Cli.files(s.vectors, ".jsonl")
   127	      val runId = Workflow.runIdFor(
   128	        cfg,
   129	        Seq(
   130	          s"graph=$graphRun",
   131	          s"items=${itemsSeg._2}",
   132	          s"vectors=${Cli.contentHash(s.vectors, vectorFiles)}",
   133	          s"embedding=$version"
   134	        ) ++ windowSummaryKey(cfg, snapshot).map(k => s"window-summary=$k"),
   135	        prompts,
   136	        RuntimeMode.FAKE
   137	      )
   138	      val runDir = s.out.resolve("dynamodb").resolve(s"snapshot=$snapshot").resolve(s"run=$runId")
   139	      val audit = FileObjectStore(s.out.resolve("s3"))
   140	      val records =
   141	        FileRuntimeRecords(runDir, cfg.graph.windows, () => Instant.now.getEpochSecond)
   142	      val state = LocalServiceStateTable(Some(runDir.resolve("ServiceState.jsonl")), Instant.now)
   143	      out(s"fake mode: runtime run $runId, snapshot $snapshot, outputs under ${s.out}")
   144	      Backend(
   145	        setup(cfg, prompts, RuntimeMode.FAKE, None, version, graphRun, snapshot, runId),
   146	        RuntimePorts(
   147	          graph.reader(GraphReadLimits.from(cfg)),
   148	          kv.reader,
   149	          objects.reader,
   150	          FileVectorStore(s.vectors).reader,
   151	          HashingEmbedder(),
   152	          audit.reader,
   153	          audit.loader,
   154	          records.writer,
   155	          summaries
   156	        ),
   157	        records.evidenceLookup,
   158	        IdempotencyStore(state, cfg.services.ttl.idempotencyHours, Instant.now),
   159	        JobStore(state, cfg.services.ttl.jobHours, Instant.now),
   160	        // shortcut: in memory, so only services in this JVM share it (LocalRun runs all three).
   161	        InMemoryJobQueue(cfg.services.queue.maxReceiveCount)
   162	      )
   163	
   164	  def live(
   165	      cfg: ScholarChainConfig,
   166	      run: LiveRun,
   167	      out: String => Unit,
   168	      answers: Boolean = true
   169	  ): Either[String, Backend] =
   170	    for
   171	      model <- EmbeddingModels.forId(cfg.models.embeddingModel)
   172	      prompts <- loadPrompts(cfg)
   173	      s3 = S3ObjectStore(
   174	        LiveAws.s3(run.target, LiveAws.overridesWithTimeout(cfg.services.timeouts.s3Ms.toLong)),
   175	        cfg.storage.bucket
   176	      )
   177	      summaries <- windowSummaries(cfg, run.snapshot, s3.reader, out, answers)
   178	    yield
   179	      val t = run.target
   180	      val tm = cfg.services.timeouts
   181	      def timeout(ms: Int) = LiveAws.overridesWithTimeout(ms.toLong)
   182	      val r = cfg.services.resilience
   183	      val policy = RetryPolicy(r.maxAttempts, r.baseDelayMs.toLong, r.maxDelayMs.toLong)
   184	      val dynamo = LiveAws.dynamo(t, timeout(tm.dynamoDbMs))
   185	      val prefix = cfg.dynamoDb.tablePrefix
   186	      val store = DynamoStore(dynamo, prefix, cfg.graph.windows, () => Instant.now.getEpochSecond)
   187	      val state = DynamoServiceStateTable(dynamo, prefix, Instant.now, policy)
   188	      val version = EmbeddingModels.version(model, Templates.Version)
   189	      val llmThrottle = Throttle(cfg.runtime.llm.requestsPerMinute, monotonic, Thread.sleep)
   190	      val embedThrottle =
   191	        Throttle(cfg.runtime.embedding.requestsPerMinute, monotonic, Thread.sleep)
   192	      val llm = Option.unless(cfg.services.stubLlm)(
   193	        BedrockHaikuClient(
   194	          LiveAws.bedrock(t, timeout(cfg.runtime.llm.timeoutMs)),
   195	          () => llmThrottle.acquire()
   196	        )
   197	      )
   198	      val runId = Workflow.runIdFor(
   199	        cfg,
   200	        Seq(
   201	          s"graph=${run.graphRun}",
   202	          s"items=${run.itemsRun}",
   203	          s"vectors=${run.vectorsRun}",
   204	          s"embedding=$version"
   205	        ) ++ windowSummaryKey(cfg, run.snapshot).map(k => s"window-summary=$k"),
   206	        prompts,
   207	        RuntimeMode.LIVE
   208	      )
   209	      out(
   210	        s"live mode: runtime run $runId, snapshot ${run.snapshot}, model calls ${
   211	            if llm.isEmpty then "stubbed" else "live"
   212	          }"
   213	      )
   214	      Backend(
   215	        setup(cfg, prompts, RuntimeMode.LIVE, llm, version, run.graphRun, run.snapshot, runId)
   216	          .copy(llmThrottle = llmThrottle, embedThrottle = Some(embedThrottle)),
   217	        RuntimePorts(
   218	          NeptuneGraphReader(
   219	            LiveAws.neptune(t, cfg.neptune.endpoint, cfg.neptune.port, timeout(tm.neptuneMs)),
   220	            GraphReadLimits.from(cfg),
   221	            policy
   222	          ),
   223	          store.reader,
   224	          s3.reader,
   225	          S3VectorStore(
   226	            LiveAws.vectors(t, timeout(tm.s3VectorsMs)),
   227	            cfg.s3Vectors.vectorBucket
   228	          ).reader,
   229	          BedrockTitanEmbedder(
   230	            LiveAws.bedrock(t, timeout(cfg.runtime.embedding.timeoutMs)),
   231	            model.modelId,
   232	            () => embedThrottle.acquire()
   233	          ),
   234	          s3.reader,
   235	          s3.loader, // audits are the runtime's only object writes
   236	          store.runtimeWriter,
   237	          summaries
   238	        ),
   239	        DynamoEvidenceLookup(dynamo, prefix, Instant.now, policy),
   240	        IdempotencyStore(state, cfg.services.ttl.idempotencyHours, Instant.now),
   241	        JobStore(state, cfg.services.ttl.jobHours, Instant.now),
   242	        SqsJobQueue(LiveAws.sqs(t), cfg.services.queue.url, tm.sqsMs.toLong)
   243	      )
   244	
   245	  /** The served window-summary key: a runtime run ID input whether or not this service opens it. */
   246	  def windowSummaryKey(cfg: ScholarChainConfig, snapshot: String): Option[String] =
   247	    val run = cfg.runtime.windowSummary.loadRun
   248	    Option.when(run.nonEmpty)(S3Layout.windowSummary(snapshot, run))
   249	
   250	  /** The corpus window summaries (work order 11): the object S3Layout.windowSummary(snapshot,
   251	    * runtime.window-summary.load-run), opened and validated once. An empty load run serves none
   252	    * (corpus questions abstain). Any violation stops startup; the message names the key and the
   253	    * violation codes only, never object contents. answers false (a service that answers no
   254	    * questions) never opens it.
   255	    */
   256	  def windowSummaries(
   257	      cfg: ScholarChainConfig,
   258	      snapshot: String,
   259	      objects: ObjectReadPort,
   260	      out: String => Unit,
   261	      answers: Boolean = true
   262	  ): Either[String, Option[WindowSummarySource]] =
   263	    windowSummaryKey(cfg, snapshot) match
   264	      case None =>
   265	        out("window summaries: none served (runtime.window-summary.load-run is empty)")
   266	        Right(None)
   267	      case Some(key) if !answers =>
   268	        out(s"window summaries: $key not opened (this service answers no questions)")
   269	        Right(None)
   270	      case Some(key) =>
   271	        WindowSummaries
   272	          .open(objects, key, cfg.graph.windows)
   273	          .left
   274	          .map(vs =>
   275	            val codes = vs.map(v => if v.line > 0 then s"${v.code} line ${v.line}" else v.code)
   276	            s"the window-summary object $key was refused: ${codes.distinct.take(5).mkString(", ")}"
   277	          )
   278	          .map { p =>
   279	            out(s"window summaries: $key")
   280	            Some(WindowSummarySource(key, p))
   281	          }
   282	
   283	  private def loadPrompts(cfg: ScholarChainConfig) =
   284	    PromptSet.load(
   285	      Paths.get(cfg.runtime.promptsDir),
   286	      cfg.runtime.planner.promptVersion,
   287	      cfg.runtime.synthesis.promptVersion
   288	    )
   289	
   290	  private def setup(
   291	      cfg: ScholarChainConfig,
   292	      prompts: PromptSet,
   293	      mode: RuntimeMode,
   294	      llm: Option[scholarchain.rag.runtime.LlmClient],
   295	      version: String,
   296	      graphRun: String,
   297	      snapshot: String,
   298	      runId: String
   299	  ) = RuntimeSetup(
   300	    cfg,
   301	    prompts,
   302	    mode,
   303	    llm,
   304	    Throttle(cfg.runtime.llm.requestsPerMinute, monotonic, Thread.sleep),
   305	    None,
   306	    version,
   307	    graphRun,
   308	    snapshot,
   309	    runId,
   310	    monotonic,
   311	    () => Instant.now
   312	  )
   313	
   314	/** Builds the HTTP services on a backend; the mains and the local runner use these. */
   315	object Apps:
   316	  /** Store calls and probes get `threads` threads (each HTTP thread holds at most one store call at
   317	    * a time); sync workflows get max(1, threads / 2) slots and no queue, so at least half the HTTP
   318	    * threads stay free for the fast routes (repair-01 F1; SERVICES.md section 2).
   319	    */
   320	  private def downstreams(cfg: ScholarChainConfig, threads: Int, metrics: Metrics) =
   321	    Downstreams(
   322	      cfg.services.resilience,
   323	      threads,
   324	      cfg.services.maxQueuedRequests,
   325	      metrics,
   326	      workflowThreads = math.max(1, threads / 2)
   327	    )
   328	
   329	  def queryApi(
   330	      cfg: ScholarChainConfig,
   331	      backend: Backend,
   332	      auth: ApiKeyAuth,
   333	      log: JsonLog,
   334	      metrics: Metrics = Metrics()
   335	  ): HttpService =
   336	    val c = cfg.services.queryApi
   337	    val ds = downstreams(cfg, c.threads, metrics)
   338	    val q = QueryService(cfg, backend, ds, log, metrics, RateLimiter(c.queryRatePerMinute))
   339	    HttpService(
   340	      "query-api",
   341	      q.routes ++ Ops.routes(q.readiness, ds, metrics, cfg.services.metricsEnabled),
   342	      HttpSettings(c.threads, cfg.services.maxQueuedRequests, cfg.services.maxBodyBytes),
   343	      auth,
   344	      log,
   345	      metrics,
   346	      () => ds.shutdown()
   347	    )
   348	
   349	  def graphEvidenceApi(
   350	      cfg: ScholarChainConfig,
   351	      backend: Backend,
   352	      auth: ApiKeyAuth,
   353	      log: JsonLog,
   354	      metrics: Metrics = Metrics()
   355	  ): HttpService =
   356	    val c = cfg.services.graphEvidenceApi
   357	    val ds = downstreams(cfg, c.threads, metrics)
   358	    val g = GraphEvidenceService(cfg, backend, ds)
   359	    HttpService(
   360	      "graph-evidence-api",
   361	      g.routes ++ Ops.routes(g.readiness, ds, metrics, cfg.services.metricsEnabled),
   362	      HttpSettings(c.threads, cfg.services.maxQueuedRequests, cfg.services.maxBodyBytes),
   363	      auth,
   364	      log,
   365	      metrics,
   366	      () => ds.shutdown()
   367	    )
   368	
   369	  /** The worker (ruling 7): the SQS poll loop, started with the HTTP service, plus health,
   370	    * readiness, and metrics on services.worker.port.
   371	    */
   372	  def worker(
   373	      cfg: ScholarChainConfig,
   374	      backend: Backend,
   375	      auth: ApiKeyAuth,
   376	      log: JsonLog,
   377	      metrics: Metrics = Metrics()
   378	  ): HttpService =
   379	    val c = cfg.services.worker
   380	    val wk = Worker(cfg, backend, log, metrics)
   381	    HttpService(
   382	      "worker",
   383	      Ops.routes(wk.readiness, wk.ds, metrics, cfg.services.metricsEnabled),
   384	      HttpSettings(c.threads, cfg.services.maxQueuedRequests, cfg.services.maxBodyBytes),
   385	      auth,
   386	      log,
   387	      metrics,
   388	      () => wk.stop(),
   389	      () => wk.start()
   390	    )
   391	
   392	/** Command line of the service mains. Fake mode binds 127.0.0.1; live mode (--live --profile, for
   393	  * Phase 12 and later; agents never start it) binds all interfaces.
   394	  */
   395	object Launcher:
   396	  val Usage: String =
   397	    "--graph <neptune CSV run dir> --items <dynamodb item run dir> --objects <object store root> " +
   398	      "--vectors <file vector store root> [--out <root, default scratch/services>] " +
   399	      "[--window-summary-root <object store root, default --objects>] | " +
   400	      "--live --profile <name> [--region <r>] --snapshot <date> --graph-run <runId> " +
   401	      "--items-run <runId> --vectors-run <runId>"
   402	  private val Opts = Set(
   403	    "graph",
   404	    "items",
   405	    "objects",
   406	    "vectors",
   407	    "out",
   408	    "profile",
   409	    "region",
   410	    "snapshot",
   411	    "graph-run",
   412	    "items-run",
   413	    "vectors-run",
   414	    "window-summary-root"
   415	  )
   416	
   417	  /** The backend the arguments name; Left is a usage or store problem. answers false: the service
   418	    * answers no questions, so the window-summary object is not opened (repair-01 m3).
   419	    */
   420	  def backend(
   421	      a: Args,
   422	      cfg: ScholarChainConfig,
   423	      out: String => Unit,
   424	      answers: Boolean = true
   425	  ): Either[String, Backend] =
   426	    if a.flag("live") then
   427	      for
   428	        profile <- a.req("profile")
   429	        snapshot <- a.req("snapshot")
   430	        graphRun <- a.req("graph-run")
   431	        itemsRun <- a.req("items-run")
   432	        vectorsRun <- a.req("vectors-run")
   433	        b <- Composition.live(
   434	          cfg,
   435	          LiveRun(
   436	            LiveTarget(profile, a.get("region").getOrElse(cfg.region)),
   437	            snapshot,
   438	            graphRun,
   439	            itemsRun,
   440	            vectorsRun
   441	          ),
   442	          out,
   443	          answers
   444	        )
   445	      yield b
   446	    else
   447	      for
   448	        graph <- a.req("graph")
   449	        items <- a.req("items")
   450	        objects <- a.req("objects")
   451	        vectors <- a.req("vectors")
   452	        root = Paths.get(a.get("out").getOrElse("scratch/services"))
   453	        _ <- Cli.freeDiskProblem(root).toLeft(())
   454	        b <- Composition.fake(
   455	          cfg,
   456	          FakeStores(
   457	            Paths.get(graph),
   458	            Paths.get(items),
   459	            Paths.get(objects),
   460	            Paths.get(vectors),
   461	            root,
   462	            a.get("window-summary-root").map(Paths.get(_))
   463	          ),
   464	          out,
   465	          answers
   466	        )
   467	      yield b
   468	
   469	  /** Arguments, configuration, key, and backend; or the exit code (2 usage or configuration, 1
   470	    * store).
   471	    */
   472	  def prepare(
   473	      args: Seq[String],
   474	      env: Map[String, String],
   475	      out: String => Unit,
   476	      answers: Boolean = true
   477	  ): Either[Int, (Args, ScholarChainConfig, ApiKeyAuth, Backend)] =
   478	    val parsed = for
   479	      a <- Cli.parse(args, Set("live"), Opts)
   480	      cfg <- ConfigLoader.load(env).left.map(_.mkString("; "))
   481	      // Ruling 5: the key is read once from the variable configuration names, never logged.
   482	      key <- env
   483	        .get(cfg.services.apiKeyEnv)
   484	        .filter(_.nonEmpty)
   485	        .toRight(s"set the environment variable ${cfg.services.apiKeyEnv} to the API key")
   486	    yield (a, cfg, ApiKeyAuth(key))
   487	    parsed match
   488	      case Left(e) =>
   489	        out(s"usage error: $e\nusage: $Usage")
   490	        Left(Cli.Usage)
   491	      case Right((a, cfg, auth)) =>
   492	        backend(a, cfg, out, answers) match
   493	          case Left(e) =>
   494	            out(s"failed: $e")
   495	            Left(1)
   496	          case Right(b) => Right((a, cfg, auth, b))
   497	
   498	  /** Fake mode binds 127.0.0.1, live mode all interfaces. */
   499	  def host(a: Args): String = if a.flag("live") then "0.0.0.0" else "127.0.0.1"
   500	
   501	  /** Starts one service; returns it, or the exit code (2 usage or configuration, 1 store). */
   502	  def start(
   503	      args: Seq[String],
   504	      build: (ScholarChainConfig, Backend, ApiKeyAuth, JsonLog) => HttpService,
   505	      port: ScholarChainConfig => Int,
   506	      env: Map[String, String] = sys.env,
   507	      out: String => Unit = println,
   508	      log: JsonLog = JsonLog.stdout,
   509	      answers: Boolean = true
   510	  ): Either[Int, HttpService] =
   511	    prepare(args, env, out, answers).map { (a, cfg, auth, b) =>
   512	      val svc = build(cfg, b, auth, log)
   513	      val bound = svc.start(host(a), port(cfg))
   514	      log.event(svc.name, "started", "port" -> bound)
   515	      svc
   516	    }
   517	
   518	  def main(
   519	      args: Array[String],
   520	      build: (ScholarChainConfig, Backend, ApiKeyAuth, JsonLog) => HttpService,
   521	      port: ScholarChainConfig => Int,
   522	      answers: Boolean = true
   523	  ): Unit =
   524	    start(args.toSeq, build, port, answers = answers) match
   525	      case Left(code) => sys.exit(code)
   526	      case Right(svc) => sys.addShutdownHook(svc.stop())
```

### modules/services/src/main/scala/scholarchain/services/Http.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import com.sun.net.httpserver.{HttpExchange, HttpServer}
     4	import scholarchain.core.ErrorCode
     5	
     6	import java.net.{InetSocketAddress, URLDecoder}
     7	import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
     8	import java.security.MessageDigest
     9	import java.util.UUID
    10	import java.util.concurrent.{
    11	  Executor,
    12	  LinkedBlockingQueue,
    13	  RejectedExecutionException,
    14	  ThreadFactory,
    15	  ThreadPoolExecutor,
    16	  TimeUnit
    17	}
    18	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger
    19	import scala.util.control.NonFatal
    20	
    21	/** A typed error that becomes the spec error body (ErrorCode carries status and retryable). The
    22	  * message is fixed service text: never a key, a request body, a question, or an exception message.
    23	  */
    24	final case class ApiError(code: ErrorCode, message: String)
    25	    extends RuntimeException(message, null, false, false)
    26	
    27	/** Request and trace IDs (law 3). A client value is echoed when it is 1 to 128 characters of
    28	  * [A-Za-z0-9._:-]; otherwise one is generated.
    29	  */
    30	object Ids:
    31	  private val Valid = "[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,128}".r
    32	  def fresh(prefix: String): String = prefix + UUID.randomUUID.toString.replace("-", "")
    33	  def orFresh(client: Option[String], prefix: String): String =
    34	    client.filter(Valid.matches).getOrElse(fresh(prefix))
    35	
    36	/** One API key compared in constant time (ruling 5): both sides are hashed to 32 bytes first, so
    37	  * the comparison time depends on neither the key's length nor its content.
    38	  */
    39	final class ApiKeyAuth(key: String):
    40	  require(key.nonEmpty, "the API key is empty")
    41	  private def digest(s: String) = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(s.getBytes(UTF_8))
    42	  private val expected = digest(key)
    43	  def accepts(header: Option[String]): Boolean =
    44	    MessageDigest.isEqual(digest(header.getOrElse("")), expected)
    45	  override def toString = "ApiKeyAuth(<redacted>)"
    46	
    47	/** One HTTP request as routes see it. Path parameters are percent-decoded path segments. */
    48	final class Request(
    49	    val method: String,
    50	    val params: Map[String, String],
    51	    val query: Map[String, Vector[String]],
    52	    headers: String => Option[String],
    53	    readBody: () => Array[Byte],
    54	    val requestId: String,
    55	    val traceId: String
    56	):
    57	  def header(name: String): Option[String] = headers(name)
    58	  lazy val body: Array[Byte] = readBody()
    59	
    60	  /** The body as a JSON value: Content-Type must be application/json, and the body must parse. */
    61	  def json(): ujson.Value =
    62	    if !header("Content-Type").exists(_.toLowerCase.startsWith("application/json")) then
    63	      throw ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_FIELD, "Content-Type must be application/json")
    64	    val bytes = body // REQUEST_TOO_LARGE surfaces here, not as malformed JSON
    65	    try ujson.read(bytes)
    66	    catch case NonFatal(_) => throw ApiError(ErrorCode.MALFORMED_JSON, "the body is not valid JSON")
    67	
    68	final case class Response(status: Int, body: ujson.Value, headers: Map[String, String] = Map.empty)
    69	
    70	object Response:
    71	  def ok[A: upickle.default.Writer](a: A, status: Int = 200): Response =
    72	    Response(status, upickle.default.writeJs(a))
    73	
    74	/** A route: method, a path template whose {name} segments bind parameters, and whether it needs the
    75	  * API key (only /healthz and /readyz are public).
    76	  */
    77	final case class Route(method: String, template: String, public: Boolean = false)(
    78	    val handle: Request => Response
    79	):
    80	  private val parts = template.split('/').toVector.drop(1)
    81	  def bind(segments: Vector[String]): Option[Map[String, String]] =
    82	    if segments.size != parts.size then None
    83	    else
    84	      parts
    85	        .zip(segments)
    86	        .foldLeft(Option(Map.empty[String, String])) {
    87	          case (Some(m), (p, s)) if p.startsWith("{") => Some(m + (p.drop(1).dropRight(1) -> s))
    88	          case (Some(m), (p, s)) if p == s            => Some(m)
    89	          case _                                      => None
    90	        }
    91	
    92	final case class HttpSettings(threads: Int, maxQueuedRequests: Int, maxBodyBytes: Int)
    93	
    94	/** The JDK HTTP server (ruling 4) with a bounded pool: `threads` workers and at most
    95	  * `maxQueuedRequests` waiting. A request beyond that is answered 503 OVERLOADED by a one-thread
    96	  * shedding pool.
    97	  */
    98	final class HttpService(
    99	    val name: String,
   100	    routes: Seq[Route],
   101	    settings: HttpSettings,
   102	    auth: ApiKeyAuth,
   103	    log: JsonLog,
   104	    metrics: Metrics,
   105	    onStop: () => Unit = () => (),
   106	    onStart: () => Unit = () => ()
   107	):
   108	  private val shedding = ThreadLocal.withInitial[Boolean](() => false)
   109	  private def pool(n: Int, queue: Int, tag: String) =
   110	    val count = AtomicInteger()
   111	    val factory: ThreadFactory = r =>
   112	      val t = Thread(r, s"$name-$tag-${count.incrementAndGet()}")
   113	      t.setDaemon(true)
   114	      t
   115	    ThreadPoolExecutor(n, n, 60, TimeUnit.SECONDS, LinkedBlockingQueue[Runnable](queue), factory)
   116	  private val workers = pool(settings.threads, settings.maxQueuedRequests, "http")
   117	  private val shed = pool(1, settings.maxQueuedRequests, "shed")
   118	  private val executor: Executor = r =>
   119	    try workers.execute(r)
   120	    catch
   121	      case _: RejectedExecutionException =>
   122	        val s: Runnable = () =>
   123	          shedding.set(true)
   124	          try r.run()
   125	          finally shedding.set(false)
   126	        // shortcut: when the shedding queue is full too, the 503 is written on the dispatcher.
   127	        try shed.execute(s)
   128	        catch case _: RejectedExecutionException => s.run()
   129	
   130	  @volatile private var server: Option[HttpServer] = None
   131	
   132	  /** (method, path template) of every route, for the OpenAPI contract test. */
   133	  def routeTable: Seq[(String, String)] = routes.map(r => r.method -> r.template)
   134	
   135	  /** Binds host:port (0 picks a free port) and returns the bound port. */
   136	  def start(host: String, port: Int): Int =
   137	    val s = HttpServer.create(InetSocketAddress(host, port), 0)
   138	    s.createContext("/", ex => handle(ex))
   139	    s.setExecutor(executor)
   140	    s.start()
   141	    server = Some(s)
   142	    onStart()
   143	    s.getAddress.getPort
   144	
   145	  def stop(): Unit =
   146	    server.foreach(_.stop(0))
   147	    server = None
   148	    workers.shutdownNow()
   149	    shed.shutdownNow()
   150	    onStop()
   151	
   152	  private def segments(rawPath: String): Vector[String] =
   153	    rawPath.split('/').toVector.drop(1).map(s => URLDecoder.decode(s.replace("+", "%2B"), UTF_8))
   154	
   155	  private def queryParams(raw: Option[String]): Map[String, Vector[String]] =
   156	    raw.toVector
   157	      .flatMap(_.split('&'))
   158	      .filter(_.nonEmpty)
   159	      .map { kv =>
   160	        val (k, v) = kv.span(_ != '=')
   161	        URLDecoder.decode(k, UTF_8) -> URLDecoder.decode(v.drop(1), UTF_8)
   162	      }
   163	      .groupMap(_._1)(_._2)
   164	
   165	  private def readBody(ex: HttpExchange): Array[Byte] =
   166	    val max = settings.maxBodyBytes
   167	    val tooLarge =
   168	      ApiError(ErrorCode.REQUEST_TOO_LARGE, s"the body is larger than $max bytes")
   169	    if Option(ex.getRequestHeaders.getFirst("Content-Length"))
   170	        .flatMap(_.toLongOption)
   171	        .exists(_ > max)
   172	    then throw tooLarge
   173	    val bytes = ex.getRequestBody.readNBytes(max + 1)
   174	    if bytes.length > max then throw tooLarge
   175	    bytes
   176	
   177	  private def handle(ex: HttpExchange): Unit =
   178	    val t0 = System.nanoTime()
   179	    val h = ex.getRequestHeaders
   180	    def header(n: String) = Option(h.getFirst(n))
   181	    val requestId = Ids.orFresh(header("X-Request-Id"), "req-")
   182	    val traceId = Ids.orFresh(header("X-Trace-Id"), "trace-")
   183	    var template = "unmatched"
   184	    val response =
   185	      try
   186	        if shedding.get then
   187	          throw ApiError(ErrorCode.OVERLOADED, "the service is at capacity; retry later")
   188	        val path =
   189	          try segments(ex.getRequestURI.getRawPath)
   190	          catch case NonFatal(_) => throw ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_FIELD, "malformed path")
   191	        val method = ex.getRequestMethod
   192	        val found = routes.view
   193	          .filter(_.method == method)
   194	          .flatMap(r => r.bind(path).map(r -> _))
   195	          .headOption
   196	        if !found.exists(_._1.public) && !auth.accepts(header("X-Api-Key")) then
   197	          throw ApiError(ErrorCode.UNAUTHORIZED, "missing or invalid API key")
   198	        found match
   199	          case None =>
   200	            throw ApiError(ErrorCode.ROUTE_NOT_FOUND, "no route for this method and path")
   201	          case Some((route, params)) =>
   202	            template = route.template
   203	            val query =
   204	              try queryParams(Option(ex.getRequestURI.getRawQuery))
   205	              catch
   206	                case NonFatal(_) =>
   207	                  throw ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_FIELD, "malformed query string")
   208	            route.handle(
   209	              Request(method, params, query, header, () => readBody(ex), requestId, traceId)
   210	            )
   211	      catch
   212	        case e: ApiError => errorResponse(e, requestId, traceId)
   213	        case NonFatal(_) =>
   214	          errorResponse(
   215	            ApiError(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR, "unexpected internal failure"),
   216	            requestId,
   217	            traceId
   218	          )
   219	    try
   220	      val bytes = response.body.render().getBytes(UTF_8)
   221	      val out = ex.getResponseHeaders
   222	      out.set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
   223	      out.set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
   224	      out.set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
   225	      out.set("X-Request-Id", requestId)
   226	      out.set("X-Trace-Id", traceId)
   227	      response.headers.foreach((k, v) => out.set(k, v))
   228	      ex.sendResponseHeaders(response.status, bytes.length.toLong)
   229	      ex.getResponseBody.write(bytes)
   230	    catch case NonFatal(_) => () // the client went away; the request is still logged
   231	    finally ex.close()
   232	    val ms = (System.nanoTime() - t0) / 1000000
   233	    val code = response.body.objOpt.flatMap(_.get("error")).flatMap(_.strOpt)
   234	    metrics.request(template, response.status, ms)
   235	    log.request(name, traceId, requestId, ex.getRequestMethod, template, response.status, ms, code)
   236	
   237	  private def errorResponse(e: ApiError, requestId: String, traceId: String): Response =
   238	    val retryAfter = Option.when(e.code == ErrorCode.RATE_LIMITED)("Retry-After" -> "60")
   239	    Response(
   240	      e.code.httpStatus,
   241	      upickle.default.writeJs(ErrorBody(e.code, e.message, traceId, requestId, e.code.retryable)),
   242	      retryAfter.toMap
   243	    )
```

   314	object EvidenceResponse:
   315	  /** The item with its text cut to maxChars (never inside a surrogate pair); textHash is still the
   316	    * hash of the full text.
   317	    */
   318	  def of(e: EvidenceItem, maxChars: Int): EvidenceResponse =
   319	    val cut = e.text.filter(_.length > maxChars).map { t =>
   320	      val n = if Character.isHighSurrogate(t.charAt(maxChars - 1)) then maxChars - 1 else maxChars
   321	      t.take(n)
   322	    }
   323	    EvidenceResponse(
   324	      e.evidenceId,
   325	      e.evidenceType,
   326	      e.subjectIds,
   327	      e.workId,
   328	      e.sourceUri,
   329	      e.chunkId,
   330	      e.graphPath,
   331	      e.score,
   332	      cut.orElse(e.text),
   333	      cut.isDefined,
   334	      e.textHash,
   335	      e.facts,
   336	      e.provenance
   337	    )
