   363	object GraphViews:
   364	  private def props(m: Map[String, Prop]): Map[String, ujson.Value] = m.map { (k, v) =>
   365	    k -> (v match
   366	      case s: String  => ujson.Str(s)
   367	      case b: Boolean => ujson.Bool(b)
   368	      case i: Int     => ujson.Num(i.toDouble)
   369	      case l: Long    => ujson.Num(l.toDouble)
   370	      case d: Double  => ujson.Num(d))
   371	  }
   372	  def node(e: GraphElement): NodeView = NodeView(e.id, e.label, props(e.props))
   373	  def edge(e: GraphElement): EdgeView = EdgeView(e.id, e.label, e.from, e.to, props(e.props))
   374	
   375	// ---- Health ----
   376	
   377	final case class Status(status: String) derives ReadWriter
   378	
   379	/** Validation and translation of the POST /v1/query body (ruling 6): 400 for malformed or invalid
   380	  * fields, 422 for valid JSON asking for something this deployment does not support.
   381	  */
   382	object QueryValidation:
   383	  // Field -> JSON type ("string", "int", "bool", "strings"). uPickle is lenient (it reads a number
   384	  // into a String, and null into a String), so every type is checked on the JSON value first.
   385	  private val Top = Map("question" -> "string", "execution" -> "string")
   386	  private val Nested: Map[String, Map[String, String]] = Map(
   387	    "constraints" -> Map(
   388	      "yearFrom" -> "int",
   389	      "yearTo" -> "int",
   390	      "topics" -> "strings",
   391	      "institutions" -> "strings",
   392	      "venues" -> "strings"
   393	    ),
   394	    "analysis" -> (Map("rankingMetric" -> "string") ++ Seq(
   395	      "includeTeamSize",
   396	      "includeBridgeMetrics",
   397	      "includeCommunities",
   398	      "includePaths"
   399	    ).map(_ -> "bool")),
   400	    "retrieval" -> Seq("graphLimit", "vectorLimit", "maxPathDepth").map(_ -> "int").toMap,
   401	    "output" -> Seq("includeEvidence", "includeScores", "includeUncertainty").map(_ -> "bool").toMap
   402	  )
   403	  // Unknown fields: a constraint or analysis field is an unsupported constraint, a retrieval field
   404	  // an unsupported retrieval mode (422); anywhere else an invalid field (400).
   405	  private val UnknownField: Map[String, String => ApiError] = Map(
   406	    "constraints" -> unsupported,
   407	    "analysis" -> unsupported,
   408	    "retrieval" -> (m => ApiError(ErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED_RETRIEVAL_MODE, m)),
   409	    "output" -> invalid
   410	  )
   411	  // Only the optional structured fields may be null; question, execution, and output flags not.
   412	  private val Nullable = Set("constraints", "analysis", "retrieval")
   413	  val Executions = Set("sync", "async")
   414	
   415	  private def invalid(m: String) = ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_FIELD, m)
   416	  private def unsupported(m: String) = ApiError(ErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED_CONSTRAINT, m)
   417	
   418	  private def typed(v: ujson.Value, t: String): Boolean = (v, t) match
   419	    case (ujson.Str(_), "string") | (ujson.Bool(_), "bool") => true
   420	    case (ujson.Num(n), "int")                              => n.isWhole && n.abs <= Int.MaxValue
   421	    case (ujson.Arr(xs), "strings")                         => xs.forall(_.strOpt.isDefined)
   422	    case _                                                  => false
   423	
   424	  private def fields(o: ujson.Obj, where: String, types: Map[String, String], nullable: Boolean) =
   425	    o.value.foreach { (k, v) =>
   426	      if !(v.isNull && nullable) && !typed(v, types(k)) then
   427	        throw invalid(s"$where$k must be ${types(k).replace("strings", "an array of strings")}")
   428	    }
   429	
   430	  /** The validated body and its normalized question. Throws ApiError. */
   431	  def parse(json: ujson.Value, cfg: ScholarChainConfig): (QueryBody, String) =
   432	    val o = json.objOpt.getOrElse(throw invalid("the body must be a JSON object"))
   433	    val allowed = Top.keySet ++ Nested.keySet
   434	    if !o.value.keySet.subsetOf(allowed) then
   435	      throw invalid(s"unsupported field; allowed: ${allowed.toVector.sorted.mkString(", ")}")
   436	    if !o.value.contains("question") then throw invalid("question is required")
   437	    fields(ujson.Obj.from(o.value.filter((k, _) => Top.contains(k))), "", Top, false)
   438	    Nested.foreach { (name, types) =>
   439	      o.value.get(name).foreach {
   440	        case ujson.Null if Nullable(name) => ()
   441	        case n: ujson.Obj                 =>
   442	          if !n.value.keySet.subsetOf(types.keySet) then
   443	            throw UnknownField(name)(
   444	              s"$name holds an unsupported field; allowed: ${types.keys.toVector.sorted.mkString(", ")}"
   445	            )
   446	          fields(n, s"$name.", types, Nullable(name))
   447	        case _ => throw invalid(s"$name must be an object")
   448	      }
   449	    }
   450	    val body =
   451	      try read[QueryBody](json)
   452	      catch
   453	        case NonFatal(_) =>
   454	          throw invalid("a field has the wrong type, or the required field question is missing")
   455	    val question = Question
   456	      .normalize(body.question, cfg.runtime.question.maxChars)
   457	      .fold(m => throw invalid(m), identity)
   458	    if !Executions(body.execution) then throw invalid("execution must be sync or async")
   459	    body.constraints.foreach { c =>
   460	      if c.yearFrom.zip(c.yearTo).exists(_ > _) then throw invalid("yearFrom is after yearTo")
   461	      val p = cfg.runtime.planner
   462	      Seq("topics" -> c.topics, "institutions" -> c.institutions, "venues" -> c.venues).foreach {
   463	        (name, terms) =>
   464	          terms.foreach { ts =>
   465	            if ts.exists(_.trim.isEmpty) then throw invalid(s"$name holds a blank term")
   466	            if ts.size > p.maxFilterTerms || ts.exists(_.length > p.maxTermChars) then
   467	              throw unsupported(
   468	                s"$name supports at most ${p.maxFilterTerms} terms of at most ${p.maxTermChars} characters"
   469	              )
   470	          }
   471	      }
   472	    }
   473	    body.analysis.flatMap(_.rankingMetric).foreach { m =>
   474	      val ranked = MetricAttributes.ranked.map(_.toString)
   475	      if !ranked.contains(m) then
   476	        throw unsupported(
   477	          s"rankingMetric must be one of the stored rankings: ${ranked.mkString(", ")}"
   478	        )
   479	    }
   480	    body.retrieval.foreach { r =>
   481	      val l = cfg.retrieval
   482	      Seq(
   483	        ("graphLimit", r.graphLimit, l.graphLimit),
   484	        ("vectorLimit", r.vectorLimit, l.vectorLimit),
   485	        ("maxPathDepth", r.maxPathDepth, l.maxPathDepth)
   486	      ).foreach { (name, v, max) =>
   487	        if v.exists(_ < 1) then throw invalid(s"retrieval.$name must be at least 1")
   488	        if v.exists(_ > max) then
   489	          throw ApiError(
   490	            ErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED_RETRIEVAL_MODE,
   491	            s"retrieval.$name above $max is not supported in this deployment"
   492	          )
   493	      }
   494	    }
   495	    (body, question)
   496	
   497	  /** sha256:<hex> of the canonical body: the model written back, so field order, whitespace, and
   498	    * explicit nulls versus omitted fields do not matter.
   499	    */
   500	  def requestHash(body: QueryBody): String = StableIds.textHash(write(body))
   501	
   502	  /** The runtime request (structured fields keep overriding the model, Phase 9 ruling 4). */
   503	  def toRuntime(body: QueryBody, queryId: String): QueryRequest =
   504	    QueryRequest(
   505	      RuntimeSchemas.QueryRequest,
   506	      queryId,
   507	      None,
   508	      body.question,
   509	      body.constraints.map(c =>
   510	        RequestConstraints(c.yearFrom, c.yearTo, c.topics, c.institutions, c.venues)
   511	      ),
   512	      body.analysis.map(a =>
   513	        RequestAnalysis(
   514	          a.rankingMetric.map(CollaborationMetric.valueOf),
   515	          a.includeTeamSize,
   516	          a.includeBridgeMetrics,
   517	          a.includeCommunities,
   518	          a.includePaths
   519	        )
   520	      ),
   521	      body.retrieval.map(r => RequestRetrieval(r.graphLimit, r.vectorLimit, r.maxPathDepth)),
   522	      body.output.map(o => RequestOutput(o.includeEvidence, o.includeScores, o.includeUncertainty))
   523	    )
```

### modules/services/src/main/scala/scholarchain/services/Observability.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.rag.runtime.{AuditRecord, StageTiming}
     4	
     5	import java.time.Instant
     6	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.LongAdder
     7	import scala.collection.concurrent.TrieMap
     8	import scala.collection.mutable
     9	
    10	/** Structured logs (ruling 12): one JSON line per request and per workflow stage. Only the fields
    11	  * named here are ever written: never a body, a question, a key, or retrieved or model text.
    12	  */
    13	final class JsonLog(sink: String => Unit):
    14	  private def emit(kind: String, fields: (String, ujson.Value)*): Unit =
    15	    val line = ujson.Obj.from(
    16	      Seq("ts" -> ujson.Str(Instant.now.toString), "type" -> ujson.Str(kind)) ++ fields
    17	    )
    18	    sink(line.render())
    19	
    20	  private def opt(s: Option[String]): ujson.Value = s.fold(ujson.Null)(ujson.Str(_))
    21	
    22	  def request(
    23	      service: String,
    24	      traceId: String,
    25	      requestId: String,
    26	      method: String,
    27	      route: String,
    28	      status: Int,
    29	      latencyMs: Long,
    30	      error: Option[String]
    31	  ): Unit =
    32	    emit(
    33	      "request",
    34	      "service" -> service,
    35	      "traceId" -> traceId,
    36	      "requestId" -> requestId,
    37	      "method" -> method,
    38	      "route" -> route,
    39	      "status" -> status,
    40	      "latencyMs" -> latencyMs.toDouble,
    41	      "error" -> opt(error)
    42	    )
    43	
    44	  def stage(r: Request, queryId: String, s: StageTiming): Unit =
    45	    emit(
    46	      "stage",
    47	      "traceId" -> r.traceId,
    48	      "requestId" -> r.requestId,
    49	      "queryId" -> queryId,
    50	      "stage" -> s.stage.toString,
    51	      "outcome" -> s.outcome.toString,
    52	      "latencyMs" -> s.latencyMs.toDouble
    53	    )
    54	
    55	  /** A workflow stage of an async job (worker): correlated by job and query ID, which query-api's
    56	    * job-queued event links to the trace and request IDs of the POST.
    57	    */
    58	  def jobStage(jobId: String, queryId: String, s: StageTiming): Unit =
    59	    emit(
    60	      "stage",
    61	      "jobId" -> jobId,
    62	      "queryId" -> queryId,
    63	      "stage" -> s.stage.toString,
    64	      "outcome" -> s.outcome.toString,
    65	      "latencyMs" -> s.latencyMs.toDouble
    66	    )
    67	
    68	  def event(service: String, what: String, fields: (String, ujson.Value)*): Unit =
    69	    emit("event", (Seq[(String, ujson.Value)]("service" -> service, "event" -> what) ++ fields)*)
    70	
    71	object JsonLog:
    72	  val stdout: JsonLog = JsonLog(line => System.out.synchronized(System.out.println(line)))
    73	
    74	/** In-process counters for GET /metrics (ruling 12). Latency percentiles are over the most recent
    75	  * Window requests of each route.
    76	  */
    77	final class Metrics:
    78	  // shortcut: percentiles over a per-route sample of the last 1024 requests; use a histogram if
    79	  // the dashboard needs whole-run percentiles (CloudWatch computes them from the request logs).
    80	  private val Window = 1024
    81	  private val requests = TrieMap.empty[(String, Int), LongAdder]
    82	  private val latencies = TrieMap.empty[String, mutable.ArrayDeque[Long]]
    83	  private val counters = TrieMap.empty[String, LongAdder]
    84	
    85	  def add(name: String, n: Long = 1): Unit = counters.getOrElseUpdate(name, LongAdder()).add(n)
    86	
    87	  def request(route: String, status: Int, ms: Long): Unit =
    88	    requests.getOrElseUpdate((route, status), LongAdder()).increment()
    89	    val q = latencies.getOrElseUpdate(route, mutable.ArrayDeque.empty[Long])
    90	    q.synchronized {
    91	      q.append(ms)
    92	      if q.size > Window then q.removeHead()
    93	    }
    94	    if status == 504 then add("timeouts")
    95	
    96	  /** Model, verification, and abstention counters of one answered query. */
    97	  def answer(a: AuditRecord): Unit =
    98	    add("answers")
    99	    add("llmRequests", a.llmCalls.map(_.requests.toLong).sum)
   100	    add("llmInputTokens", a.llmCalls.flatMap(_.inputTokens).map(_.toLong).sum)
   101	    add("llmOutputTokens", a.llmCalls.flatMap(_.outputTokens).map(_.toLong).sum)
   102	    add("verifiedClaims", a.verification.verified.toLong)
   103	    add("partiallySupportedClaims", a.verification.partiallySupported.toLong)
   104	    add("unsupportedClaims", a.verification.unsupported.toLong)
   105	    add("conflictingClaims", a.verification.conflicting.toLong)
   106	    if a.abstained then add("abstentions")
   107	
   108	  private def pct(sorted: Vector[Long], p: Double): Long =
   109	    if sorted.isEmpty then 0L
   110	    else sorted(math.min(sorted.size - 1, math.ceil(p * sorted.size).toInt - 1).max(0))
   111	
   112	  def snapshot: ujson.Obj =
   113	    val byRoute = requests.toVector
   114	      .groupMap(_._1._1)((k, v) => k._2.toString -> v.sum())
   115	      .map((route, xs) =>
   116	        route -> ujson.Obj.from(xs.sortBy(_._1).map((s, n) => s -> ujson.Num(n.toDouble)))
   117	      )
   118	    val lat = latencies.toVector.map { (route, q) =>
   119	      val xs = q.synchronized(q.toVector).sorted
   120	      route -> ujson.Obj(
   121	        "count" -> xs.size,
   122	        "p50" -> pct(xs, 0.50).toDouble,
   123	        "p95" -> pct(xs, 0.95).toDouble,
   124	        "p99" -> pct(xs, 0.99).toDouble
   125	      )
   126	    }
   127	    val names = Vector(
   128	      "timeouts",
   129	      "answers",
   130	      "abstentions",
   131	      "llmRequests",
   132	      "llmInputTokens",
   133	      "llmOutputTokens",
   134	      "verifiedClaims",
   135	      "partiallySupportedClaims",
   136	      "unsupportedClaims",
   137	      "conflictingClaims",
   138	      "queueReceives",
   139	      "jobsQueued",
   140	      "jobsSucceeded",
   141	      "jobsFailed",
   142	      "duplicateDeliveries",
   143	      "poisonMessages",
   144	      "stuckJobsFailed"
   145	    )
   146	    ujson.Obj(
   147	      "requests" -> ujson.Obj.from(byRoute.toVector.sortBy(_._1)),
   148	      "latencyMs" -> ujson.Obj.from(lat.sortBy(_._1)),
   149	      "counters" -> ujson.Obj.from(
   150	        (names ++ counters.keys.toVector.filterNot(names.contains).sorted)
   151	          .map(n => n -> ujson.Num(counters.get(n).fold(0L)(_.sum()).toDouble))
   152	      )
   153	    )
```

### modules/services/src/main/scala/scholarchain/services/Resilience.scala [CHANGED BY repair-01 Part A]
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.core.ErrorCode
     4	import scholarchain.core.config.ResilienceConfig
     5	import scholarchain.storage.{GraphReadTimeoutException, RetryPolicy, TransientStoreException}
     6	import scholarchain.storage.live.LiveAws
     7	
     8	import java.util.concurrent.{
     9	  BlockingQueue,
    10	  Callable,
    11	  ExecutionException,
    12	  Future,
    13	  LinkedBlockingQueue,
    14	  RejectedExecutionException,
    15	  SynchronousQueue,
    16	  ThreadPoolExecutor,
    17	  TimeUnit,
    18	  TimeoutException
    19	}
    20	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger
    21	import scala.collection.mutable
    22	
    23	/** A consecutive-failure circuit breaker (ruling 9). It opens after `threshold` consecutive
    24	  * failures and fails fast for `openMs`; after that, calls pass again, and the first failure
    25	  * reopens it at once (half-open), while a success closes it.
    26	  */
    27	final class Breaker(threshold: Int, openMs: Long, nowMs: () => Long):
    28	  private var failures = 0
    29	  private var openedAt = Long.MinValue
    30	  def isOpen: Boolean = synchronized(failures >= threshold && nowMs() - openedAt < openMs)
    31	  def success(): Unit = synchronized { failures = 0 }
    32	
    33	  /** Records a failure; true when it opened the breaker. */
    34	  def failure(): Boolean = synchronized {
    35	    failures += 1
    36	    if failures >= threshold then
    37	      openedAt = nowMs()
    38	      true
    39	    else false
    40	  }
    41	
    42	/** A failure of a downstream call that a route turns into no other status (502). */
    43	final class DownstreamFailed(message: String) extends RuntimeException(message)
    44	
    45	/** How one downstream call is bounded (ruling 9): a client-side timeout, retries for reads whose
    46	  * adapter does not retry itself (so there is one retry layer), and which code a timeout maps to.
    47	  * interruptOnTimeout false lets a timed-out call run to its end on the pool (the workflow, so its
    48	  * audit is still written completely). workflow true runs the call on the workflow pool, apart from
    49	  * store calls and readiness probes (repair-01 F1).
    50	  */
    51	final case class CallPolicy(
    52	    downstream: String,
    53	    timeoutMs: Long,
    54	    retryReads: Boolean = false,
    55	    timeoutCode: ErrorCode = ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT,
    56	    timeoutIsFailure: Boolean = true,
    57	    interruptOnTimeout: Boolean = true,
    58	    workflow: Boolean = false
    59	)
    60	
    61	/** Runs downstream calls with a timeout and one breaker per downstream name, on two bounded pools
    62	  * so a timed-out call frees the request thread (repair-01 F1):
    63	  *   - store calls and readiness probes: `threads` threads and a queue of `queue`;
    64	  *   - workflows (CallPolicy.workflow): `workflowThreads` threads and no queue, so long workflows
    65	  *     never hold the threads store calls and probes need.
    66	  *
    67	  * A call's timeout counts from its start. A call that cannot start (a full pool, or still waiting
    68	  * when its timeout passes) answers 503 OVERLOADED and touches no breaker: waiting for a thread
    69	  * says nothing about the downstream.
    70	  */
    71	final class Downstreams(
    72	    cfg: ResilienceConfig,
    73	    threads: Int,
    74	    queue: Int,
    75	    metrics: Metrics,
    76	    workflowThreads: Int = 1,
    77	    nowMs: () => Long = () => System.nanoTime() / 1000000,
    78	    sleep: Long => Unit = Thread.sleep
    79	):
    80	  private val breakers = mutable.HashMap.empty[String, Breaker]
    81	  private def breaker(name: String) = synchronized(
    82	    breakers.getOrElseUpdate(
    83	      name,
    84	      Breaker(cfg.breakerFailureThreshold, cfg.breakerOpenMs.toLong, nowMs)
    85	    )
    86	  )
    87	  def openBreakers: Vector[String] =
    88	    synchronized(breakers.toVector).filter(_._2.isOpen).map(_._1).sorted
    89	
    90	  private val retry = RetryPolicy(cfg.maxAttempts, cfg.baseDelayMs.toLong, cfg.maxDelayMs.toLong)
    91	  private val count = AtomicInteger()
    92	  private def executor(n: Int, q: BlockingQueue[Runnable], tag: String) = ThreadPoolExecutor(
    93	    n,
    94	    n,
    95	    60,
    96	    TimeUnit.SECONDS,
    97	    q,
    98	    r =>
    99	      val t = Thread(r, s"$tag-${count.incrementAndGet()}")
   100	      t.setDaemon(true)
   101	      t
   102	  )
   103	  private val pool = executor(threads, LinkedBlockingQueue[Runnable](queue), "downstream")
   104	  // Threads are created on first use, so a service without workflows starts none.
   105	  private val workflows = executor(workflowThreads, SynchronousQueue[Runnable](), "workflow")
   106	  def shutdown(): Unit =
   107	    pool.shutdownNow()
   108	    workflows.shutdownNow()
   109	
   110	  /** Whether a workflow could start now (the worker receives a job only then). */
   111	  def workflowSlotFree: Boolean = workflows.getActiveCount < workflowThreads
   112	
   113	  private val overloaded = ApiError(ErrorCode.OVERLOADED, "the service is at capacity; retry later")
   114	
   115	  /** Whether a call has started; the caller and the pool thread race once to decide. */
   116	  private final class Start:
   117	    private val state = AtomicInteger(0) // 0 waiting, 1 started, 2 given up by the caller
   118	    @volatile var atNs = 0L
   119	    def begin(): Boolean =
   120	      atNs = System.nanoTime()
   121	      state.compareAndSet(0, 1)
   122	    def giveUp(): Boolean = state.compareAndSet(0, 2)
   123	
   124	  /** The result within timeoutMs of the call's start. A call still waiting to start when timeoutMs
   125	    * has passed is given up: 503 OVERLOADED, never a downstream failure.
   126	    */
   127	  private def await[A](f: Future[A], start: Start, timeoutMs: Long): A =
   128	    try f.get(timeoutMs, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
   129	    catch
   130	      case e: TimeoutException =>
   131	        if start.giveUp() then
   132	          f.cancel(false)
   133	          throw overloaded
   134	        val left = timeoutMs - (System.nanoTime() - start.atNs) / 1000000
   135	        if left <= 0 then throw e else f.get(left, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
   136	
   137	  private def retryable(e: Throwable) =
   138	    e.isInstanceOf[TransientStoreException] || LiveAws.isTransient(e)
   139	
   140	  private def attempts[A](retryReads: Boolean)(body: => A): A =
   141	    var n = 1
   142	    var result: Option[A] = None
   143	    while result.isEmpty do
   144	      try result = Some(body)
   145	      catch
   146	        case scala.util.control.NonFatal(e)
   147	            if retryReads && n < retry.maxAttempts && retryable(e) =>
   148	          sleep(retry.delayBeforeRetry(n))
   149	          n += 1
   150	    result.get
   151	
   152	  /** Runs body under p. An ApiError from body passes through and is no downstream failure. */
   153	  def call[A](p: CallPolicy)(body: => A): A =
   154	    val b = breaker(p.downstream)
   155	    if b.isOpen then
   156	      throw ApiError(ErrorCode.CIRCUIT_OPEN, s"${p.downstream} is unavailable; retry later")
   157	    val start = Start()
   158	    val task: Callable[A] = () =>
   159	      if start.begin() then attempts(p.retryReads)(body) else null.asInstanceOf[A]
   160	    val f =
   161	      try (if p.workflow then workflows else pool).submit(task)
   162	      catch case _: RejectedExecutionException => throw overloaded
   163	    def failed(code: ErrorCode, message: String): Nothing =
   164	      if b.failure() then metrics.add(s"circuitOpened.${p.downstream}")
   165	      throw ApiError(code, message)
   166	    try
   167	      val a = await(f, start, p.timeoutMs)
   168	      b.success()
   169	      a
   170	    catch
   171	      case _: TimeoutException =>
   172	        f.cancel(p.interruptOnTimeout)
   173	        val message = s"${p.downstream} did not answer within ${p.timeoutMs} ms"
   174	        if p.timeoutIsFailure then failed(p.timeoutCode, message)
   175	        else throw ApiError(p.timeoutCode, message)
   176	      case e: ExecutionException =>
   177	        e.getCause match
   178	          case a: ApiError                  => throw a
   179	          case _: GraphReadTimeoutException =>
   180	            failed(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT, s"${p.downstream} query timed out")
   181	          case _: IllegalArgumentException =>
   182	            // Routes validate first, so a failed check inside a downstream is a server defect
   183	            // (repair-01 m5): 500 with no detail, and no failure of the downstream itself.
   184	            throw ApiError(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR, "unexpected internal failure")
   185	          case _ => failed(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE, s"${p.downstream} failed")
   186	      case _: InterruptedException =>
   187	        f.cancel(true)
   188	        throw ApiError(ErrorCode.OVERLOADED, "the request was interrupted")
   189	
   190	/** Accepted requests per minute per instance (ruling 6, 429), over a sliding 60 s window. */
   191	final class RateLimiter(perMinute: Int, nowMs: () => Long = () => System.nanoTime() / 1000000):
   192	  private val times = mutable.ArrayDeque.empty[Long]
   193	  def tryAcquire(): Boolean = synchronized {
   194	    val now = nowMs()
   195	    while times.headOption.exists(_ <= now - 60000) do times.removeHead()
   196	    if times.size < perMinute then
   197	      times.append(now)
   198	      true
   199	    else false
   200	  }
```

### modules/services/src/main/scala/scholarchain/services/Services.scala [CHANGED BY repair-01 Part A]
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.core.{ErrorCode, IdFormat, JobState, StableIds}
     4	import scholarchain.core.config.ScholarChainConfig
     5	import scholarchain.rag.runtime.{Audit, RuntimeApi, RuntimeSetup}
     6	import scholarchain.storage.{
     7	  Direction,
     8	  JobError,
     9	  JobMessage,
    10	  JobRecord,
    11	  LoadOutcome,
    12	  NeptuneContract,
    13	  Stored
    14	}
    15	
    16	import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
    17	import java.util.Base64
    18	import scala.util.{Failure, Success, Try}
    19	import upickle.default.write
    20	
    21	// Routes are thin (ruling 3): they validate, translate, call one RuntimeApi function or one port
    22	// under a CallPolicy, and map the outcome to a status. No retrieval, verification, query building,
    23	// or store logic lives here.
    24	
    25	/** Downstream call policies from configuration (services.timeouts, ruling 9). Neptune and the
    26	  * DynamoDB service-state adapters retry reads themselves (live adapters get the services
    27	  * RetryPolicy), so only S3 reads retry here: one retry layer. Conditional writes never retry.
    28	  */
    29	final class Policies(cfg: ScholarChainConfig):
    30	  private val t = cfg.services.timeouts
    31	  val neptune = CallPolicy("neptune", t.neptuneMs.toLong)
    32	  val dynamodb = CallPolicy("dynamodb", t.dynamoDbMs.toLong)
    33	  val s3 = CallPolicy("s3", t.s3Ms.toLong, retryReads = true)
    34	  // SQS calls are never retried (ruling 9: reads only; a receive is not idempotent).
    35	  val sqs = CallPolicy("sqs", t.sqsMs.toLong)
    36	  val syncDeadlineMs: Long = cfg.operations.synchronousDeadlineMs.toLong
    37	
    38	  /** The whole synchronous workflow, on the workflow pool: a hard timeout at the sync deadline (the
    39	    * runtime's deadline is cooperative). A passed deadline is 504 and is no downstream failure; a
    40	    * RuntimeFailure is.
    41	    */
    42	  val runtime = CallPolicy(
    43	    "runtime",
    44	    syncDeadlineMs,
    45	    timeoutCode = ErrorCode.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED,
    46	    timeoutIsFailure = false,
    47	    interruptOnTimeout = false,
    48	    workflow = true
    49	  )
    50	
    51	object Probes:
    52	  /** Keys and IDs no store holds; a probe read proves the store answers, not that data exists. */
    53	
    54	  /** Under the audit prefix of the served runtime run, which query-api and the worker already read
    55	    * (repair-01 m1; SERVICES.md section 4 names the S3 permissions).
    56	    */
    57	  def objectKey(setup: RuntimeSetup): String =
    58	    s"${Audit.base("logs", setup.snapshot, setup.runId)}/readiness-probe"
    59	  val NodeId = "probe:readiness"
    60	  val EvidenceId: String = "ev-" + "0" * 32
    61	  val IdempotencyKey = "readiness-probe"
    62	  val JobId: String = "job-" + "0" * 32
    63	
    64	  def fail(e: Either[String, ?]): Unit = e.left.foreach(m => throw DownstreamFailed(m))
    65	
    66	/** GET /healthz (liveness: no downstream), GET /readyz (readiness: probes and breakers), and GET
    67	  * /metrics (API key required, ruling 1). Neither health route reveals configuration.
    68	  */
    69	object Ops:
    70	  def routes(
    71	      readiness: Vector[(String, () => Unit)],
    72	      ds: Downstreams,
    73	      metrics: Metrics,
    74	      metricsEnabled: Boolean
    75	  ): Seq[Route] =
    76	    Seq(
    77	      Route("GET", "/healthz", public = true)(_ => Response.ok(Status("ok"))),
    78	      Route("GET", "/readyz", public = true) { _ =>
    79	        val failing = readiness.filterNot((_, probe) => Try(probe()).isSuccess).map(_._1)
    80	        val down = (failing ++ ds.openBreakers).distinct.sorted
    81	        if down.isEmpty then Response.ok(Status("ready"))
    82	        else throw ApiError(ErrorCode.NOT_READY, s"not ready: ${down.mkString(", ")} unavailable")
    83	      }
    84	    ) ++ Option
    85	      .when(metricsEnabled)(Route("GET", "/metrics")(_ => Response(200, metrics.snapshot)))
    86	      .toSeq
    87	
    88	  def noQuery(r: Request): Unit =
    89	    if r.query.nonEmpty then
    90	      throw ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_FIELD, "this route takes no query parameters")
    91	
    92	/** query-api (ruling 1): POST /v1/query (sync or async), GET /v1/jobs/{jobId}, GET
    93	  * /v1/jobs/{jobId}/result, and GET /v1/explain/{queryId}.
    94	  */
    95	final class QueryService(
    96	    cfg: ScholarChainConfig,
    97	    backend: Backend,
    98	    ds: Downstreams,
    99	    log: JsonLog,
   100	    metrics: Metrics,
   101	    limiter: RateLimiter
   102	):
   103	  private val p = Policies(cfg)
   104	  private val setup = backend.setup
   105	  private val ports = backend.ports
   106	  private val IdempotencyKeyMaxChars = 255
   107	
   108	  def routes: Seq[Route] = Seq(
   109	    Route("POST", "/v1/query")(query),
   110	    Route("GET", "/v1/jobs/{jobId}")(job),
   111	    Route("GET", "/v1/jobs/{jobId}/result")(result),
   112	    Route("GET", "/v1/explain/{queryId}")(explain)
   113	  )
   114	
   115	  def readiness: Vector[(String, () => Unit)] = Vector(
   116	    "s3" -> (() => ds.call(p.s3)(ports.auditReader.get(Probes.objectKey(setup)))),
   117	    "dynamodb" -> (() =>
   118	      ds.call(p.dynamodb)(Probes.fail(backend.idempotency.get(Probes.IdempotencyKey)))
   119	    ),
   120	    "neptune" -> (() => ds.call(p.neptune)(ports.graph.vertex(Probes.NodeId)))
   121	  )
   122	
   123	  private def query(r: Request): Response =
   124	    Ops.noQuery(r)
   125	    val key = r.header("Idempotency-Key")
   126	    if key.exists(k => !IdFormat.isVisibleAscii(k) || k.length > IdempotencyKeyMaxChars) then
   127	      throw ApiError(
   128	        ErrorCode.INVALID_FIELD,
   129	        s"Idempotency-Key must be 1 to $IdempotencyKeyMaxChars visible ASCII characters"
   130	      )
   131	    val (body, question) = QueryValidation.parse(r.json(), cfg)
   132	    if !limiter.tryAcquire() then
   133	      throw ApiError(
   134	        ErrorCode.RATE_LIMITED,
   135	        s"at most ${cfg.services.queryApi.queryRatePerMinute} queries per minute per instance"
   136	      )
   137	    val hash = QueryValidation.requestHash(body)
   138	    // A fresh query ID per POST (the nonce): two equal requests without a key are two queries.
   139	    val minted = StableIds.queryId(
   140	      setup.runId,
   141	      StableIds.textHash(question),
   142	      StableIds.textHash(s"$hash ${Ids.fresh("nonce-")}"),
   143	      1
   144	    )
   145	    def run(): Response =
   146	      if body.execution == "async" then enqueue(r, body, minted) else answer(r, body, minted)
   147	    key match
   148	      case None    => run()
   149	      case Some(k) =>
   150	        ds.call(p.dynamodb)(backend.idempotency.claim(k, hash, minted)) match
   151	          case Right(Stored(LoadOutcome.Inserted, _))  => run()
   152	          case Right(Stored(LoadOutcome.Unchanged, s)) => replay(s.queryId)
   153	          case Right(Stored(LoadOutcome.Conflict, _))  =>
   154	            throw ApiError(
   155	              ErrorCode.IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT,
   156	              "this Idempotency-Key was used with a different request body"
   157	            )
   158	          case Left(_) =>
   159	            throw ApiError(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE, "the idempotency record was not stored")
   160	
   161	  private def answer(r: Request, body: QueryBody, queryId: String): Response =
   162	    val result = ds.call(p.runtime) {
   163	      RuntimeApi.answer(
   164	        QueryValidation.toRuntime(body, queryId),
   165	        ports,
   166	        setup,
   167	        p.syncDeadlineMs
   168	      ) match
   169	        case Right(res)                  => res
   170	        case Left(f) if f.deadlinePassed =>
   171	          throw ApiError(
   172	            ErrorCode.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED,
   173	            s"the synchronous deadline of ${p.syncDeadlineMs} ms passed before a plan was made"
   174	          )
   175	        case Left(f) => throw DownstreamFailed(f.stage.toString)
   176	    }
   177	    metrics.answer(result.audit)
   178	    result.audit.stages.foreach(log.stage(r, queryId, _))
   179	    if result.deadlinePassed then
   180	      throw ApiError(
   181	        ErrorCode.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED,
   182	        s"the synchronous deadline of ${p.syncDeadlineMs} ms passed; the abstention is recorded as $queryId"
   183	      )
   184	    Response.ok(SyncQueryResponse.of(result.answer))
   185	
   186	  /** Async (ruling 7): a QUEUED job record holding the canonical request, then one message holding
   187	    * only the job and query IDs; 202 with the job reference. A message that cannot be sent fails
   188	    * the job at once (QUEUED -> FAILED), so no job waits for a message that never comes.
   189	    */
   190	  private def enqueue(r: Request, body: QueryBody, queryId: String): Response =
   191	    val jobId = StableIds.jobId(queryId)
   192	    val request = write(body)
   193	    def stored(j: JobRecord) = j.queryId == queryId && j.request == request
   194	    val job = Try(ds.call(p.dynamodb)(backend.jobs.create(queryId, request))) match
   195	      case Success(Right(Stored(LoadOutcome.Conflict, _))) | Success(Left(_)) =>
   196	        throw ApiError(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE, "the job record was not stored")
   197	      case Success(Right(s)) => s.record
   198	      // A timed-out conditional put has an unknown outcome: read the record back (WO4).
   199	      case Failure(e: ApiError) => readJob(jobId).filter(stored).getOrElse(throw e)
   200	      case Failure(e)           => throw e
   201	    Try(ds.call(p.sqs)(backend.queue.send(JobMessage.of(queryId)))) match
   202	      case Success(_) => ()
   203	      case Failure(e) =>
   204	        Try(
   205	          ds.call(p.dynamodb)(
   206	            backend.jobs.transition(
   207	              jobId,
   208	              JobState.QUEUED,
   209	              JobState.FAILED,
   210	              error = Some(
   211	                JobError(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE, "the job could not be queued; submit again")
   212	              )
   213	            )
   214	          )
   215	        )
   216	        throw e match
   217	          case a: ApiError => a
   218	          case _           => ApiError(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE, "the job could not be queued")
   219	    metrics.add("jobsQueued")
   220	    log.event(
   221	      "query-api",
   222	      "job-queued",
   223	      "traceId" -> r.traceId,
   224	      "requestId" -> r.requestId,
   225	      "jobId" -> jobId,
   226	      "queryId" -> queryId
   227	    )
   228	    Response.ok(AsyncQueryResponse.of(job), 202)
   229	
   230	  private def readJob(jobId: String): Option[JobRecord] =
   231	    Try(ds.call(p.dynamodb)(backend.jobs.get(jobId))).toOption.flatMap(_.toOption).flatten
   232	
   233	  /** The stored response of an earlier request with the same key and body: the job reference when
   234	    * the first request was async, otherwise its stored answer.
   235	    */
   236	  private def replay(queryId: String): Response =
   237	    val replayed = Map("Idempotent-Replay" -> "true")
   238	    ds.call(p.dynamodb)(backend.jobs.get(StableIds.jobId(queryId))) match
   239	      case Right(Some(j)) => Response.ok(AsyncQueryResponse.of(j), 202).copy(headers = replayed)
   240	      case Left(_)        =>
   241	        throw ApiError(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE, "the job record could not be read")
   242	      case Right(None) =>
   243	        ds.call(p.s3)(RuntimeApi.storedAnswer(queryId, setup, ports.auditReader)) match
   244	          case Right(Some(a)) => Response.ok(SyncQueryResponse.of(a)).copy(headers = replayed)
   245	          case Right(None)    =>
   246	            throw ApiError(
   247	              ErrorCode.IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT,
   248	              "the first request with this Idempotency-Key has no stored answer: it is still running or it failed (use a new key after a failure)"
   249	            )
   250	          case Left(_) =>
   251	            throw ApiError(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE, "the stored answer could not be read")
   252	
   253	  private def jobOf(r: Request): JobRecord =
   254	    Ops.noQuery(r)
   255	    val id = r.params("jobId")
   256	    val notFound = ApiError(ErrorCode.JOB_NOT_FOUND, "no job with this ID")
   257	    if !IdFormat.isJobId(id) then throw notFound
   258	    ds.call(p.dynamodb)(backend.jobs.get(id)) match
   259	      case Right(Some(j)) => j
   260	      case Right(None)    => throw notFound
   261	      case Left(_)        =>
   262	        throw ApiError(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE, "the job record could not be read")
   263	
   264	  private def job(r: Request): Response = Response.ok(JobResponse.of(jobOf(r)))
   265	
   266	  /** SUCCEEDED: the stored answer; QUEUED or RUNNING: 409 JOB_NOT_FINISHED; FAILED: the job's typed
   267	    * error with its own status.
   268	    */
   269	  private def result(r: Request): Response =
   270	    val j = jobOf(r)
   271	    j.state match
   272	      case JobState.QUEUED | JobState.RUNNING =>
   273	        throw ApiError(
   274	          ErrorCode.JOB_NOT_FINISHED,
   275	          s"the job is ${j.state}; poll /v1/jobs/${j.jobId} until it is SUCCEEDED or FAILED"
   276	        )
   277	      case JobState.FAILED =>
   278	        val e = j.error.getOrElse(JobError(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR, "the job failed"))
   279	        throw ApiError(e.code, e.message)
   280	      case JobState.SUCCEEDED =>
   281	        ds.call(p.s3)(RuntimeApi.storedAnswer(j.queryId, setup, ports.auditReader)) match
   282	          case Right(Some(a)) => Response.ok(SyncQueryResponse.of(a, "asynchronous"))
   283	          case _              =>
   284	            throw ApiError(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE, "the stored answer could not be read")
   285	
   286	  private def explain(r: Request): Response =
   287	    Ops.noQuery(r)
   288	    val id = r.params("queryId")
   289	    val notFound = ApiError(ErrorCode.QUERY_NOT_FOUND, "no query with this ID")
   290	    if !IdFormat.isQueryId(id) then throw notFound
   291	    ds.call(p.s3)(RuntimeApi.audit(id, setup, ports.auditReader)) match
   292	      case Right(Some(a)) => Response.ok(ExplainResponse.of(a))
   293	      case Right(None)    => throw notFound
   294	      case Left(_)        =>
   295	        throw ApiError(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE, "the audit record could not be read")
   296	
   297	/** An opaque neighbor-page cursor: the next offset, bound to (node, direction, depth, edge types),
   298	  * so a cursor from another neighborhood is rejected.
   299	  */
   300	object Cursor:
   301	  def binding(nodeId: String, direction: String, depth: Int, edgeTypes: Seq[String]): String =
   302	    StableIds.hash(
   303	      "neighbors-cursor-v1",
   304	      nodeId,
   305	      direction,
   306	      depth.toString,
   307	      edgeTypes.mkString(",")
   308	    )
   309	  def encode(offset: Int, binding: String): String =
   310	    Base64.getUrlEncoder.withoutPadding.encodeToString(s"v1.$offset.$binding".getBytes(UTF_8))
   311	  def decode(cursor: String, binding: String): Option[Int] =
   312	    Try(new String(Base64.getUrlDecoder.decode(cursor), UTF_8)).toOption.collect {
   313	      case s"v1.$offset.$b" if b == binding && offset.toIntOption.exists(_ >= 0) => offset.toInt
   314	    }
   315	
   316	/** graph-evidence-api (ruling 1): evidence by ID, a graph node, and its bounded neighbors. */
   317	final class GraphEvidenceService(cfg: ScholarChainConfig, backend: Backend, ds: Downstreams):
   318	  private val p = Policies(cfg)
   319	  private val graph = backend.ports.graph
   320	  private val limits = graph.limits
   321	  private val edgeTypes = NeptuneContract.Edges.map(_.label).sorted
   322	  private val NeighborParams = Set("direction", "depth", "limit", "edgeTypes", "cursor")
   323	
   324	  def routes: Seq[Route] = Seq(
   325	    Route("GET", "/v1/evidence/{evidenceId}")(evidence),
   326	    Route("GET", "/v1/graph/nodes/{nodeId}")(node),
   327	    Route("GET", "/v1/graph/nodes/{nodeId}/neighbors")(neighbors)
   328	  )
   329	
   330	  def readiness: Vector[(String, () => Unit)] = Vector(
   331	    "neptune" -> (() => ds.call(p.neptune)(graph.vertex(Probes.NodeId))),
   332	    "dynamodb" -> (() => ds.call(p.dynamodb)(backend.evidence.evidenceItem(Probes.EvidenceId)))
   333	  )
   334	
   335	  private def evidence(r: Request): Response =
   336	    Ops.noQuery(r)
   337	    val id = r.params("evidenceId")
   338	    val notFound = ApiError(ErrorCode.EVIDENCE_NOT_FOUND, "no evidence with this ID")
   339	    if !IdFormat.isEvidenceId(id) then throw notFound
   340	    val item = ds.call(p.dynamodb)(backend.evidence.evidenceItem(id)).getOrElse(throw notFound)
   341	    ds.call(p.s3)(RuntimeApi.evidenceOf(item, backend.ports.objects)) match
   342	      case Right(e) => Response.ok(EvidenceResponse.of(e, cfg.services.evidenceMaxContentChars))
   343	      case Left(_)  =>
   344	        throw ApiError(
   345	          ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE,
   346	          "the evidence payload failed its integrity check"
   347	        )
   348	
   349	  private def nodeId(r: Request): String =
   350	    val id = r.params("nodeId")
   351	    if !IdFormat.isNodeId(id) then throw ApiError(ErrorCode.NODE_NOT_FOUND, "no node with this ID")
   352	    id
   353	
   354	  private def node(r: Request): Response =
   355	    Ops.noQuery(r)
   356	    val id = nodeId(r)
   357	    ds.call(p.neptune)(graph.vertex(id)) match
   358	      case Some(v) => Response.ok(NodeResponse(GraphViews.node(v)))
   359	      case None    => throw ApiError(ErrorCode.NODE_NOT_FOUND, "no node with this ID")
   360	
   361	  private def neighbors(r: Request): Response =
   362	    def bad(m: String) = ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_FIELD, m)
   363	    if !r.query.keySet.subsetOf(NeighborParams) then
   364	      throw bad(
   365	        s"unsupported query parameter; allowed: ${NeighborParams.toVector.sorted.mkString(", ")}"
   366	      )
   367	    if r.query.values.exists(_.size > 1) then throw bad("each query parameter may appear once")
   368	    def one(k: String) = r.query.get(k).flatMap(_.headOption)
   369	    def bounded(k: String, max: Int) =
   370	      one(k)
   371	        .flatMap(_.toIntOption)
   372	        .filter(n => n >= 1 && n <= max)
   373	        .getOrElse(throw bad(s"$k must be 1 to $max"))
   374	    val direction = one("direction") match
   375	      case Some("in")   => Direction.IN
   376	      case Some("out")  => Direction.OUT
   377	      case Some("both") => Direction.BOTH
   378	      case _            => throw bad("direction must be in, out, or both")
   379	    val depth = bounded("depth", limits.neighborsMaxDepth)
   380	    val limit = bounded("limit", limits.neighborsMaxLimit)
   381	    val types = one("edgeTypes")
   382	      .map(_.split(",", -1).toVector.map(_.trim))
   383	      .filter(ts => ts.nonEmpty && ts.forall(edgeTypes.contains))
   384	      .map(_.distinct.sorted)
   385	      .getOrElse(
   386	        throw bad(s"edgeTypes must be a comma-separated subset of ${edgeTypes.mkString(", ")}")
   387	      )
   388	    val id = nodeId(r)
   389	    val binding = Cursor.binding(id, direction.toString, depth, types)
   390	    val offset = one("cursor").fold(0)(c =>
   391	      Cursor
   392	        .decode(c, binding)
   393	        .getOrElse(
   394	          throw bad("cursor is not a cursor of this node, direction, depth, and edge types")
   395	        )
   396	    )
   397	    if offset > limits.maxResults - limit then
   398	      throw bad(s"the cursor is past the last page (at most ${limits.maxResults} nodes in all)")
   399	    val n = ds.call(p.neptune)(graph.neighbors(id, direction, depth, limit, types, offset))
   400	    if n.start.isEmpty then throw ApiError(ErrorCode.NODE_NOT_FOUND, "no node with this ID")
   401	    val next = offset + limit
   402	    Response.ok(
   403	      NeighborsResponse(
   404	        id,
   405	        direction.toString.toLowerCase,
   406	        depth,
   407	        limit,
   408	        types,
   409	        n.nodes.map(GraphViews.node),
   410	        n.edges.map(GraphViews.edge),
   411	        n.truncated,
   412	        Option.when(n.truncated && n.nodes.size == limit && next <= limits.maxResults - limit)(
   413	          Cursor.encode(next, binding)
   414	        )
   415	      )
   416	    )
```

### modules/services/src/main/scala/scholarchain/services/Worker.scala [CHANGED BY repair-01 Part A]
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.core.{ErrorCode, JobState}
     4	import scholarchain.core.config.ScholarChainConfig
     5	import scholarchain.rag.runtime.{RuntimeApi, Stage}
     6	import scholarchain.storage.{JobError, JobRecord, JobTransition, ReceivedJob}
     7	import upickle.default.read
     8	
     9	import scala.util.control.NonFatal
    10	import scala.util.{Success, Try}
    11	
    12	/** The async job worker (ruling 7). It long-polls the job queue and moves each job QUEUED ->
    13	  * RUNNING -> SUCCEEDED or FAILED with the job store's conditional writes. Rules:
    14	  *   - Only the worker whose QUEUED -> RUNNING write passed runs the workflow, so a redelivered
    15	  *     message never runs a job twice. When the outcome of that write is unknown (a timeout), the
    16	  *     job is not run.
    17	  *   - A message is deleted only after its job is in a final state (written by this worker, read
    18	  *     back, or found on redelivery).
    19	  *   - Stuck RUNNING: a job still RUNNING when its message comes back is left alone until the last
    20	  *     delivery (receiveCount >= services.queue.max-receive-count). Then it is moved RUNNING ->
    21	  *     FAILED (DEADLINE_EXCEEDED) and its message deleted. The visibility timeout exceeds the job
    22	  *     deadline plus one model timeout, so by then its worker has stopped or overrun.
    23	  *   - Poison: a malformed message, or one whose job does not exist, is never run and never
    24	  *     deleted; the queue's redrive policy moves it to the dead-letter queue after
    25	  *     max-receive-count receives.
    26	  *   - While any downstream breaker is open the worker does not receive (it fails fast).
    27	  */
    28	final class Worker(cfg: ScholarChainConfig, backend: Backend, log: JsonLog, metrics: Metrics):
    29	  private val w = cfg.services.worker
    30	  private val p = Policies(cfg)
    31	  private val jobs = backend.jobs
    32	  private val maxReceives = cfg.services.queue.maxReceiveCount
    33	  private val deadlineMs = cfg.runtime.question.deadlineMs.toLong
    34	  private val Service = "worker"
    35	
    36	  /** Store calls: one thread per poll loop plus one per HTTP thread (readiness probes); workflows:
    37	    * one slot per concurrent job, apart from them (repair-01 F1).
    38	    */
    39	  val ds: Downstreams =
    40	    Downstreams(
    41	      cfg.services.resilience,
    42	      w.concurrency + w.threads,
    43	      cfg.services.maxQueuedRequests,
    44	      metrics,
    45	      workflowThreads = w.concurrency
    46	    )
    47	
    48	  // A receive may wait poll-wait-seconds longer than an SQS call (services.timeouts.sqs-ms).
    49	  private val receivePolicy = p.sqs.copy(timeoutMs = p.sqs.timeoutMs + w.pollWaitSeconds * 1000L)
    50	
    51	  /** The whole job workflow, on the workflow pool: the runtime's cooperative deadline
    52	    * (runtime.question.deadline-ms) ends it; a hard timeout one model timeout later is the
    53	    * backstop, under the visibility timeout.
    54	    */
    55	  private val runtime = CallPolicy(
    56	    "runtime",
    57	    deadlineMs + cfg.runtime.llm.timeoutMs,
    58	    timeoutCode = ErrorCode.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED,
    59	    timeoutIsFailure = false,
    60	    workflow = true
    61	  )
    62	
    63	  def readiness: Vector[(String, () => Unit)] = Vector(
    64	    "dynamodb" -> (() => ds.call(p.dynamodb)(Probes.fail(jobs.get(Probes.JobId)))),
    65	    "s3" -> (() => ds.call(p.s3)(backend.ports.auditReader.get(Probes.objectKey(backend.setup)))),
    66	    "neptune" -> (() => ds.call(p.neptune)(backend.ports.graph.vertex(Probes.NodeId)))
    67	  )
    68	
    69	  @volatile private var running = false
    70	  private var threads = Vector.empty[Thread]
    71	
    72	  def start(): Unit = synchronized {
    73	    running = true
    74	    threads = Vector.tabulate(w.concurrency) { i =>
    75	      val t = Thread(() => loop(), s"worker-poll-${i + 1}")
    76	      t.setDaemon(true)
    77	      t.start()
    78	      t
    79	    }
    80	  }
    81	
    82	  /** Stops receiving; waits one poll for the loops, then stops the downstream pool. A job still
    83	    * running stays RUNNING and is ended by the stuck-RUNNING rule on a later delivery.
    84	    */
    85	  def stop(): Unit =
    86	    running = false
    87	    synchronized(threads).foreach(_.join((w.pollWaitSeconds + 1) * 1000L))
    88	    ds.shutdown()
    89	
    90	  private def loop(): Unit =
    91	    while running do
    92	      // No receive while a breaker is open or every workflow slot is held by an overrun job.
    93	      if ds.openBreakers.nonEmpty || !ds.workflowSlotFree then Thread.sleep(1000)
    94	      else
    95	        try pollOnce()
    96	        catch
    97	          case NonFatal(_) =>
    98	            log.event(Service, "poll-failed")
    99	            Thread.sleep(1000)
   100	
   101	  /** One receive of at most one message, and its processing; the number of messages received. */
   102	  def pollOnce(): Int =
   103	    val got = ds.call(receivePolicy)(
   104	      backend.queue.receive(1, w.pollWaitSeconds, w.visibilityTimeoutSeconds)
   105	    )
   106	    got.foreach(handle)
   107	    got.size
   108	
   109	  private def event(what: String, m: ReceivedJob, fields: (String, ujson.Value)*): Unit =
   110	    log.event(
   111	      Service,
   112	      what,
   113	      (Seq[(String, ujson.Value)]("messageId" -> m.messageId, "receiveCount" -> m.receiveCount) ++
   114	        m.message.toOption.toSeq
   115	          .flatMap(j => Seq("jobId" -> ujson.Str(j.jobId), "queryId" -> ujson.Str(j.queryId))) ++
   116	        fields)*
   117	    )
   118	
   119	  private def handle(m: ReceivedJob): Unit =
   120	    metrics.add("queueReceives")
   121	    m.message match
   122	      case Left(_)    => poison(m, "malformed message")
   123	      case Right(msg) =>
   124	        Try(ds.call(p.dynamodb)(jobs.get(msg.jobId))) match
   125	          case Success(Right(None))    => poison(m, "no such job")
   126	          case Success(Right(Some(j))) =>
   127	            j.state match
   128	              case JobState.SUCCEEDED | JobState.FAILED =>
   129	                metrics.add("duplicateDeliveries")
   130	                event("duplicate-delivery", m, "state" -> j.state.toString)
   131	                delete(m)
   132	              case JobState.RUNNING if m.receiveCount >= maxReceives =>
   133	                val error = JobError(
   134	                  ErrorCode.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED,
   135	                  s"the job did not finish within $maxReceives deliveries; submit the query again"
   136	                )
   137	                val (mine, now) =
   138	                  move(j.jobId, JobState.RUNNING, JobState.FAILED, None, Some(error))
   139	                if mine then metrics.add("stuckJobsFailed")
   140	                event("stuck-running", m, "failed" -> mine)
   141	                settle(m, now)
   142	              case JobState.RUNNING => event("running-elsewhere", m)
   143	              case JobState.QUEUED  => begin(m, j)
   144	          case _ =>
   145	            event("job-read-failed", m) // the message comes back after its visibility timeout
   146	
   147	  private def poison(m: ReceivedJob, why: String): Unit =
   148	    metrics.add("poisonMessages")
   149	    event("poison-message", m, "reason" -> why)
   150	
   151	  private def begin(m: ReceivedJob, j: JobRecord): Unit =
   152	    Try(read[QueryBody](j.request)).toOption match
   153	      case None =>
   154	        val error = JobError(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR, "the stored request does not decode")
   155	        val (_, now) = move(j.jobId, JobState.QUEUED, JobState.FAILED, None, Some(error))
   156	        settle(m, now)
   157	      case Some(_) if !ds.workflowSlotFree =>
   158	        // Not claimed: the job stays QUEUED and its message comes back (repair-01 F1).
   159	        event("no-workflow-slot", m)
   160	      case Some(body) =>
   161	        val first = Some(Stage.QUERY_PLANNING.toString)
   162	        val (mine, now) = move(j.jobId, JobState.QUEUED, JobState.RUNNING, first, None)
   163	        if mine then run(m, j, body)
   164	        else
   165	          event("claim-refused", m, "state" -> now.fold("unknown")(_.state.toString))
   166	          settle(m, now)
   167	
   168	  private def run(m: ReceivedJob, j: JobRecord, body: QueryBody): Unit =
   169	    val t0 = System.nanoTime()
   170	    var failedStage: Option[String] = None
   171	    val outcome: Either[JobError, Option[String]] =
   172	      try
   173	        val res = ds.call(runtime) {
   174	          RuntimeApi.answer(
   175	            QueryValidation.toRuntime(body, j.queryId),
   176	            backend.ports,
   177	            backend.setup,
   178	            deadlineMs
   179	          ) match
   180	            case Right(res)                  => Right(res)
   181	            case Left(f) if f.deadlinePassed => Left(f)
   182	            case Left(f)                     =>
   183	              failedStage = Some(f.stage.toString)
   184	              throw DownstreamFailed(f.stage.toString) // counts against the runtime breaker
   185	        }
   186	        res match
   187	          case Left(f) =>
   188	            Left(
   189	              JobError(
   190	                ErrorCode.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED,
   191	                s"the job deadline of $deadlineMs ms passed in ${f.stage}"
   192	              )
   193	            )
   194	          case Right(r) =>
   195	            metrics.answer(r.audit)
   196	            r.audit.stages.foreach(log.jobStage(j.jobId, j.queryId, _))
   197	            val last = r.audit.stages.lastOption.map(_.stage.toString)
   198	            if r.deadlinePassed then
   199	              Left(
   200	                JobError(
   201	                  ErrorCode.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED,
   202	                  s"the job deadline of $deadlineMs ms passed; the abstention is recorded"
   203	                )
   204	              )
   205	            else Right(last)
   206	      catch
   207	        case e: ApiError if e.code == ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE =>
   208	          Left(JobError(e.code, s"the ${failedStage.getOrElse("runtime")} stage failed"))
   209	        case e: ApiError => Left(JobError(e.code, e.message))
   210	        case NonFatal(_) => Left(JobError(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR, "unexpected internal failure"))
   211	    val (to, stage, error) = outcome match
   212	      case Right(last) => (JobState.SUCCEEDED, last, None)
   213	      case Left(e)     => (JobState.FAILED, failedStage, Some(e))
   214	    val (_, now) = move(j.jobId, JobState.RUNNING, to, stage, error)
   215	    if now.exists(_.state == to) then
   216	      metrics.add(if to == JobState.SUCCEEDED then "jobsSucceeded" else "jobsFailed")
   217	    event(
   218	      "job-finished",
   219	      m,
   220	      "state" -> now.fold("unknown")(_.state.toString),
   221	      "error" -> error.fold(ujson.Null)(e => ujson.Str(e.code.toString)),
   222	      "latencyMs" -> ((System.nanoTime() - t0) / 1000000).toDouble
   223	    )
   224	    settle(m, now)
   225	
   226	  /** A conditional transition: (whether this call moved the job, the record stored now). After a
   227	    * failed or timed-out write the outcome is unknown, so the record is read back (WO4) and the
   228	    * move never counts as this worker's.
   229	    */
   230	  private def move(
   231	      jobId: String,
   232	      from: JobState,
   233	      to: JobState,
   234	      stage: Option[String],
   235	      error: Option[JobError]
   236	  ): (Boolean, Option[JobRecord]) =
   237	    Try(ds.call(p.dynamodb)(jobs.transition(jobId, from, to, stage, error))) match
   238	      case Success(Right(JobTransition.Moved(job)))   => (true, Some(job))
   239	      case Success(Right(JobTransition.Refused(cur))) => (false, cur)
   240	      case _                                          =>
   241	        (false, Try(ds.call(p.dynamodb)(jobs.get(jobId))).toOption.flatMap(_.toOption).flatten)
   242	
   243	  /** Deletes the message only when its job is in a final state; otherwise it comes back. */
   244	  private def settle(m: ReceivedJob, now: Option[JobRecord]): Unit =
   245	    if now.exists(_.state.isFinal) then delete(m)
   246	    else event("message-kept", m)
   247	
   248	  private def delete(m: ReceivedJob): Unit =
   249	    val ok = Try(ds.call(p.sqs)(backend.queue.delete(m.receiptHandle))).getOrElse(false)
   250	    if !ok then event("delete-failed", m) // a later delivery finds the final state and deletes it
```

### modules/services/src/main/scala/scholarchain/services/main/LocalRun.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.services.main
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.services.{Apps, HttpService, JsonLog, Launcher}
     4	
     5	/** The local runner (ruling 13, runMain only): query-api, graph-evidence-api, and worker in one JVM
     6	  * on 127.0.0.1, sharing one fake-mode backend (so the in-memory job queue and the file-backed
     7	  * evidence are shared). Fake mode only: --live is refused.
     8	  */
     9	object LocalRun:
    10	  /** The started services, or the exit code. port maps each configured port (tests pass 0). */
    11	  def start(
    12	      args: Seq[String],
    13	      env: Map[String, String] = sys.env,
    14	      out: String => Unit = println,
    15	      log: JsonLog = JsonLog.stdout,
    16	      port: Int => Int = identity
    17	  ): Either[Int, Vector[HttpService]] =
    18	    if args.contains("--live") then
    19	      out("usage error: LocalRun runs fake mode only; start the service mains for live mode")
    20	      Left(2)
    21	    else
    22	      Launcher.prepare(args, env, out).map { (_, cfg, auth, b) =>
    23	        val s = cfg.services
    24	        Vector(
    25	          Apps.queryApi(cfg, b, auth, log) -> s.queryApi.port,
    26	          Apps.graphEvidenceApi(cfg, b, auth, log) -> s.graphEvidenceApi.port,
    27	          Apps.worker(cfg, b, auth, log) -> s.worker.port
    28	        ).map { (svc, p) =>
    29	          log.event(svc.name, "started", "port" -> svc.start("127.0.0.1", port(p)))
    30	          svc
    31	        }
    32	      }
    33	
    34	  def main(args: Array[String]): Unit =
    35	    start(args.toSeq) match
    36	      case Left(code)  => sys.exit(code)
    37	      case Right(svcs) => sys.addShutdownHook(svcs.reverse.foreach(_.stop()))
```

### modules/services/src/test/scala/scholarchain/services/BuildingBlocksSpec.scala [CHANGED BY repair-01 Part A]
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.ErrorCode
     5	import scholarchain.core.config.ResilienceConfig
     6	import scholarchain.storage.TransientStoreException
     7	
     8	import java.util.concurrent.{CountDownLatch, Executors, TimeUnit}
     9	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger
    10	
    11	/** The breaker, retries, rate limiter, cursor, IDs, and key check on their own. */
    12	class BuildingBlocksSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    13	  private def thrown(body: => Any): ApiError =
    14	    intercept[ApiError](body)
    15	
    16	  test("breaker: opens after the threshold, fails fast while open, half-opens after the interval") {
    17	    var now = 0L
    18	    val b = Breaker(3, 1000, () => now)
    19	    assert(!b.failure() && !b.failure() && !b.isOpen)
    20	    b.success() // a success resets the count
    21	    assert(!b.failure() && !b.failure() && b.failure() && b.isOpen)
    22	    now = 999
    23	    assert(b.isOpen)
    24	    now = 1000
    25	    assert(!b.isOpen) // half-open: one trial passes
    26	    assert(b.failure() && b.isOpen) // and its failure reopens at once
    27	    now = 2000
    28	    b.success()
    29	    assert(!b.isOpen && !b.failure())
    30	  }
    31	
    32	  test("downstream calls: timeout, retries only for retryable reads, breaker per downstream") {
    33	    val m = Metrics()
    34	    var slept = Vector.empty[Long]
    35	    val ds =
    36	      Downstreams(ResilienceConfig(3, 100, 1000, 2, 60000), 2, 2, m, sleep = ms => slept :+= ms)
    37	    try
    38	      val n = AtomicInteger()
    39	      val ok = ds.call(CallPolicy("s3", 1000, retryReads = true)) {
    40	        if n.incrementAndGet() < 3 then throw TransientStoreException("throttled") else "done"
    41	      }
    42	      assert(ok == "done" && n.get == 3 && slept == Vector(100L, 200L))
    43	      // Without retryReads (adapters that retry themselves, writes) one attempt only.
    44	      n.set(0)
    45	      assert(thrown(ds.call(CallPolicy("neptune", 1000)) {
    46	        n.incrementAndGet(); throw TransientStoreException("x")
    47	      }).code == ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE)
    48	      assert(n.get == 1)
    49	      // A non-retryable error is not retried even for reads.
    50	      n.set(0)
    51	      thrown(ds.call(CallPolicy("dynamodb", 1000, retryReads = true)) {
    52	        n.incrementAndGet(); throw IllegalStateException("x")
    53	      })
    54	      assert(n.get == 1)
    55	      assert(
    56	        thrown(
    57	          ds.call(CallPolicy("dynamodb", 1000))(throw IllegalStateException("x"))
    58	        ).code == ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE
    59	      )
    60	      assert(
    61	        thrown(ds.call(CallPolicy("dynamodb", 1000))("never runs")).code == ErrorCode.CIRCUIT_OPEN
    62	      )
    63	      assert(
    64	        ds.openBreakers == Vector("dynamodb") && m
    65	          .snapshot("counters")("circuitOpened.dynamodb")
    66	          .num == 1
    67	      )
    68	      assert(
    69	        thrown(
    70	          ds.call(CallPolicy("x", 50))(Thread.sleep(2000))
    71	        ).code == ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT
    72	      )
    73	      // An ApiError from the body is the route's own outcome, not a failure.
    74	      (1 to 3).foreach(_ =>
    75	        thrown(ds.call(CallPolicy("y", 1000))(throw ApiError(ErrorCode.NODE_NOT_FOUND, "x")))
    76	      )
    77	      assert(!ds.openBreakers.contains("y"))
    78	      // A failed check inside a downstream is a server defect (repair-01 m5), not a client error.
    79	      val bug = thrown(ds.call(CallPolicy("z", 1000))(require(false, "internal-detail")))
    80	      assert(bug.code == ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR && !bug.message.contains("internal-detail"))
    81	      assert(!ds.openBreakers.contains("z"))
    82	    finally ds.shutdown()
    83	  }
    84	
    85	  test("waiting to start is no downstream failure: 503 OVERLOADED, no breaker (repair-01 F1)") {
    86	    val m = Metrics()
    87	    // One store thread, a queue of 4, one workflow slot; one counted failure would open a breaker.
    88	    val ds = Downstreams(ResilienceConfig(1, 100, 1000, 1, 60000), 1, 4, m, workflowThreads = 1)
    89	    val release = CountDownLatch(1)
    90	    val bg = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(3)
    91	    def hold(p: CallPolicy): CountDownLatch =
    92	      val entered = CountDownLatch(1)
    93	      bg.submit(() => ds.call(p) { entered.countDown(); release.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS) })
    94	      assert(entered.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
    95	      entered
    96	    try
    97	      val ran = AtomicInteger()
    98	      hold(CallPolicy("neptune", 10000)) // the only store thread
    99	      // Queued behind it past its timeout: given up, 503, never run, and no breaker failure.
   100	      (1 to 3).foreach { _ =>
   101	        assert(
   102	          thrown(ds.call(CallPolicy("dynamodb", 100))(ran.incrementAndGet())).code ==
   103	            ErrorCode.OVERLOADED
   104	        )
   105	      }
   106	      // The workflow pool has no queue: a workflow that cannot start is refused at once.
   107	      assert(ds.workflowSlotFree)
   108	      hold(CallPolicy("runtime", 10000, workflow = true))
   109	      assert(!ds.workflowSlotFree)
   110	      val t0 = System.nanoTime()
   111	      val wf = CallPolicy("runtime", 10000, workflow = true)
   112	      assert(thrown(ds.call(wf)(ran.incrementAndGet())).code == ErrorCode.OVERLOADED)
   113	      assert((System.nanoTime() - t0) / 1000000 < 1000)
   114	      assert(
   115	        ds.openBreakers.isEmpty && !m.snapshot("counters").obj.keys.exists(_.startsWith("circuit"))
   116	      )
   117	      release.countDown()
   118	      // A timeout counts from the call's start: 300 ms waiting plus 300 ms running fits in 500 ms.
   119	      val busy = CountDownLatch(1)
   120	      bg.submit(() => ds.call(CallPolicy("s3", 5000)) { busy.countDown(); Thread.sleep(300); 0 })
   121	      assert(busy.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   122	      assert(ds.call(CallPolicy("s3", 500)) { Thread.sleep(300); "ok" } == "ok")
   123	      assert(ran.get == 0 && ds.openBreakers.isEmpty)
   124	    finally
   125	      release.countDown()
   126	      bg.shutdownNow()
   127	      ds.shutdown()
   128	  }
   129	
   130	  test("rate limiter: a sliding minute") {
   131	    var now = 0L
   132	    val r = RateLimiter(2, () => now)
   133	    assert(r.tryAcquire() && r.tryAcquire() && !r.tryAcquire())
   134	    now = 59999
   135	    assert(!r.tryAcquire())
   136	    now = 60000 // both slots of time 0 have left the window
   137	    assert(r.tryAcquire() && r.tryAcquire() && !r.tryAcquire())
   138	  }
   139	
   140	  test("cursor: round trip, bound to its walk, and opaque garbage rejected") {
   141	    val b = Cursor.binding("scr:x", "BOTH", 2, Seq("AUTHORED"))
   142	    assert(Cursor.decode(Cursor.encode(5, b), b).contains(5))
   143	    assert(
   144	      Cursor.decode(Cursor.encode(5, b), Cursor.binding("scr:x", "IN", 2, Seq("AUTHORED"))).isEmpty
   145	    )
   146	    assert(Cursor.decode(Cursor.encode(-1, b), b).isEmpty)
   147	    assert(Cursor.decode("!!!", b).isEmpty && Cursor.decode("", b).isEmpty)
   148	  }
   149	
   150	  test("IDs: valid client values echoed, anything else replaced") {
   151	    assert(Ids.orFresh(Some("abc-1.2:3_x"), "req-") == "abc-1.2:3_x")
   152	    Seq(None, Some(""), Some("a b"), Some("x" * 129), Some("é")).foreach(v =>
   153	      assert(Ids.orFresh(v, "req-").matches("req-[0-9a-f]{32}"), v)
   154	    )
   155	  }
   156	
   157	  test("API key: exact match only, any length; the key is never printed") {
   158	    val a = ApiKeyAuth("s3cr3t")
   159	    assert(a.accepts(Some("s3cr3t")))
   160	    Seq(None, Some(""), Some("s3cr3"), Some("s3cr3tt"), Some("S3CR3T"), Some(" s3cr3t")).foreach(
   161	      v => assert(!a.accepts(v), v)
   162	    )
   163	    assert(!a.toString.contains("s3cr3t"))
   164	    intercept[IllegalArgumentException](ApiKeyAuth(""))
   165	  }
```

### modules/services/src/test/scala/scholarchain/services/CorpusQuerySpec.scala [CHANGED BY repair-01 Part A]

     1	package scholarchain.services

    12	
    13	/** Work order 11: the corpus window summaries wired into the services (runtime.window-summary.

    15	  */
    16	class CorpusQuerySpec extends AnyFunSuite:

    23	  test("B13 shape: corpus components in 2000-2014 answered with VERIFIED claims from the summary") {

    31	          assert(a.claims.nonEmpty, r.body)

    34	          assert(a.answer.verification.unsupported == 0 && a.answer.verification.conflicting == 0)

    51	          val x = read[ExplainResponse](q.get(a.links.explain).body)
    52	          assert(x.verification.verified == a.claims.size)
    53	        }
    54	      }
    55	      // The served key is a runtime run ID input.
    56	      assert(w.backend.setup.runId != withWorld()(_.backend.setup.runId))
    57	    }
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  test("an empty runtime.window-summary.load-run leaves the port unset: corpus questions abstain") {
    61	    withWorld(summaries = true) { w =>

    63	        val r = q.post("/v1/query", queryJson(b13))
    64	        assert(r.status == 200, r.body)

    72	  }
    73	
    74	  test("LocalRun passes --window-summary-root through; a bad or missing object stops startup") {
    75	    val root = Files.createTempDirectory("services-test-")
    76	    try
    77	      val stores = writeStores(root.resolve("stores"))
    78	      val env = baseEnv + ("SCHOLARCHAIN_WINDOW_SUMMARY_RUN" -> summaryLoadRun)
    79	      def start(extra: Vector[String]) =
    80	        val out = ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
    81	        (
    82	          LocalRun.start(stores ++ extra, env, out += _, JsonLog(_ => ()), _ => 0),
    83	          out.mkString("\n")
    84	        )
    85	      val refused = s"failed: the window-summary object $summaryKey was refused: "
    86	
    87	      // Malformed content: exit 1, the key and the code are named, the content is not.
    88	      val marker = "fixture-content-that-must-not-be-echoed"
    89	      val bad = writeSummaries(root.resolve("bad"), s"""{"window": "$marker"}\n""".getBytes(UTF_8))
    90	      val (b, badOut) = start(bad)
    91	      assert(b == Left(1), badOut)
    92	      assert(badOut.contains(refused + "MALFORMED line 1"), badOut)
    93	      assert(!badOut.contains(marker), badOut)
    94	
    95	      // No object under the root.
    96	      val (m, missingOut) = start(Vector("--window-summary-root", root.resolve("none").toString))
    97	      assert(m == Left(1) && missingOut.contains(refused + "OBJECT_MISSING"), missingOut)
    98	
    99	      // A valid object: the three services start and name the served key.
   100	      val cfg = scholarchain.core.config.ConfigLoader.load(env).toOption.get
   101	      val good = writeSummaries(
   102	        root.resolve("good"),
   103	        scholarchain.storage.WindowSummaries.render(summaryRows(cfg.graph.windows))
   104	      )
   105	      val (s, goodOut) = start(good)
   106	      val svcs = s.fold(c => fail(s"exit $c: $goodOut"), identity)
   107	      svcs.foreach(_.stop())
   108	      assert(svcs.size == 3 && goodOut.contains(s"window summaries: $summaryKey"), goodOut)
   109	    finally deleteTree(root)
   110	  }
   111	
   112	  test(
   113	    "graph-evidence-api never opens the window-summary object; same runtime run (repair-01 m3)"
   114	  ) {
   115	    withWorld(loadRun, summaries = true) { w =>
   116	      // query-api answers a corpus question and stores its evidence.
   117	      val a = w.query() { q => read[SyncQueryResponse](q.post("/v1/query", queryJson(b13)).body) }
   118	      assert(!a.answer.abstained, a.answer)
   119	      // Without the object, a question-answering service cannot start; graph-evidence-api can.
   120	      Files.delete(w.root.resolve("window-summary").resolve(summaryKey))
   121	      val args = scholarchain.storage.cli.Cli
   122	        .parse(w.args, Set("live"), w.args.collect { case s"--$o" => o }.toSet)
   123	        .toOption
   124	        .get
   125	      val out = ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
   126	      val asked = Launcher.backend(args, w.cfg, out += _)
   127	      assert(asked.left.exists(_.contains("OBJECT_MISSING")), asked)
   128	      out.clear()
   129	      val ge = Launcher
   130	        .backend(args, w.cfg, out += _, answers = false)
   131	        .fold(e => fail(s"$e: ${out.mkString("\n")}"), identity)
   132	      assert(ge.ports.windowSummaries.isEmpty)
   133	      assert(
   134	        out.contains(
   135	          s"window summaries: $summaryKey not opened (this service answers no questions)"
   136	        )
   137	      )
   138	      // The key is still a run ID input, so evidence written by query-api resolves.
   139	      assert(ge.setup.runId == w.backend.setup.runId)
   140	      w.graphEvidence(ge) { g =>
   141	        assert(g.get(s"/v1/evidence/${a.claims.head.evidenceIds.head}").status == 200)
   142	      }
   143	      // The main of graph-evidence-api starts the same way.
   144	      val svc = Launcher
   145	        .start(
   146	          w.args,
   147	          Apps.graphEvidenceApi(_, _, _, _),
   148	          _ => 0,
   149	          w.env,
   150	          out += _,
   151	          JsonLog(_ => ()),
   152	          answers = false
   153	        )
   154	        .fold(c => fail(s"exit $c: ${out.mkString("\n")}"), identity)
   155	      svc.stop()
   156	    }
   157	  }

    77	    withWorld() { w =>
    78	      w.graphEvidence() { c =>
    79	        val base = s"/v1/graph/nodes/${enc(grace)}/neighbors"
    80	        def q(p: String) = c.get(s"$base?$p")
    81	        val ok = "direction=both&depth=1&limit=10&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH"
    82	        val r = q(ok)
    83	        assert(r.status == 200, r.body)
    84	        val n = read[NeighborsResponse](r.body)
    85	        assert(
    86	          n.nodes.map(_.nodeId).toSet == Set(ada, edsger) && !n.truncated && n.nextCursor.isEmpty
    87	        )
    88	        assert(n.edges.forall(_.edgeType == "COAUTHORED_WITH") && n.edges.size == 2)
    89	        Vector(
    90	          "direction=sideways&depth=1&limit=10&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH",
    91	          "depth=1&limit=10&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH",
    92	          "direction=both&depth=0&limit=10&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH",
    93	          "direction=both&depth=4&limit=10&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH",
    94	          "direction=both&depth=x&limit=10&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH",
    95	          "direction=both&depth=1&limit=0&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH",
    96	          "direction=both&depth=1&limit=201&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH",
    97	          "direction=both&depth=1&limit=10",
    98	          "direction=both&depth=1&limit=10&edgeTypes=",
    99	          "direction=both&depth=1&limit=10&edgeTypes=KNOWS",
   100	          "direction=both&depth=1&limit=10&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH,",
   101	          s"$ok&depth=2",
   102	          s"$ok&maxNodes=5000",
   103	          s"$ok&cursor=garbage"
   104	        ).foreach(p => code(q(p), "INVALID_FIELD", 400))
   105	        // depth 3 and limit 200 are the inclusive maxima.
   106	        assert(q("direction=out&depth=3&limit=200&edgeTypes=AUTHORED,HAS_TOPIC").status == 200)
   107	        code(
   108	          c.get(s"/v1/graph/nodes/${enc("dblp:conf/fx/W999")}/neighbors?$ok"),
   109	          "NODE_NOT_FOUND",
   110	          404
   111	        )
   112	
   113	        // Pages of one node concatenate to the whole walk; a cursor is bound to its walk.
   114	        val walk = "direction=both&depth=2&limit=1&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH,AUTHORED"
   115	        var cursor: Option[String] = None
   116	        val pages = Vector.newBuilder[String]
   117	        var guard = 0
   118	        while
   119	          val p = read[NeighborsResponse](q(walk + cursor.fold("")(x => s"&cursor=$x")).body)
   120	          pages ++= p.nodes.map(_.nodeId)
   121	          cursor = p.nextCursor
   122	          guard += 1
   123	          cursor.isDefined && guard < 50
   124	        do ()
   125	        val all = read[NeighborsResponse](
   126	          q("direction=both&depth=2&limit=200&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH,AUTHORED").body
   127	        )
   128	        assert(pages.result() == all.nodes.map(_.nodeId) && all.nodes.size > 2)
   129	        val first = read[NeighborsResponse](q(walk).body).nextCursor.get
   130	        code(
   131	          q(s"direction=in&depth=2&limit=1&edgeTypes=COAUTHORED_WITH,AUTHORED&cursor=$first"),
   132	          "INVALID_FIELD",
   133	          400
   134	        )
   135	        code(
   136	          c.get(s"/v1/graph/nodes/${enc(ada)}/neighbors?$walk&cursor=$first"),
   137	          "INVALID_FIELD",
   138	          400
   139	        )
   140	        // Edge types are a set: order and repeats do not change the binding.
   141	        assert(
   142	          q(
   143	            s"direction=both&depth=2&limit=1&edgeTypes=AUTHORED,COAUTHORED_WITH,AUTHORED&cursor=$first"
   144	          ).status == 200
   145	        )
   146	      }
   147	    }
   148	  }
   149	
   150	  test("a slow store is 504, a failing one 502, then the breaker fails fast and readiness drops") {
   151	    withWorld(
   152	      "services.timeouts.dynamodb-ms = 200\nservices.resilience.breaker-failure-threshold = 2"

   155	        code(c.get("/v1/evidence/ev-" + "b" * 32), "DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT", 504)
