   159	        code(c.get("/v1/evidence/ev-" + "b" * 32), "DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE", 502)
   160	        code(c.get("/v1/evidence/ev-" + "b" * 32), "CIRCUIT_OPEN", 503)
   161	        // Other downstreams keep working; readiness reports the open breaker; liveness is fine.
   162	        assert(c.get(s"/v1/graph/nodes/${enc(ada)}").status == 200)
   163	        val ready = c.get("/readyz", Seq())
   164	        code(ready, "NOT_READY", 503)
   165	        assert(ready.body("message").str == "not ready: dynamodb unavailable")
   166	        assert(c.get("/healthz", Seq()).status == 200)
   167	      }
   168	    }
   169	  }
   170	
   171	  test(
   172	    "an IllegalArgumentException inside a downstream call is 500 with no detail (repair-01 m5)"
   173	  ) {
   174	    withWorld("services.resilience.breaker-failure-threshold = 1") { w =>
   175	      val bad = "ev-" + "d" * 32
   176	      val m = Metrics()
   177	      val b = lookup(w)(id => require(id != bad, "internal-detail-of-a-server-check"))
   178	      w.serve(Apps.graphEvidenceApi(w.cfg, b, w.auth, log, m)) { c =>
   179	        (1 to 2).foreach { _ =>
   180	          val r = c.get(s"/v1/evidence/$bad")
   181	          expectError(r, "INTERNAL_ERROR", 500)
   182	          assert(r.body("message").str == "unexpected internal failure")
   183	          assert(!r.body.render().contains("internal-detail"))
   184	        }
   185	        // A server defect is no failure of the store: no breaker, and readiness holds.
   186	        assert(c.get("/readyz", Seq()).status == 200)
   187	        assert(!m.snapshot("counters").obj.keys.exists(_.startsWith("circuitOpened")))
   188	        // Route validation is still a client error.
   189	        code(c.get(s"/v1/graph/nodes/${enc(ada)}/neighbors?direction=up"), "INVALID_FIELD", 400)
   190	      }
   191	      assert(!logged.exists(_.contains("internal-detail")))
   192	    }
   193	  }
   194	
   195	  test("beyond its threads and queue a service sheds load with 503 OVERLOADED") {
   196	    withWorld(
   197	      "services.graph-evidence-api.threads = 1\nservices.max-queued-requests = 1\nservices.timeouts.dynamodb-ms = 10000"
   198	    ) { w =>
   199	      val entered = CountDownLatch(1)
   200	      val release = CountDownLatch(1)
   201	      val slow = lookup(w) { _ =>
   202	        entered.countDown()
   203	        release.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
   204	      }
   205	      w.graphEvidence(slow) { c =>
   206	        val pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2)
   207	        try
   208	          val busy = pool.submit(() => c.get("/v1/evidence/ev-" + "c" * 32))
   209	          assert(entered.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
   210	          val queued = pool.submit(() => c.get("/healthz", Seq()))
   211	          Thread.sleep(300) // let the second request reach the queue
   212	          code(c.get("/healthz", Seq()), "OVERLOADED", 503)
   213	          release.countDown()
   214	          assert(busy.get(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS).status == 404)
   215	          assert(queued.get(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS).status == 200)
   216	        finally
   217	          release.countDown()
   218	          pool.shutdownNow()
   219	      }
   220	    }
   221	  }

### modules/services/src/test/scala/scholarchain/services/LifecycleSpec.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.{JobState, StableIds, VerificationStatus}
     5	import scholarchain.services.main.LocalRun
     6	import upickle.default.read
     7	
     8	/** The full query-to-evidence lifecycle with fakes (work order 6 item 2): query-api,
     9	  * graph-evidence-api, and the worker on one backend, over real sockets; and the local runner.
    10	  */
    11	class LifecycleSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    12	  import ServiceFixtures.*
    13	
    14	  /** A cited evidence ID resolves with provenance; explain names it and the verified count. */
    15	  private def traced(q: Client, g: Client, a: SyncQueryResponse): Unit =
    16	    val verified = a.claims.filter(_.verificationStatus == VerificationStatus.VERIFIED)
    17	    assert(verified.nonEmpty, a)
    18	    val ev = verified.head.evidenceIds.head
    19	    assert(a.links.evidence.contains(s"/v1/evidence/$ev"))
    20	    val e = g.get(s"/v1/evidence/$ev")
    21	    assert(e.status == 200, e.body)
    22	    val item = read[EvidenceResponse](e.body)
    23	    assert(item.evidenceId == ev && item.provenance.runId.nonEmpty)
    24	    val x = q.get(a.links.explain)
    25	    assert(x.status == 200, x.body)
    26	    val plan = read[ExplainResponse](x.body)
    27	    assert(plan.queryId == a.queryId && plan.llm == "STUBBED")
    28	    assert((plan.graphEvidenceIds ++ plan.semanticEvidenceIds).contains(ev))
    29	    assert(plan.verification.verified == verified.size)
    30	
    31	  test("query to evidence: sync and async answers, cited evidence, and explain") {
    32	    withWorld("services.worker.poll-wait-seconds = 1") { w =>
    33	      val worker = Worker(w.cfg, w.backend, log, Metrics())
    34	      try
    35	        w.query() { q =>
    36	          w.graphEvidence() { g =>
    37	            // Sync.
    38	            val s = q.post("/v1/query", queryJson())
    39	            assert(s.status == 200, s.body)
    40	            traced(q, g, read[SyncQueryResponse](s.body))
    41	
    42	            // Async: 202, QUEUED, result not finished, an idempotent replay sends no message.
    43	            val key = Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "lifecycle-1", "X-Trace-Id" -> "trace-lifecycle-1")
    44	            val r = q.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = Async), key)
    45	            assert(r.status == 202, r.body)
    46	            val a = read[AsyncQueryResponse](r.body)
    47	            assert(a.state == JobState.QUEUED && a.jobId == StableIds.jobId(a.queryId))
    48	            expectError(q.get(s"${a.statusUrl}/result"), "JOB_NOT_FINISHED", 409)
    49	            val replay = q.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = Async), key)
    50	            assert(replay.status == 202 && replay.headers.get("Idempotent-Replay").contains("true"))
    51	            assert(read[AsyncQueryResponse](replay.body).jobId == a.jobId)
    52	
    53	            // The worker takes the one message and runs the job.
    54	            assert(worker.pollOnce() == 1)
    55	            val j = read[JobResponse](q.get(a.statusUrl).body)
    56	            assert(j.state == JobState.SUCCEEDED && j.stage.contains("AUDIT"), j)
    57	            assert(j.startedAt.isDefined && j.finishedAt.isDefined && j.error.isEmpty)
    58	            assert(j.resultUrl.contains(s"${a.statusUrl}/result"))
    59	            val res = q.get(j.resultUrl.get)
    60	            assert(res.status == 200, res.body)
    61	            val answer = read[SyncQueryResponse](res.body)
    62	            assert(answer.mode == "asynchronous" && answer.queryId == a.queryId)
    63	            traced(q, g, answer)
    64	            val again = q.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = Async), key)
    65	            assert(read[AsyncQueryResponse](again.body).state == JobState.SUCCEEDED)
    66	            assert(worker.pollOnce() == 0) // no second message was sent
    67	
    68	            // The trace ID links the POST to the job; the worker's stage lines name the job.
    69	            assert(
    70	              logged.exists(l =>
    71	                l.contains("\"event\":\"job-queued\"") && l.contains("trace-lifecycle-1") &&
    72	                  l.contains(a.jobId)
    73	              )
    74	            )
    75	            assert(logged.count(l => l.contains("\"type\":\"stage\"") && l.contains(a.jobId)) == 10)
    76	          }
    77	        }
    78	      finally worker.stop()
    79	    }
    80	  }
    81	
    82	  test("local runner: three services on one backend, fake mode only") {
    83	    withWorld() { w =>
    84	      val out = scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
    85	      val lines = scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
    86	      val local = JsonLog(l => lines.synchronized(lines += l))
    87	      assert(LocalRun.start(w.args :+ "--live", w.env, out += _, local, _ => 0) == Left(2))
    88	      assert(
    89	        LocalRun.start(w.args, w.env - "SCHOLARCHAIN_API_KEY", out += _, local, _ => 0) == Left(2)
    90	      )
    91	      val svcs =
    92	        LocalRun.start(w.args, w.env, out += _, local, _ => 0).fold(c => fail(s"exit $c"), identity)
    93	      try
    94	        assert(svcs.map(_.name) == Vector("query-api", "graph-evidence-api", "worker"))
    95	        val ports = lines.collect {
    96	          case l if l.contains("\"event\":\"started\"") => ujson.read(l)("port").num.toInt
    97	        }
    98	        assert(ports.size == 3 && ports.distinct.size == 3)
    99	        ports.foreach(p => assert(Client(p).get("/healthz", Seq()).status == 200))
   100	      finally svcs.foreach(_.stop())
   101	      assert(!(out ++ lines).exists(_.contains(ApiKey)))
   102	    }
   103	  }
   104	
   105	/** Test-scope helper for the local smoke (not a test): writes the fixture store files under the
   106	  * given directory and prints the LocalRun arguments.
   107	  */
   108	object WriteFixtureStores:
   109	  def main(args: Array[String]): Unit =
   110	    val root = java.nio.file.Paths.get(args(0)).toAbsolutePath
   111	    java.nio.file.Files.createDirectories(root)
   112	    println(ServiceFixtures.writeStores(root).mkString(" "))

### modules/services/src/test/scala/scholarchain/services/QueryApiSpec.scala [CHANGED BY repair-01 Part A]
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.rag.runtime.{Audit, RuntimeApi, RuntimeSchemas}
     5	import scholarchain.storage.{Item, KeyValueReadPort, TableName}
     6	import upickle.default.{read, write}
     7	
     8	import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
     9	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger
    10	
    11	/** query-api over a real localhost socket: sync answers, validation, idempotency, deadlines and
    12	  * downstream failures, authentication, rate limits, explain, and metrics (Phase 10 rulings 5 to
    13	  * 9).
    14	  */
    15	class QueryApiSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    16	  import ServiceFixtures.*
    17	
    18	  private def errorShape(r: Reply, code: String, status: Int): Unit =
    19	    assert(r.status == status, r.body)
    20	    assert(r.error == code, r.body)
    21	    val traceId = r.body("traceId").str
    22	    assert(traceId.nonEmpty && r.headers.get("X-Trace-Id").contains(traceId))
    23	    assert(r.headers.get("X-Request-Id").contains(r.body("requestId").str))
    24	    assert(r.body("retryable").bool == scholarchain.core.ErrorCode.valueOf(code).retryable)
    25	    assert(r.body.obj.keySet == Set("error", "message", "traceId", "requestId", "retryable"))
    26	
    27	  test("a synchronous query returns an evidence-backed answer with links; IDs are echoed") {
    28	    withWorld() { w =>
    29	      w.query() { c =>
    30	        val r = c.post(
    31	          "/v1/query",
    32	          queryJson(),
    33	          Seq("X-Request-Id" -> "client-req-1", "X-Trace-Id" -> "client-trace-1")
    34	        )
    35	        assert(r.status == 200, r.body)
    36	        assert(r.headers.get("X-Request-Id").contains("client-req-1"))
    37	        assert(r.headers.get("X-Trace-Id").contains("client-trace-1"))
    38	        assert(r.headers.get("Content-Type").exists(_.startsWith("application/json")))
    39	        val a = read[SyncQueryResponse](r.body)
    40	        assert(a.mode == "synchronous" && !a.answer.abstained)
    41	        assert(a.claims.exists(_.verificationStatus.toString == "VERIFIED"), r.body)
    42	        assert(a.claims.forall(_.evidenceIds.nonEmpty))
    43	        assert(a.links.explain == s"/v1/explain/${a.queryId}")
    44	        assert(
    45	          a.links.evidence.nonEmpty && a.links.evidence.forall(_.startsWith("/v1/evidence/ev-"))
    46	        )
    47	        // An invalid client request ID is replaced.
    48	        val bad = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), Seq("X-Request-Id" -> "has space"))
    49	        assert(bad.headers("X-Request-Id").startsWith("req-"))
    50	        // The same body without a key is a new query.
    51	        assert(read[SyncQueryResponse](bad.body).queryId != a.queryId)
    52	        // One log line per request and per workflow stage, with the trace ID.
    53	        val stages =
    54	          logged.filter(l => l.contains("\"type\":\"stage\"") && l.contains("client-trace-1"))
    55	        assert(stages.size == 10, stages)
    56	        assert(
    57	          logged.exists(l =>
    58	            l.contains("\"type\":\"request\"") && l.contains("client-trace-1") &&
    59	              l.contains("\"route\":\"/v1/query\"") && l.contains("\"status\":200")
    60	          )
    61	        )
    62	      }
    63	    }
    64	  }
    65	
    66	  test("malformed JSON, invalid fields, a wrong Content-Type, and an oversized body are 400") {
    67	    withWorld("services.max-body-bytes = 2048") { w =>
    68	      w.query() { c =>
    69	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", "{\"question\": "), "MALFORMED_JSON", 400)
    70	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", "[1, 2]"), "INVALID_FIELD", 400)
    71	        // Types are checked on the JSON value (uPickle alone would read 7 or null as a String).
    72	        Vector(
    73	          "{\"question\": 7}",
    74	          "{\"question\": null}",
    75	          "{\"question\": [\"a\"]}",

    85	          queryJson(extra = ", \"output\": {\"verbose\": true}")
    86	        ).foreach(b => errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", b), "INVALID_FIELD", 400))
    87	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", "{}"), "INVALID_FIELD", 400)
    88	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson("   ")), "INVALID_FIELD", 400)
    89	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = ", \"colour\": 1")), "INVALID_FIELD", 400)
    90	        errorShape(
    91	          c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = ", \"execution\": \"later\"")),
    92	          "INVALID_FIELD",
    93	          400
    94	        )
    95	        errorShape(
    96	          c.post(
    97	            "/v1/query",
    98	            queryJson(extra = ", \"constraints\": {\"yearFrom\": 2020, \"yearTo\": 2010}")
    99	          ),
   100	          "INVALID_FIELD",
   101	          400
   102	        )

   105	          "INVALID_FIELD",
   106	          400
   107	        )
   108	        errorShape(
   109	          c.send(
   110	            "POST",
   111	            "/v1/query",
   112	            Some(queryJson()),
   113	            Seq("X-Api-Key" -> ApiKey, "Content-Type" -> "text/plain")
   114	          ),
   115	          "INVALID_FIELD",
   116	          400
   117	        )
   118	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson("q" * 3000)), "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE", 400)
   119	        errorShape(
   120	          c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "")),
   121	          "INVALID_FIELD",
   122	          400
   123	        )
   124	        errorShape(c.get("/v1/nothing"), "ROUTE_NOT_FOUND", 404)
   125	        errorShape(c.get("/v1/query"), "ROUTE_NOT_FOUND", 404)
   126	      }

   156	  test("Idempotency-Key: the same body replays the stored answer; a different body is 409") {
   157	    withWorld() { w =>
   158	      val calls = AtomicInteger()
   159	      val counting = w.backend.copy(ports = w.backend.ports.copy(kv = new KeyValueReadPort:
   160	        def get(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String): Option[Item] =
   161	          calls.incrementAndGet(); w.backend.ports.kv.get(t, pk, sk)
   162	        def query(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String, limit: Int): Vector[Item] =
   163	          calls.incrementAndGet(); w.backend.ports.kv.query(t, pk, sk, limit)))
   164	      w.query(counting) { c =>
   165	        val key = Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "order-42")
   166	        val first = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), key)
   167	        assert(first.status == 200 && first.headers.get("Idempotent-Replay").isEmpty, first.body)
   168	        val reads = calls.get
   169	        // Same key, same canonical body (other whitespace and an explicit default): the replay.
   170	        val again = c.post(
   171	          "/v1/query",
   172	          s"""{ "execution":"sync",  "question" : ${ujson.write(Question)} }""",
   173	          key
   174	        )
   175	        assert(again.status == 200 && again.headers.get("Idempotent-Replay").contains("true"))
   176	        assert(again.body == first.body)
   177	        assert(calls.get == reads, "the replay ran the workflow again")
   178	        errorShape(
   179	          c.post("/v1/query", queryJson("Who is Grace Hopper?"), key),
   180	          "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
   181	          409
   182	        )
   183	        // A claimed key whose query has no stored answer (still running or failed) is 409 too.
   184	        val unknown = scholarchain.core.StableIds.queryId(w.backend.setup.runId, "x", "y", 1)
   185	        val hash = QueryValidation.requestHash(QueryBody("Who?"))
   186	        assert(w.backend.idempotency.claim("pending", hash, unknown).isRight)
   187	        errorShape(
   188	          c.post("/v1/query", queryJson("Who?"), Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "pending")),
   189	          "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
   190	          409
   191	        )
   192	      }
   193	    }
   194	  }

   196	  test("a passed deadline is 504 (cooperative and hard); a failing store is 502; breakers open") {
   197	    withWorld(
   198	      "operations.synchronous-deadline-ms = 300\nservices.resilience.breaker-failure-threshold = 2"
   199	    ) { w =>
   200	      def kv(body: => Unit) = w.backend.copy(ports = w.backend.ports.copy(kv = new KeyValueReadPort:
   201	        def get(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String): Option[Item] =
   202	          body; w.backend.ports.kv.get(t, pk, sk)
   203	        def query(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String, limit: Int): Vector[Item] =
   204	          body; w.backend.ports.kv.query(t, pk, sk, limit)))
   205	      // Every read sleeps 120 ms: the runtime's own deadline check ends it (cooperative).
   206	      w.query(kv(Thread.sleep(120))) { c =>
   207	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson()), "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED", 504)
   208	      }
   209	      // One read hangs past the deadline: the service's hard timeout answers at the deadline.
   210	      w.query(kv(Thread.sleep(5000))) { c =>
   211	        val t0 = System.nanoTime()
   212	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson()), "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED", 504)
   213	        assert((System.nanoTime() - t0) / 1000000 < 3000)
   214	        // A deadline is no downstream failure: the breaker stays closed.
   215	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson()), "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED", 504)
   216	      }
   217	      val m = Metrics()
   218	      w.query(kv(throw IllegalStateException("store down: secret-endpoint.internal")), m) { c =>
   219	        val r = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson())
   220	        errorShape(r, "DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE", 502)
   221	        assert(!r.body.render().contains("secret-endpoint"))
   222	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson()), "DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE", 502)
   223	        // Two consecutive failures open the runtime breaker: fail fast, and not ready.
   224	        errorShape(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson()), "CIRCUIT_OPEN", 503)
   225	        errorShape(c.get("/readyz", Seq()), "NOT_READY", 503)
   226	        assert(c.get("/healthz", Seq()).status == 200)
   227	        assert(m.snapshot("counters")("circuitOpened.runtime").num == 1)
   228	        assert(m.snapshot("counters")("timeouts").num == 0)
   229	      }
   230	    }
   231	  }
   232

   413	  test(
   414	    "launcher: fake mode starts on localhost; a missing key or incomplete live arguments stop it"
   415	  ) {
   416	    withWorld() { w =>
   417	      val out = scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
   418	      val lines = scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
   419	      val local = JsonLog(l => lines.synchronized(lines += l))

   421	        Launcher.start(args, Apps.queryApi(_, _, _, _), _ => 0, env, out += _, local)
   422	      assert(start(w.args, w.env - "SCHOLARCHAIN_API_KEY") == Left(2))
   423	      assert(out.exists(_.contains("set the environment variable SCHOLARCHAIN_API_KEY")))
   424	      assert(start(w.args, w.env + ("SCHOLARCHAIN_API_KEY" -> "")) == Left(2))
   425	      assert(start(Seq("--live", "--profile", "p"), w.env) == Left(1))
   426	      assert(out.exists(_.contains("missing --snapshot")))
   427	      assert(start(w.args :+ "--bogus", w.env) == Left(2))
   428	      val svc = start(w.args, w.env).fold(c => fail(s"exit $c: ${out.mkString("\n")}"), identity)
   429	      try
   430	        val started = ujson.read(lines.find(_.contains("\"event\":\"started\"")).get)
   431	        val c = Client(started("port").num.toInt)
   432	        assert(c.get("/healthz", Seq()).status == 200)
   433	        assert(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson()).status == 200)
   434	      finally svc.stop()
   435	      assert(!(out ++ lines).exists(_.contains(ApiKey)))
   436	    }

   439	  test("readiness probes S3 under the audit prefix query-api and the worker read (repair-01 m1)") {
   440	    withWorld() { w =>
   441	      val keys = java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue[String]()
   442	      val orig = w.backend.ports.auditReader
   443	      val recording = w.backend.copy(ports =
   444	        w.backend.ports.copy(auditReader = new scholarchain.storage.ObjectReadPort:
   445	          def get(key: String): Option[Array[Byte]] =
   446	            keys.add(key); orig.get(key)
   447	          def list(prefix: String): Vector[String] = orig.list(prefix))
   448	      )
   449	      val probe =
   450	        s"${Audit.base("logs", w.backend.setup.snapshot, w.backend.setup.runId)}/readiness-probe"
   451	      w.query(recording) { c => assert(c.get("/readyz", Seq()).status == 200) }
   452	      assert(keys.toArray.toVector == Vector(probe))
   453	      keys.clear()
   454	      val wk = Worker(w.cfg, recording, log, Metrics())
   455	      try wk.readiness.foreach(_._2())
   456	      finally wk.ds.shutdown()
   457	      assert(keys.toArray.toVector == Vector(probe))
   458	    }

### modules/services/src/test/scala/scholarchain/services/SaturationSpec.scala [CHANGED BY repair-01 Part A]
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.JobState
     5	import scholarchain.storage.*
     6	import upickle.default.read
     7	
     8	import java.util.concurrent.{CountDownLatch, Executors, TimeUnit}
     9	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.{AtomicBoolean, AtomicInteger}
    10	
    11	/** Repair-01 F1: long workflows hold only the workflow slots. Store calls and readiness probes keep
    12	  * their own threads, answer within their timeouts, and open no breaker against a healthy store; a
    13	  * workflow that cannot start answers 503 OVERLOADED.
    14	  */
    15	class SaturationSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    16	  import ServiceFixtures.*
    17	
    18	  /** b whose store reads block while hold is set, ignoring interrupts, as an overrunning model call
    19	    * does; audits counts the object writes after hold was set (an audit and an answer per
    20	    * workflow).
    21	    */
    22	  private final class Blocked(b: Backend):
    23	    val hold = AtomicBoolean(false)
    24	    val release = CountDownLatch(1)
    25	    val audits = AtomicInteger()
    26	    val blocked = AtomicInteger()
    27	    private def block(): Unit =
    28	      if hold.get then
    29	        blocked.incrementAndGet()
    30	        val until = System.nanoTime() + 20000000000L
    31	        while release.getCount > 0 && System.nanoTime() < until do
    32	          try release.await(100, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
    33	          catch case _: InterruptedException => ()
    34	    val backend: Backend = b.copy(ports =
    35	      b.ports.copy(
    36	        kv = new KeyValueReadPort:
    37	          def get(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String): Option[Item] =
    38	            block(); b.ports.kv.get(t, pk, sk)
    39	          def query(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String, limit: Int): Vector[Item] =
    40	            block(); b.ports.kv.query(t, pk, sk, limit)
    41	        ,
    42	        auditWriter = new ObjectLoadPort:
    43	          def putNew(key: String, bytes: Array[Byte]) =
    44	            try b.ports.auditWriter.putNew(key, bytes)
    45	            finally if hold.get then audits.incrementAndGet()
    46	      )
    47	    )
    48	
    49	    /** Lets the blocked workflows end and waits (bounded) until they wrote their audits and
    50	      * answers, so no write lands in the test directory while it is deleted.
    51	      */
    52	    def drain(started: Int): Unit =
    53	      release.countDown()
    54	      val until = System.nanoTime() + 10000000000L
    55	      while audits.get < 2 * started && System.nanoTime() < until do Thread.sleep(50)
    56	      Thread.sleep(500)
    57	
    58	  private def ms(t0: Long) = (System.nanoTime() - t0) / 1000000
    59	  private def timed[A](body: => A): (A, Long) =
    60	    val t0 = System.nanoTime()
    61	    val a = body
    62	    (a, ms(t0))
    63	  private def noBreakerOpened(m: Metrics): Boolean =
    64	    !m.snapshot("counters").obj.keys.exists(_.startsWith("circuitOpened"))
    65	
    66	  private val Limits = Seq(
    67	    "services.query-api.threads = 4", // 4 HTTP and store threads, 2 workflow slots
    68	    "operations.synchronous-deadline-ms = 300",
    69	    "services.timeouts.dynamodb-ms = 1000",
    70	    "services.timeouts.s3-ms = 1000",
    71	    "services.resilience.breaker-failure-threshold = 1"
    72	  ).mkString("\n")
    73	
    74	  test("query-api: with every workflow slot held, jobs, explain, and readiness still answer") {
    75	    withWorld(Limits) { w =>
    76	      val x = Blocked(w.backend)
    77	      val m = Metrics()
    78	      w.query(x.backend, m) { c =>
    79	        val answered = read[SyncQueryResponse](c.post("/v1/query", queryJson()).body)
    80	        val (_, jobId) = submit(x.backend)
    81	        x.hold.set(true)
    82	        val pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(4)
    83	        try
    84	          // 4 POSTs at once: 2 take the workflow slots and overrun their deadline; 2 cannot start.
    85	          val posts = Vector
    86	            .fill(4)(pool.submit(() => c.post("/v1/query", queryJson())))
    87	            .map(_.get(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
    88	          // The fast routes keep their threads: answered within their store timeouts (1000 ms).
    89	          (1 to 3).foreach { _ =>
    90	            val (j, jMs) = timed(c.get(s"/v1/jobs/$jobId"))
    91	            assert(j.status == 200 && read[JobResponse](j.body).state == JobState.QUEUED, j.body)
    92	            assert(jMs < 1000, s"job read took $jMs ms")
    93	            val (e, eMs) = timed(c.get(s"/v1/explain/${answered.queryId}"))
    94	            assert(e.status == 200 && eMs < 1000, s"${e.body} after $eMs ms")
    95	            val (r, rMs) = timed(c.get("/readyz", Seq()))
    96	            assert(r.status == 200 && r.body == ujson.Obj("status" -> "ready"), r.body)
    97	            assert(rMs < 3000, s"readiness took $rMs ms")
    98	          }
    99	          assert(noBreakerOpened(m), m.snapshot("counters"))
   100	          val codes = posts.map(r => (r.status, r.error)).sorted
   101	          assert(
   102	            codes == Vector(
   103	              (503, "OVERLOADED"),
   104	              (503, "OVERLOADED"),
   105	              (504, "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED"),
   106	              (504, "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED")
   107	            ),
   108	            codes
   109	          )
   110	          posts.filter(_.status == 503).foreach(expectError(_, "OVERLOADED", 503))
   111	          // A workflow that cannot start: 503 OVERLOADED at once, and still no breaker.
   112	          val (o, oMs) = timed(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson()))
   113	          expectError(o, "OVERLOADED", 503)
   114	          assert(oMs < 1000 && noBreakerOpened(m))
   115	          assert(c.get("/readyz", Seq()).status == 200)
   116	        finally
   117	          x.drain(2)
   118	          pool.shutdownNow()
   119	      }
   120	    }
   121	  }
   122	
   123	  test("worker: an overrun job holds only its workflow slot; no job is claimed until it frees") {
   124	    withWorld(
   125	      Limits + "\nservices.worker.poll-wait-seconds = 1\nruntime.question.deadline-ms = 200" +
   126	        "\nruntime.llm.timeout-ms = 200"
   127	    ) { w =>
   128	      val clock = Clock()
   129	      val (b0, _) = withQueue(w.backend, w.cfg, clock)
   130	      val x = Blocked(b0)
   131	      val b = x.backend
   132	      val m = Metrics()
   133	      val wk = Worker(w.cfg, b, log, m)
   134	      try
   135	        x.hold.set(true)
   136	        val (_, first) = submit(b)
   137	        // Its workflow overruns the backstop (deadline + model timeout) and keeps its slot.
   138	        assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1)
   139	        assert(jobOf(b, first).state == JobState.FAILED)
   140	        val later = Vector.fill(2)(submit(b)._2)
   141	        later.foreach(_ => assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1))
   142	        // Store calls and readiness probes keep their threads and their timeouts; no breaker opens.
   143	        wk.readiness.foreach { (name, probe) =>
   144	          val (outcome, took) = timed(scala.util.Try(probe()))
   145	          assert(outcome.isSuccess && took < 1000, s"$name probe: $outcome after $took ms")
   146	        }
   147	        assert(wk.ds.openBreakers.isEmpty && noBreakerOpened(m), m.snapshot("counters"))
   148	        // Later jobs are not claimed while the slot is held: they stay QUEUED, messages kept.
   149	        later.foreach(j => assert(jobOf(b, j).state == JobState.QUEUED))
   150	        assert(x.blocked.get == 1, "a later job ran")
   151	        assert(!wk.ds.workflowSlotFree)
   152	        // The poll loop does not receive while no workflow slot is free.
   153	        clock.advance(w.cfg.services.worker.visibilityTimeoutSeconds.toLong + 1)
   154	        val receives = m.snapshot("counters")("queueReceives").num
   155	        wk.start()
   156	        Thread.sleep(1500)
   157	        assert(m.snapshot("counters")("queueReceives").num == receives)
   158	        later.foreach(j => assert(jobOf(b, j).state == JobState.QUEUED))
   159	      finally
   160	        x.drain(1)
   161	        wk.stop()
   162	    }
   163	  }
```

   306	  /** Every log line any test service wrote (the leak test scans them). */
   307	  val logLines: mutable.ArrayBuffer[String] = mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty
   308	  val log: JsonLog = JsonLog(l => logLines.synchronized(logLines += l))
   309	  def logged: Vector[String] = logLines.synchronized(logLines.toVector)
   310	
   311	  /** Every response body and header value any test client received. */
   312	  val seen: mutable.ArrayBuffer[String] = mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty
   313	
   314	  final case class Reply(status: Int, body: ujson.Value, headers: Map[String, String]):
   315	    def error: String = body("error").str
   316	
   317	  private val client = HttpClient.newBuilder().version(HttpClient.Version.HTTP_1_1).build()
   318	
   319	  final class Client(port: Int):
   320	    def send(
   321	        method: String,
   322	        path: String,
   323	        body: Option[String] = None,
   324	        headers: Seq[(String, String)] =
   325	          Seq("X-Api-Key" -> ApiKey, "Content-Type" -> "application/json")
   326	    ): Reply =
   327	      val b = HttpRequest
   328	        .newBuilder(URI.create(s"http://127.0.0.1:$port$path"))
   329	        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(30))
   330	        .method(
   331	          method,
   332	          body.fold(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.noBody())(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString)
   333	        )
   334	      headers.foreach((k, v) => b.header(k, v))
   335	      val r = client.send(b.build(), HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString())
   336	      val hs = Map.from(
   337	        Seq("X-Request-Id", "X-Trace-Id", "Idempotent-Replay", "Retry-After", "Content-Type")
   338	          .flatMap(h => Option(r.headers.firstValue(h).orElse(null)).map(h -> _))
   339	      )
   340	      seen.synchronized(seen ++= (r.body +: hs.values.toSeq))
   341	      Reply(r.statusCode, if r.body.isEmpty then ujson.Null else ujson.read(r.body), hs)
   342	    def get(path: String, headers: Seq[(String, String)] = Seq("X-Api-Key" -> ApiKey)): Reply =
   343	      send("GET", path, None, headers)
   344	    def post(path: String, body: String, headers: Seq[(String, String)] = Seq()): Reply =
   345	      send(
   346	        "POST",
   347	        path,
   348	        Some(body),
   349	        Seq("X-Api-Key" -> ApiKey, "Content-Type" -> "application/json") ++ headers
   350	      )
   351	
   352	  /** The store files, the configuration (HOCON overrides under scholarchain), and a backend from
   353	    * the fake composition root.
   354	    */
   355	  final class World(val root: Path, hocon: String, summaries: Boolean = false):
   356	    val env: Map[String, String] =
   357	      if hocon.isEmpty then baseEnv
   358	      else
   359	        val f = root.resolve("test.conf")
   360	        Files.writeString(f, s"scholarchain { $hocon }")
   361	        baseEnv + ("SCHOLARCHAIN_CONFIG_FILE" -> f.toString)
   362	    val cfg =
   363	      scholarchain.core.config.ConfigLoader.load(env).fold(e => sys.error(e.mkString), identity)
   364	    // summaries: the fixture window-summary object under its own root, as the smoke passes it.
   365	    val args: Vector[String] = writeStores(root) ++ (
   366	      if summaries then
   367	        writeSummaries(
   368	          root.resolve("window-summary"),
   369	          WindowSummaries.render(summaryRows(cfg.graph.windows))
   370	        )
   371	      else Vector()
   372	    )
   373	    val backend: Backend = Launcher
   374	      .backend(
   375	        scholarchain.storage.cli.Cli
   376	          .parse(args, Set("live"), args.collect { case s"--$o" => o }.toSet)
   377	          .toOption
   378	          .get,
   379	        cfg,
   380	        _ => ()
   381	      )
   382	      .fold(sys.error, identity)
   383	    val auth = ApiKeyAuth(ApiKey)
   384	
   385	    /** Starts a service on 127.0.0.1:0, runs body with a client, and always stops it. */
   386	    def serve[A](svc: HttpService)(body: Client => A): A =
   387	      val port = svc.start("127.0.0.1", 0)
   388	      try body(Client(port))
   389	      finally svc.stop()
   390	    def query[A](b: Backend = backend, metrics: Metrics = Metrics())(body: Client => A): A =
   391	      serve(Apps.queryApi(cfg, b, auth, log, metrics))(body)
   392	    def graphEvidence[A](b: Backend = backend)(body: Client => A): A =
   393	      serve(Apps.graphEvidenceApi(cfg, b, auth, log))(body)
   394	
   395	  def withWorld[A](hocon: String = "", summaries: Boolean = false)(body: World => A): A =
   396	    val root = Files.createTempDirectory("services-test-")
   397	    try body(World(root, hocon, summaries))
   398	    finally deleteTree(root)
   399	
   400	  val Question = "Have Ada Lovelace and Grace Hopper coauthored a paper?"
   401	  val Async = ", \"execution\": \"async\""
   402	
   403	  /** An error reply in the spec error model, with the IDs echoed in the headers. */
   404	  def expectError(r: Reply, code: String, status: Int): Unit =
   405	    import org.scalatest.Assertions.*
   406	    assert(r.status == status, r.body)
   407	    assert(r.error == code, r.body)
   408	    assert(r.headers.get("X-Trace-Id").contains(r.body("traceId").str))
   409	    assert(r.headers.get("X-Request-Id").contains(r.body("requestId").str))
   410	    assert(r.body("retryable").bool == scholarchain.core.ErrorCode.valueOf(code).retryable)
   411	    assert(r.body.obj.keySet == Set("error", "message", "traceId", "requestId", "retryable"))
   412	
   413	  /** A settable clock for the fake queue's visibility timeouts. */
   414	  final class Clock:
   415	    @volatile var now: Instant = Instant.now()
   416	    def advance(seconds: Long): Unit = now = now.plusSeconds(seconds)
   417	
   418	  /** b with a fresh in-memory queue on clock (max receive count from cfg). */
   419	  def withQueue(
   420	      b: Backend,
   421	      cfg: scholarchain.core.config.ScholarChainConfig,
   422	      clock: Clock
   423	  ): (Backend, InMemoryJobQueue) =
   424	    val q = InMemoryJobQueue(cfg.services.queue.maxReceiveCount, () => clock.now)
   425	    (b.copy(queue = q), q)
   426	
   427	  /** A QUEUED job and its message, as query-api's async path writes them; (queryId, jobId). */
   428	  def submit(
   429	      b: Backend,
   430	      question: String = Question,
   431	      request: Option[String] = None
   432	  ): (String, String) =
   433	    val queryId = StableIds.queryId(b.setup.runId, StableIds.textHash(question), Ids.fresh("t"), 1)
   434	    val body = request.getOrElse(upickle.default.write(QueryBody(question)))
   435	    assert(b.jobs.create(queryId, body).isRight)
   436	    b.queue.send(JobMessage.of(queryId))
   437	    (queryId, StableIds.jobId(queryId))
   438	
   439	  def jobOf(b: Backend, jobId: String): JobRecord = b.jobs.get(jobId).toOption.flatten.get
   440	  def queryJson(question: String = Question, extra: String = ""): String =
   441	    s"""{"question": ${ujson.write(ujson.Str(question))}$extra}"""
```

### modules/services/src/test/scala/scholarchain/services/ServicesContractSpec.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.ErrorCode
     5	import upickle.default.{Reader, Writer, read, writeJs}
     6	
     7	import java.nio.file.{Files, Paths}
     8	import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*
     9	
    10	/** openapi.yaml against the code: every example parses against its model (responses round-trip
    11	  * exactly, so no field is missing or extra), every example file is referenced, and the paths and
    12	  * methods equal the route tables of the three services.
    13	  */
    14	class ServicesContractSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    15	  import ServiceFixtures.*
    16	
    17	  private val root = Paths.get("modules/services")
    18	  private val spec = Files.readString(root.resolve("openapi.yaml"))
    19	  private val examples = root.resolve("openapi/examples")
    20	
    21	  private def roundTrip[A: Reader: Writer](v: ujson.Value): Unit =
    22	    assert(writeJs(read[A](v)) == v)
    23	
    24	  test("every example is referenced, exists, and parses against its model") {
    25	    val refs = "externalValue: (\\S+)".r.findAllMatchIn(spec).map(_.group(1)).toSet
    26	    val files =
    27	      Files.list(examples).iterator.asScala.map(p => s"openapi/examples/${p.getFileName}").toSet
    28	    assert(refs == files)
    29	    withWorld() { w =>
    30	      files.toVector.sorted.foreach { f =>
    31	        val name = f.stripPrefix("openapi/examples/").stripSuffix(".json")
    32	        val v = ujson.read(Files.readString(root.resolve(f)))
    33	        withClue(name) {
    34	          name match
    35	            case s"query-request$_" =>
    36	              val (body, _) = QueryValidation.parse(v, w.cfg)
    37	              assert(body.question.nonEmpty)
    38	            case "query-response-sync" | "job-result-response" => roundTrip[SyncQueryResponse](v)
    39	            case "query-response-async"                        => roundTrip[AsyncQueryResponse](v)
    40	            case s"job-response$_"                             => roundTrip[JobResponse](v)
    41	            case "explain-response"                            => roundTrip[ExplainResponse](v)
    42	            case "evidence-response"                           => roundTrip[EvidenceResponse](v)
    43	            case "node-response"                               => roundTrip[NodeResponse](v)
    44	            case "neighbors-response"                          => roundTrip[NeighborsResponse](v)
    45	            case s"status-$_"                                  => roundTrip[Status](v)
    46	            case "metrics-response"                            =>
    47	              assert(v.obj.keySet == Set("requests", "latencyMs", "counters"))
    48	            case s"error-$code" =>
    49	              roundTrip[ErrorBody](v)
    50	              val e = read[ErrorBody](v)
    51	              assert(e.error == ErrorCode.valueOf(code.toUpperCase.replace('-', '_')))
    52	              assert(e.retryable == e.error.retryable)
    53	            case other => fail(s"no model for example $other")
    54	        }
    55	      }
    56	    }
    57	  }
    58	
    59	  test(
    60	    "the documented paths and methods are exactly the routes of query-api, graph-evidence-api, and worker"
    61	  ) {
    62	    val documented = Vector.newBuilder[(String, String)]
    63	    var path = ""
    64	    spec.linesIterator.dropWhile(_ != "paths:").takeWhile(_ != "components:").foreach {
    65	      case s"  /$p:"                                            => path = s"/$p"
    66	      case s"    $m:" if Set("get", "post", "put", "delete")(m) =>
    67	        documented += m.toUpperCase -> path
    68	      case _ => ()
    69	    }
    70	    withWorld() { w =>
    71	      val services = Seq(
    72	        Apps.queryApi(w.cfg, w.backend, w.auth, log),
    73	        Apps.graphEvidenceApi(w.cfg, w.backend, w.auth, log),
    74	        Apps.worker(w.cfg, w.backend, w.auth, log)
    75	      )
    76	      try assert(documented.result().toSet == services.flatMap(_.routeTable).toSet)
    77	      finally services.foreach(_.stop())
    78	    }
    79	  }
```

### modules/services/src/test/scala/scholarchain/services/WorkerSpec.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.services
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.{ErrorCode, JobState, StableIds}
     5	import scholarchain.rag.runtime.Audit
     6	import scholarchain.storage.*
     7	import upickle.default.read
     8	
     9	import java.time.Instant
    10	import java.util.concurrent.{Callable, ConcurrentLinkedQueue, Executors, TimeUnit}
    11	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger
    12	import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*
    13	
    14	/** The async worker (Phase 10 ruling 7): one run per job however often its message arrives, delete
    15	  * only after a final state, the stuck-RUNNING rule, poison messages and redrive, typed failures,
    16	  * the async POST and jobs routes, and the worker service over HTTP.
    17	  */
    18	class WorkerSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    19	  import ServiceFixtures.*
    20	
    21	  private val Fast = "services.worker.poll-wait-seconds = 1"
    22	
    23	  /** b whose audit writer records keys; the count of audit writes of a query (one per run). */
    24	  private def countingAudits(b: Backend): (Backend, String => Int) =
    25	    val keys = ConcurrentLinkedQueue[String]()
    26	    val orig = b.ports.auditWriter
    27	    val port = new ObjectLoadPort:
    28	      def putNew(key: String, bytes: Array[Byte]) =
    29	        keys.add(key)
    30	        orig.putNew(key, bytes)
    31	    (
    32	      b.copy(ports = b.ports.copy(auditWriter = port)),
    33	      q => keys.asScala.count(_ == Audit.auditKey(b.setup.snapshot, b.setup.runId, q))
    34	    )
    35	
    36	  private def kv(b: Backend)(body: => Unit): Backend =
    37	    b.copy(ports = b.ports.copy(kv = new KeyValueReadPort:
    38	      def get(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String): Option[Item] =
    39	        body; b.ports.kv.get(t, pk, sk)
    40	      def query(t: TableName, pk: String, sk: String, limit: Int): Vector[Item] =
    41	        body; b.ports.kv.query(t, pk, sk, limit)))
    42	
    43	  private def counter(m: Metrics, name: String) = m.snapshot("counters")(name).num.toLong
    44	
    45	  test("a redelivered or duplicated message never runs a job twice") {
    46	    withWorld(Fast) { w =>
    47	      val clock = Clock()
    48	      val (b0, q) = withQueue(w.backend, w.cfg, clock)
    49	      val (b, audits) = countingAudits(b0)
    50	      val m = Metrics()
    51	      val wk = Worker(w.cfg, b, log, m)
    52	      try
    53	        val (qid, jid) = submit(b)
    54	        b.queue.send(JobMessage.of(qid)) // SQS standard queues may deliver a message twice
    55	        assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1)
    56	        val done = jobOf(b, jid)
    57	        assert(done.state == JobState.SUCCEEDED, done)
    58	        assert(
    59	          done.stage.contains("AUDIT") && done.startedAt.isDefined && done.finishedAt.isDefined
    60	        )
    61	        assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1)
    62	        assert(jobOf(b, jid) == done, "the duplicate changed the job")
    63	        assert(audits(qid) == 1 && q.size == 0)
    64	        assert(counter(m, "duplicateDeliveries") == 1 && counter(m, "jobsSucceeded") == 1)
    65	
    66	        // Four copies taken by four workers at once: exactly one claim wins and runs.
    67	        val (qid2, jid2) = submit(b)
    68	        (1 to 3).foreach(_ => b.queue.send(JobMessage.of(qid2)))
    69	        val workers = Vector.fill(4)(Worker(w.cfg, b, log, Metrics()))
    70	        val pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(4)
    71	        try
    72	          val fs = workers.map(x => pool.submit(new Callable[Int] { def call() = x.pollOnce() }))
    73	          assert(fs.map(_.get(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)).sum == 4)
    74	        finally
    75	          pool.shutdown()
    76	          workers.foreach(_.stop())
    77	        assert(jobOf(b, jid2).state == JobState.SUCCEEDED && audits(qid2) == 1)
    78	        // Copies kept while the job was RUNNING come back and are deleted as duplicates.
    79	        clock.advance(301)
    80	        (1 to 4).foreach(_ => if q.size > 0 then wk.pollOnce())
    81	        assert(q.size == 0 && q.deadLetters.isEmpty && audits(qid2) == 1)
    82	      finally wk.stop()
    83	    }
    84	  }
    85	
    86	  test(
    87	    "a message is deleted only after a final state; a stuck RUNNING job fails at its last delivery"
    88	  ) {
    89	    withWorld(Fast) { w =>
    90	      val clock = Clock()
    91	      val (b1, q) = withQueue(w.backend, w.cfg, clock)
    92	      @volatile var finalWritesFail = true
    93	      val table = LocalServiceStateTable(None, () => Instant.now)
    94	      val failing = new ServiceStateTable:
    95	        def get(recordKey: String, recordType: String) = table.get(recordKey, recordType)
    96	        def putIfAbsent(item: Item) = table.putIfAbsent(item)
    97	        def putIfState(item: Item, expected: JobState) =
    98	          if finalWritesFail && ServiceStateContract.job(item).exists(_.state.isFinal) then
    99	            throw IllegalStateException("store down")
   100	          table.putIfState(item, expected)
   101	      val (b, audits) = countingAudits(
   102	        b1.copy(jobs = JobStore(failing, w.cfg.services.ttl.jobHours, () => Instant.now))
   103	      )
   104	      val m = Metrics()
   105	      val wk = Worker(w.cfg, b, log, m)
   106	      try
   107	        val (qid, jid) = submit(b)
   108	        // Delivery 1 runs the job, but its final state cannot be written: the message is kept.
   109	        assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1)
   110	        assert(jobOf(b, jid).state == JobState.RUNNING && q.size == 1 && audits(qid) == 1)
   111	        finalWritesFail = false
   112	        // Delivery 2: still RUNNING and not the last delivery, so left alone and not run again.
   113	        clock.advance(301)
   114	        assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1)
   115	        assert(jobOf(b, jid).state == JobState.RUNNING && q.size == 1 && audits(qid) == 1)
   116	        // Delivery 3 = max-receive-count: RUNNING -> FAILED, then the message is deleted.
   117	        clock.advance(301)
   118	        assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1)
   119	        val j = jobOf(b, jid)
   120	        assert(
   121	          j.state == JobState.FAILED && j.error.map(_.code).contains(ErrorCode.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED)
   122	        )
   123	        assert(q.size == 0 && q.deadLetters.isEmpty && audits(qid) == 1)
   124	        assert(counter(m, "stuckJobsFailed") == 1)
   125	        w.query(b) { c =>
   126	          expectError(c.get(s"/v1/jobs/$jid/result"), "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED", 504)
   127	          val v = read[JobResponse](c.get(s"/v1/jobs/$jid").body)
   128	          assert(v.state == JobState.FAILED && v.error.exists(_.retryable) && v.resultUrl.isEmpty)
   129	        }
   130	      finally wk.stop()
   131	    }
   132	  }
   133	
   134	  test("poison messages are never run or deleted; redrive moves them to the dead-letter queue") {
   135	    withWorld(Fast) { w =>
   136	      val clock = Clock()
   137	      val (b0, q) = withQueue(w.backend, w.cfg, clock)
   138	      val reads = AtomicInteger()
   139	      val b = kv(b0)(reads.incrementAndGet())
   140	      val m = Metrics()
   141	      val wk = Worker(w.cfg, b, log, m)
   142	      try
   143	        q.sendRaw("{\"not\": \"a job\"}")
   144	        q.send(JobMessage.of(StableIds.queryId(b.setup.runId, "no", "job", 1))) // no job record
   145	        (1 to w.cfg.services.queue.maxReceiveCount).foreach { _ =>
   146	          assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1 && wk.pollOnce() == 1)
   147	          assert(q.size == 2)
   148	          clock.advance(301)
   149	        }
   150	        assert(wk.pollOnce() == 0) // the next receive attempt redrives both
   151	        assert(q.deadLetters.size == 2 && q.size == 0 && reads.get == 0)
   152	        assert(counter(m, "poisonMessages") == 6 && counter(m, "queueReceives") == 6)
   153	      finally wk.stop()
   154	    }
   155	  }
   156	
   157	  test(
   158	    "a failing, late, or undecodable job ends FAILED with its typed error; its message is deleted"
   159	  ) {
   160	    withWorld(s"$Fast\nruntime.question.deadline-ms = 300") { w =>
   161	      val clock = Clock()
   162	      def runOne(b0: Backend, request: Option[String] = None): JobRecord =
   163	        val (b, q) = withQueue(b0, w.cfg, clock)
   164	        val wk = Worker(w.cfg, b, log, Metrics())
   165	        try
   166	          val (_, jid) = submit(b, request = request)
   167	          assert(wk.pollOnce() == 1)
   168	          assert(q.size == 0)
   169	          jobOf(b, jid)
   170	        finally wk.stop()
   171	      val failed = runOne(kv(w.backend)(throw IllegalStateException("down: secret-endpoint")))
   172	      assert(failed.state == JobState.FAILED)
   173	      assert(failed.error.map(_.code).contains(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE), failed)
   174	      assert(!failed.error.exists(_.message.contains("secret-endpoint")))
   175	      val late = runOne(kv(w.backend)(Thread.sleep(120)))
   176	      assert(late.error.map(_.code).contains(ErrorCode.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED), late)
   177	      val bad = runOne(w.backend, Some("{}"))
   178	      assert(bad.error.map(_.code).contains(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR) && bad.startedAt.isEmpty)
   179	      w.query() { c =>
   180	        expectError(c.get(s"/v1/jobs/${failed.jobId}/result"), "DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE", 502)
   181	        expectError(c.get(s"/v1/jobs/${late.jobId}/result"), "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED", 504)
   182	        expectError(c.get(s"/v1/jobs/${bad.jobId}/result"), "INTERNAL_ERROR", 500)
   183	      }
   184	    }
   185	  }
   186	
   187	  test("async POST: 202 job reference, job IDs checked first, a send failure fails the job") {
   188	    withWorld(Fast) { w =>
   189	      w.query() { c =>
   190	        val r = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = Async))
   191	        assert(r.status == 202, r.body)
   192	        val a = read[AsyncQueryResponse](r.body)
   193	        assert(a.jobId == StableIds.jobId(a.queryId) && a.state == JobState.QUEUED)
   194	        assert(a.mode == "asynchronous" && a.statusUrl == s"/v1/jobs/${a.jobId}")
   195	        assert(
   196	          w.backend.queue.receive(10, 0, 0).map(_.message) == Vector(
   197	            Right(JobMessage.of(a.queryId))
   198	          )
   199	        )
   200	        val j = read[JobResponse](c.get(a.statusUrl).body)
   201	        assert(j.state == JobState.QUEUED && j.resultUrl.isEmpty && j.error.isEmpty)
   202	        expectError(c.get(s"${a.statusUrl}/result"), "JOB_NOT_FINISHED", 409)
   203	        expectError(c.get("/v1/jobs/job-" + "0" * 32), "JOB_NOT_FOUND", 404)
   204	        expectError(c.get("/v1/jobs/..%2Fetc/result"), "JOB_NOT_FOUND", 404)
   205	        expectError(c.get(s"${a.statusUrl}?verbose=1"), "INVALID_FIELD", 400)
   206	        expectError(c.get(a.statusUrl, Seq()), "UNAUTHORIZED", 401)
   207	        expectError(c.get(s"${a.statusUrl}/result", Seq()), "UNAUTHORIZED", 401)
   208	      }
   209	      // The queue refuses the message: the job does not stay QUEUED; a replay shows it FAILED.
   210	      val down = w.backend.copy(queue = new JobQueuePort:
   211	        def send(m: JobMessage) = throw IllegalStateException("sqs down")
   212	        def receive(n: Int, s: Int, v: Int) = Vector.empty
   213	        def delete(h: String) = false
   214	        def changeVisibility(h: String, s: Int) = false)
   215	      w.query(down) { c =>
   216	        val key = Seq("Idempotency-Key" -> "async-down-1")
   217	        expectError(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = Async), key), "DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE", 502)
   218	        val qid = w.backend.idempotency.get("async-down-1").toOption.flatten.get.queryId
   219	        val j = jobOf(w.backend, StableIds.jobId(qid))
   220	        assert(
   221	          j.state == JobState.FAILED && j.error.map(_.code).contains(ErrorCode.DOWNSTREAM_FAILURE)
   222	        )
   223	        val again = c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = Async), key)
   224	        assert(again.status == 202 && again.headers.get("Idempotent-Replay").contains("true"))
   225	        assert(read[AsyncQueryResponse](again.body).state == JobState.FAILED)
   226	        // The same key with the sync body is another request: 409.
   227	        expectError(c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(), key), "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT", 409)
   228	      }
   229	    }
   230	  }
   231	
   232	  test("worker service: liveness, readiness, key-protected metrics, and the poll loop") {
   233	    withWorld(Fast) { w =>
   234	      val m = Metrics()
   235	      w.query() { c =>
   236	        val a = read[AsyncQueryResponse](c.post("/v1/query", queryJson(extra = Async)).body)
   237	        w.serve(Apps.worker(w.cfg, w.backend, w.auth, log, m)) { wc =>
   238	          assert(wc.get("/healthz", Seq()).body == ujson.Obj("status" -> "ok"))
   239	          assert(wc.get("/readyz", Seq()).body == ujson.Obj("status" -> "ready"))
   240	          expectError(wc.get("/metrics", Seq()), "UNAUTHORIZED", 401)
   241	          expectError(wc.get("/v1/query"), "ROUTE_NOT_FOUND", 404)
   242	          val until = System.nanoTime() + 20000000000L
   243	          while read[JobResponse](c.get(a.statusUrl).body).state != JobState.SUCCEEDED &&
   244	            System.nanoTime() < until
   245	          do Thread.sleep(50)
   246	          assert(read[JobResponse](c.get(a.statusUrl).body).state == JobState.SUCCEEDED)
   247	          val counters = wc.get("/metrics").body("counters")
   248	          assert(counters("queueReceives").num == 1 && counters("jobsSucceeded").num == 1)
   249	          assert(counters("answers").num == 1 && counters("verifiedClaims").num >= 1)
   250	        }
   251	      }
   252	    }
   253	  }
```

### modules/spark/src/main/scala/scholarchain/spark/CorpusWindowSummaryJob.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.spark
     2	
     3	import org.apache.hadoop.fs.Path
     4	import org.apache.spark.sql.expressions.Window
     5	import org.apache.spark.sql.functions.*
     6	import org.apache.spark.sql.types.StructType
     7	import org.apache.spark.sql.{Column, DataFrame, Row, SparkSession}
     8	import scholarchain.core.config.{ConfigLoader, ScholarChainConfig}
     9	
    10	/** Corpus-level window summaries (Phase 10 work order 7, FU2): one row per configured window with
    11	  * the graph's node and edge counts, components, largest component, singletons, communities, and
    12	  * largest community, recomputed from the Phase 6 Parquet tables and checked against the Phase 6
    13	  * `temporal/window-summary`.
    14	  *
    15	  * Input: one complete TemporalSummaryExportJob run directory (`--input`), read-only; its manifest
    16	  * names the community-path, researcher-analytics, and canonical-graph runs, which must form one
    17	  * chain. Output and run identity: JobRun (a new run; nothing in the input runs is written).
    18	  *
    19	  * Usage: `sbt "sparkAnalytics/runMain scholarchain.spark.CorpusWindowSummaryJob --input
    20	  * <temporal-summary-export run dir> --code-version <v> [--out <root>] [--checkpoint-dir <dir>]"`
    21	  */
    22	object CorpusWindowSummaryJob:
    23	  val Stage = "spark-corpus-window-summary"
    24	  val Table = "corpus/window-summary"
    25	  val ViolationsTable = "corpus/window-summary-violations"
    26	  val NodeBasis =
    27	    "researchers with at least one DBLP publication in the window (one analytics/researcher-metrics " +
    28	      "row each); researchers with only OpenAlex or Crossref authorship are not nodes"
    29	
    30	  /** Columns shared with the Phase 6 `temporal/window-summary`; they must be equal. */
    31	  val Phase6Checked = Seq(
    32	    "researchers",
    33	    "coauthorEdges",
    34	    "sumJointPublicationCount",
    35	    "components",
    36	    "largestComponentSize",
    37	    "singletonResearchers",
    38	    "communities",
    39	    "largestCommunitySize"
    40	  )
    41	
    42	  final case class RunResult(runId: String, attempt: Int, runDir: String, manifest: ujson.Obj)
    43	
    44	  def main(args: Array[String]): Unit =
    45	    val s = SparkSettings.parse(args.toSeq) match
    46	      case Right(v) => v
    47	      case Left(e)  => System.err.println(e); sys.exit(2)
    48	    val cfg = ConfigLoader.load().fold(es => sys.error(es.mkString("; ")), identity)
    49	    val spark = JobRun.session(s, Stage)
    50	    try
    51	      val r = run(spark, s, cfg)
    52	      println(s"runId=${r.runId} attempt=${r.attempt} runDir=${r.runDir}")
    53	    finally spark.stop()
    54	
    55	  /** The per-window values, from four Phase 6 tables (all keyed by `window`):
    56	    *   - metrics: one row per graph node (researcher-metrics);
    57	    *   - edges: coauthor edges with `jointPublicationCount`;
    58	    *   - components: `componentId`, `componentSize`; communities: `communityId`, `communitySize`.
    59	    *
    60	    * Largest component and community: the biggest, ties to the smallest ID (as jobs 5 and 9). Count
    61	    * columns are 0 for a window with no rows; fractions are null when the window has no researcher.
    62	    * `componentMembers` and `communityMembers` (sums of sizes) are kept for the integrity checks.
    63	    */
    64	  def summarize(
    65	      windows: DataFrame,
    66	      metrics: DataFrame,
    67	      edges: DataFrame,
    68	      components: DataFrame,
    69	      communities: DataFrame,
    70	      minCommunitySize: Int
    71	  ): DataFrame =
    72	    def per(df: DataFrame, aggs: Column*) = df.groupBy("window").agg(aggs.head, aggs.tail*)
    73	    def n(c: Column) = sum(when(c, 1L).otherwise(0L))
    74	    def largest(df: DataFrame, id: String, size: String, as: String) = df
    75	      .withColumn(
    76	        "r",
    77	        row_number().over(Window.partitionBy("window").orderBy(desc(size), col(id)))
    78	      )
    79	      .where(col("r") === 1)
    80	      .select(col("window"), col(id).as(as))
    81	    val counts = Seq(
    82	      "researchers",
    83	      "coauthorEdges",
    84	      "sumJointPublicationCount",
    85	      "components",
    86	      "largestComponentSize",
    87	      "singletonResearchers",
    88	      "componentMembers",
    89	      "communities",
    90	      "largestCommunitySize",
    91	      "singletonCommunities",
    92	      "communitiesAtLeastMinSize",
    93	      "communityMembers"
    94	    )
    95	    def share(c: String) =
    96	      when(col("researchers") > 0, col(c) / col("researchers")).cast("double")
    97	    Seq(
    98	      per(metrics, count(lit(1)).as("researchers")),
    99	      per(
   100	        edges,
   101	        count(lit(1)).as("coauthorEdges"),
   102	        sum("jointPublicationCount").as("sumJointPublicationCount")
   103	      ),
   104	      per(
   105	        components,
   106	        count(lit(1)).as("components"),
   107	        max("componentSize").as("largestComponentSize"),
   108	        n(col("componentSize") === 1).as("singletonResearchers"),
   109	        sum("componentSize").as("componentMembers")
   110	      ),
   111	      largest(components, "componentId", "componentSize", "largestComponentId"),
   112	      per(
   113	        communities,
   114	        count(lit(1)).as("communities"),
   115	        max("communitySize").as("largestCommunitySize"),
   116	        n(col("communitySize") === 1).as("singletonCommunities"),
   117	        n(col("communitySize") >= minCommunitySize).as("communitiesAtLeastMinSize"),
   118	        sum("communitySize").as("communityMembers")
   119	      ),
   120	      largest(communities, "communityId", "communitySize", "largestCommunityId")
   121	    ).foldLeft(windows)((d, x) => d.join(x, Seq("window"), "left"))
   122	      .select(
   123	        (windows.columns.toSeq.map(col) ++
   124	          counts.map(c => coalesce(col(c).cast("long"), lit(0L)).as(c)) ++
   125	          Seq(col("largestComponentId"), col("largestCommunityId")))*
   126	      )
   127	      .withColumn("years", col("windowEndYear") - col("windowStartYear") + 1)
   128	      .withColumn("largestComponentFraction", share("largestComponentSize"))
   129	      .withColumn("largestCommunityFraction", share("largestCommunitySize"))
   130	      .withColumn("communityMinSize", lit(minCommunitySize))
   131	
   132	  /** Integrity checks of a `summarize` result, one violation row per failure:
   133	    *   - `COMPONENT_MEMBERS_MISMATCH` / `COMMUNITY_MEMBERS_MISMATCH`: the component (community)
   134	    *     sizes of a window do not sum to its researchers (every node is in exactly one of each);
   135	    *   - `PHASE6_WINDOW_SUMMARY_MISMATCH`: a `Phase6Checked` value (plus
   136	    *     `communitiesAtLeastMinSize` when `sameMinSize`) differs from the Phase 6
   137	    *     `temporal/window-summary` row, or the window has no Phase 6 row. subjectId = window,
   138	    *     relatedId = column.
   139	    */
   140	  def violations(summary: DataFrame, phase6: DataFrame, sameMinSize: Boolean): DataFrame =
   141	    def members(check: String, c: String) = Violations(
   142	      check,
   143	      summary.where(col(c) =!= col("researchers")),
   144	      col("window"),
   145	      lit(c),
   146	      concat_ws(" != ", col(c).cast("string"), col("researchers").cast("string"))
   147	    )
   148	    val checked = Phase6Checked ++ Option.when(sameMinSize)("communitiesAtLeastMinSize")
   149	    val p6 = phase6.select(
   150	      (col("window") +: checked.map(c => col(c).cast("long").as(s"p6_$c")))*
   151	    )
   152	    val joined = summary.join(p6, Seq("window"), "left")
   153	    (members("COMPONENT_MEMBERS_MISMATCH", "componentMembers") +:
   154	      members("COMMUNITY_MEMBERS_MISMATCH", "communityMembers") +:
   155	      checked.map(c =>
   156	        Violations(
   157	          "PHASE6_WINDOW_SUMMARY_MISMATCH",
   158	          joined.where(!col(c).eqNullSafe(col(s"p6_$c"))),
   159	          col("window"),
   160	          lit(c),
   161	          concat_ws(
   162	            " != ",
   163	            col(c).cast("string"),
   164	            coalesce(col(s"p6_$c").cast("string"), lit("null"))
   165	          )
   166	        )
   167	      )).reduce(_ union _)
   168	
   169	  def run(spark: SparkSession, s: SparkSettings, cfg: ScholarChainConfig): RunResult =
   170	    val hconf = spark.sparkContext.hadoopConfiguration
   171	    def manifestOf(p: Path) =
   172	      ujson.read(JobRun.readText(p.getFileSystem(hconf), new Path(p, "_manifest.json")))
   173	    def sizeOf(p: Path) = p.getFileSystem(hconf).getContentSummary(p).getLength
   174	    val exPath = new Path(s.input)
   175	    val ex = manifestOf(exPath)
   176	    require(ex("stage").str == TemporalSummaryExportJob.Stage, s"${s.input} is not an export run")
   177	    val cpPath = new Path(ex("input")("path").str)
   178	    val raPath = new Path(ex("input")("researcherAnalyticsPath").str)
   179	    val cgPath = new Path(ex("input")("canonicalGraphPath").str)
   180	    val (cp, ra, cg) = (manifestOf(cpPath), manifestOf(raPath), manifestOf(cgPath))
   181	    require(cp("runId").str == ex("input")("communityPathRunId").str, "community-path run moved")
   182	    require(ra("runId").str == ex("input")("researcherAnalyticsRunId").str, "analytics run moved")
   183	    require(cg("runId").str == ex("input")("canonicalGraphRunId").str, "canonical run moved")
   184	    require(cg("runId").str == ra("input")("canonicalGraphRunId").str, "runs do not form one chain")
   185	    require(ra("runId").str == cp("input")("researcherAnalyticsRunId").str, "runs do not chain")
   186	    val windows = cfg.graph.windows
   187	    val windowNames = windows.map(w => s"${w.name}:${w.startYear}-${w.endYear}").mkString(",")
   188	    require(
   189	      ex("fingerprints").arr.exists(_.str == s"config:windows=$windowNames"),
   190	      "configured windows differ from the input run's windows"
   191	    )
   192	    val graphPolicy = ex("settings")("graphPolicy").str
   193	    val phase6MinSize = ex("settings")("summaryMinCommunitySize").num.toInt
   194	    val fingerprints = Seq(
   195	      s"temporal-summary-export-run=${ex("runId").str}",
   196	      s"community-path-run=${cp("runId").str}",
   197	      s"researcher-analytics-run=${ra("runId").str}",
   198	      s"canonical-graph-run=${cg("runId").str}",
   199	      s"input:temporal-summary-export=$exPath:${sizeOf(exPath)}",
   200	      s"input:community-path=$cpPath:${sizeOf(cpPath)}",
   201	      s"input:researcher-analytics=$raPath:${sizeOf(raPath)}",
   202	      s"input:canonical-graph=$cgPath:${sizeOf(cgPath)}",
   203	      s"config:graph-policy=$graphPolicy",
   204	      s"config:windows=$windowNames",
   205	      s"config:summary-min-community-size=${s.summaryMinCommunitySize}"
   206	    )
   207	    val jr = new JobRun(spark, Stage, s, cfg, ex("snapshot").str, fingerprints)
   208	    val sourceRuns = ujson.Obj(
   209	      "temporalSummaryExportRunId" -> ex("runId").str,
   210	      "communityPathRunId" -> cp("runId").str,
   211	      "researcherAnalyticsRunId" -> ra("runId").str,
   212	      "canonicalGraphRunId" -> cg("runId").str,
   213	      "inputHadoopRunId" -> ex("lineage")("inputHadoopRunId").str,
   214	      "matchPolicyVersion" -> ex("lineage")("matchPolicyVersion").str
   215	    )
   216	    val head = jr.head(
   217	      "input" -> ujson.Obj(
   218	        "path" -> s.input,
   219	        "temporalSummaryExportRunId" -> ex("runId").str,
   220	        "communityPathPath" -> cpPath.toString,
   221	        "researcherAnalyticsPath" -> raPath.toString,
   222	        "canonicalGraphPath" -> cgPath.toString
   223	      ),
   224	      "lineage" -> ex("lineage"),
   225	      "sourceRuns" -> sourceRuns,
   226	      "settings" -> ujson.Obj(
   227	        "master" -> spark.sparkContext.master,
   228	        "shufflePartitions" -> s.shufflePartitions,
   229	        "graphPolicy" -> graphPolicy,
   230	        "windows" -> ex("settings")("windows"),
   231	        "communityMinSize" -> s.summaryMinCommunitySize,
   232	        "phase6CommunityMinSize" -> phase6MinSize,
   233	        "nodeBasis" -> NodeBasis,
   234	        "minFreeDiskGiB" -> s.minFreeDiskGiB.toDouble,
   235	        "driverMaxHeapBytes" -> Runtime.getRuntime.maxMemory.toDouble
   236	      )
   237	    )
   238	    jr.start(head)
   239	    import jr.{step, write}
   240	    val ctx =
   241	      Ctx(ex("snapshot").str, jr.runId, cg("settings")("canonicalPolicy").str, graphPolicy, 0)
   242	
   243	    val recorded =
   244	      Seq(exPath -> ex, cpPath -> cp, raPath -> ra, cgPath -> cg).map((p, m) => p.toString -> m)
   245	    def input(dir: Path, rel: String, cols: String*): DataFrame = step("read inputs") {
   246	      val p = new Path(dir, rel)
   247	      val t = recorded.toMap.apply(dir.toString)("tables").arr.find(_("name").str == rel)
   248	      // An empty partitioned table has no files to read: use the schema its manifest recorded.
   249	      val raw =
   250	        if t.exists(_("rowCount").num == 0) then
   251	          spark.createDataFrame(java.util.List.of[Row](), StructType.fromDDL(t.get("schema").str))
   252	        else spark.read.parquet(p.toString)
   253	      val df = raw.select(cols.map(col)*)
   254	      jr.recordInput(p.toString, p.getFileSystem(hconf).exists(p), df.count())
   255	      df
   256	    }
   257	    val metrics = input(raPath, "analytics/researcher-metrics", "window", "researcherId")
   258	    val components = input(raPath, "analytics/components", "window", "componentId", "componentSize")
   259	    val communities =
   260	      input(cpPath, "analytics/communities", "window", "communityId", "communitySize")
   261	    val edges = input(cgPath, "graph/coauthor-edges", "window", "jointPublicationCount")
   262	    val phase6Cols = ("window" +: Phase6Checked) :+ "communitiesAtLeastMinSize"
   263	    val phase6 = input(exPath, "temporal/window-summary", phase6Cols*)
   264	
   265	    // Label-propagation convergence per window, from the community-path manifest (3 values).
   266	    val converged = cp("counts")("communityConverged").obj
   267	    val convergence = spark.createDataFrame(
   268	      java.util.Arrays.asList(
   269	        windows.map(w => Row(w.name, converged.get(w.name).map(_.bool).orNull))*
   270	      ),
   271	      StructType.fromDDL("window STRING, communityConverged BOOLEAN")
   272	    )
   273	    val rounds = cp("counts")("communityIterations").num.toInt
   274	
   275	    val summary = step("summarize") {
   276	      summarize(
   277	        CoauthorGraph.windows(spark, windows),
   278	        metrics,
   279	        edges,
   280	        components,
   281	        communities,
   282	        s.summaryMinCommunitySize
   283	      ).join(convergence, Seq("window"), "left")
   284	        .withColumn("communityRounds", lit(rounds))
   285	        .persist()
   286	    }
   287	    val stamped = summary
   288	      .drop("componentMembers", "communityMembers")
   289	      .withColumn("nodeBasis", lit(NodeBasis))
   290	      .withColumn(
   291	        "sourceRuns",
   292	        struct(sourceRuns.value.toSeq.map((k, v) => lit(v.str).as(k))*)
   293	      )
   294	      .withColumn("provenance", ctx.provenance(col("window"), graphPolicy))
   295	      .orderBy("windowStartYear", "windowEndYear")
   296	    val written = write(Table, stamped, Seq("dynamodb", "s3"), 1)
   297	    val violated = write(
   298	      ViolationsTable,
   299	      Violations.stamped(
   300	        ctx,
   301	        violations(summary, phase6, phase6MinSize == s.summaryMinCommunitySize)
   302	      ),
   303	      Seq("s3"),
   304	      1
   305	    )
   306	    val counts = step("report") {
   307	      ujson.Obj(
   308	        "windowSummary" -> JobRun.rows(
   309	          written.drop("provenance", "sourceRuns", "nodeBasis").orderBy("windowStartYear", "window")
   310	        ),
   311	        "integrityViolations" -> JobRun.rows(
   312	          violated.drop("provenance").orderBy("check", "subjectId", "relatedId")
   313	        )
   314	      )
   315	    }
   316	    val manifest = jr.finish(head, "counts" -> counts)
   317	    summary.unpersist()
   318	    RunResult(jr.runId, jr.attempt, jr.runDir.toString, manifest)
```

### modules/spark/src/test/scala/scholarchain/spark/CorpusWindowSummarySpec.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.spark
     2	
     3	import org.apache.spark.sql.functions.*
     4	import org.apache.spark.sql.types.StructType
     5	import org.apache.spark.sql.{DataFrame, Row}
     6	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     7	import scholarchain.core.config.TimeWindow
     8	
     9	import java.nio.file.{Files, Path}
    10	import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*
    11	
    12	/** Corpus-level window summaries (Phase 10 work order 7). Expected values are worked out by hand.
    13	  */
    14	class CorpusWindowSummarySpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    15	  private lazy val spark = TestSpark.spark
    16	
    17	  private def df(schema: String, rows: Seq[Any]*): DataFrame =
    18	    spark.createDataFrame(
    19	      java.util.Arrays.asList(rows.map(r => Row.fromSeq(r))*),
    20	      StructType.fromDDL(schema)
    21	    )
    22	
    23	  // Window e (2000-2009): triangle a-b-c (component X, community E1) and isolated d (Y, E2).
    24	  //   edges a-b (2 joint works), a-c (1), b-c (1): 3 edges, 4 joint works.
    25	  // Window l (2010-2020): a-b (1) and d-e (3); components P {a,b} and Q {d,e} tie at 2, so the
    26	  //   largest is P (smallest ID); communities L1 {a,b} and L2 {d,e} tie the same way.
    27	  // Window z (2021-2022): no researcher at all.
    28	  private val windows =
    29	    CoauthorGraph.windows(
    30	      spark,
    31	      Seq(TimeWindow("e", 2000, 2009), TimeWindow("l", 2010, 2020), TimeWindow("z", 2021, 2022))
    32	    )
    33	  private val metrics = df(
    34	    "window STRING, researcherId STRING",
    35	    Seq("e", "a"),
    36	    Seq("e", "b"),
    37	    Seq("e", "c"),
    38	    Seq("e", "d"),
    39	    Seq("l", "a"),
    40	    Seq("l", "b"),
    41	    Seq("l", "d"),
    42	    Seq("l", "e")
    43	  )
    44	  private val edges = df(
    45	    "window STRING, jointPublicationCount BIGINT",
    46	    Seq("e", 2L),
    47	    Seq("e", 1L),
    48	    Seq("e", 1L),
    49	    Seq("l", 1L),
    50	    Seq("l", 3L)
    51	  )
    52	  private val components = df(
    53	    "window STRING, componentId STRING, componentSize BIGINT",
    54	    Seq("e", "X", 3L),
    55	    Seq("e", "Y", 1L),
    56	    Seq("l", "Q", 2L),
    57	    Seq("l", "P", 2L)
    58	  )
    59	  private val communities = df(
    60	    "window STRING, communityId STRING, communitySize BIGINT",
    61	    Seq("e", "E1", 3L),
    62	    Seq("e", "E2", 1L),
    63	    Seq("l", "L2", 2L),
    64	    Seq("l", "L1", 2L)
    65	  )
    66	  private def summary(m: DataFrame = metrics) =
    67	    CorpusWindowSummaryJob.summarize(windows, m, edges, components, communities, 3)
    68	
    69	  test("per window: nodes, edges, components, largest component, singletons, communities") {
    70	    val cols = Seq(
    71	      "window",
    72	      "years",
    73	      "researchers",
    74	      "coauthorEdges",
    75	      "sumJointPublicationCount",
    76	      "components",
    77	      "largestComponentId",
    78	      "largestComponentSize",
    79	      "largestComponentFraction",
    80	      "singletonResearchers",
    81	      "communities",
    82	      "largestCommunityId",
    83	      "largestCommunitySize",
    84	      "largestCommunityFraction",
    85	      "singletonCommunities",
    86	      "communityMinSize",
    87	      "communitiesAtLeastMinSize"
    88	    )
    89	    val got = summary().select(cols.map(col)*).orderBy("window").collect().map(_.toSeq).toSeq
    90	    assert(
    91	      got == Seq(
    92	        Seq("e", 10, 4L, 3L, 4L, 2L, "X", 3L, 0.75, 1L, 2L, "E1", 3L, 0.75, 1L, 3, 1L),
    93	        Seq("l", 11, 4L, 2L, 4L, 2L, "P", 2L, 0.5, 0L, 2L, "L1", 2L, 0.5, 0L, 3, 0L),
    94	        Seq("z", 2, 0L, 0L, 0L, 0L, null, 0L, null, 0L, 0L, null, 0L, null, 0L, 3, 0L)
    95	      )
    96	    )
    97	  }
    98	
    99	  test("checks: member sums and every shared value against the Phase 6 window summary") {
   100	    val s = summary()
   101	    val same = s.select(
   102	      (col("window") +: (CorpusWindowSummaryJob.Phase6Checked :+ "communitiesAtLeastMinSize")
   103	        .map(col))*
   104	    )
   105	    assert(CorpusWindowSummaryJob.violations(s, same, sameMinSize = true).count() == 0)
   106	    // Phase 6 differs on e.components and has no row for l or z.
   107	    val tampered = same.where(col("window") === "e").withColumn("components", lit(9L))
   108	    def got(v: DataFrame) =
   109	      v.select("check", "subjectId", "relatedId").collect().map(r => (r(0), r(1), r(2))).toSet
   110	    val p6 = got(CorpusWindowSummaryJob.violations(s, tampered, sameMinSize = false))
   111	    val missing = for
   112	      w <- Seq("l", "z")
   113	      c <- CorpusWindowSummaryJob.Phase6Checked
   114	    yield ("PHASE6_WINDOW_SUMMARY_MISMATCH", w, c)
   115	    assert(p6 == (missing :+ ("PHASE6_WINDOW_SUMMARY_MISMATCH", "e", "components")).toSet)
   116	    // The threshold column is compared only when both runs used the same minimum size (then the
   117	    // tampered e value and the missing l and z rows fail it too).
   118	    val thr = tampered.withColumn("communitiesAtLeastMinSize", lit(7L))
   119	    assert(
   120	      got(CorpusWindowSummaryJob.violations(s, thr, sameMinSize = true)) -- p6 ==
   121	        Seq("e", "l", "z")
   122	          .map(w => ("PHASE6_WINDOW_SUMMARY_MISMATCH", w, "communitiesAtLeastMinSize"))
   123	          .toSet
   124	    )
   125	    // A node missing from the metrics leaves component and community sizes summing past it.
   126	    val short = summary(metrics.where(!(col("window") === "e" && col("researcherId") === "d")))
   127	    assert(
   128	      got(CorpusWindowSummaryJob.violations(short, same, sameMinSize = true))
   129	        .filterNot(_._1 == "PHASE6_WINDOW_SUMMARY_MISMATCH") == Set(
   130	        ("COMPONENT_MEMBERS_MISMATCH", "e", "componentMembers"),
   131	        ("COMMUNITY_MEMBERS_MISMATCH", "e", "communityMembers")
   132	      )
   133	    )
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  // ---- End to end on the Fixture pipeline: a new run that leaves the Phase 6 runs untouched ------
   137	  test("the job writes a new run equal to the Phase 6 window summary, with provenance") {
   138	    val p = PipelineFixture.result
   139	    val st = p.manifest("stages").arr
   140	    val dirs = st.map(_("runDir").str).toSeq
   141	    val (cgDir, raDir, cpDir, exDir) = (dirs(0), dirs(1), dirs(2), dirs(3))
   142	    def listing(dir: String) =
   143	      Files.walk(Path.of(dir)).iterator.asScala.map(f => f.toString -> f.toFile.lastModified).toSet
   144	    val before = Seq(cgDir, raDir, cpDir, exDir).map(listing)
   145	    val tmp = Files.createTempDirectory("sc-corpus")
   146	    val s = PipelineFixture.base.copy(
   147	      input = exDir,
   148	      out = tmp.resolve("out").toString,
   149	      checkpointDir = tmp.resolve("ck").toString
   150	    )
   151	    val r = CorpusWindowSummaryJob.run(spark, s, PipelineFixture.cfg)
   152	    assert(Seq(cgDir, raDir, cpDir, exDir).map(listing) == before)
