   153	    assert(r.manifest("stage").str == CorpusWindowSummaryJob.Stage)
   154	    assert(r.manifest("counts")("integrityViolations").arr.isEmpty)
   155	
   156	    val t = spark.read.parquet(s"${r.runDir}/${CorpusWindowSummaryJob.Table}")
   157	    val names = PipelineFixture.cfg.graph.windows.map(_.name)
   158	    assert(t.count() == names.size)
   159	    val phase6 = spark.read.parquet(s"$exDir/temporal/window-summary")
   160	    val shared = "window" +: CorpusWindowSummaryJob.Phase6Checked :+ "communitiesAtLeastMinSize"
   161	    def rows(d: DataFrame) = d.select(shared.map(c => col(c).cast("string"))*).collect().toSet
   162	    assert(rows(t) == rows(phase6))
   163	    assert(t.where(col("schemaVersion") =!= "spark-corpus-window-summary-v1").count() == 0)
   164	    assert(
   165	      t.where(
   166	        col("provenance.runId") =!= r.runId || col("provenance.sourceRecordId") =!= col("window") ||
   167	          col("provenance.sourceDataset") =!= "DERIVED" ||
   168	          col("sourceRuns.temporalSummaryExportRunId") =!= st(3)("runId").str ||
   169	          col("sourceRuns.communityPathRunId") =!= st(2)("runId").str ||
   170	          col("sourceRuns.researcherAnalyticsRunId") =!= st(1)("runId").str ||
   171	          col("sourceRuns.canonicalGraphRunId") =!= st(0)("runId").str ||
   172	          col("sourceRuns.inputHadoopRunId") =!= Fixture.HadoopRunId ||
   173	          col("nodeBasis") =!= CorpusWindowSummaryJob.NodeBasis
   174	      ).count() == 0
   175	    )
   176	    // The largest component's ID names a component of that size in the Phase 6 table.
   177	    val comps = spark.read.parquet(s"$raDir/analytics/components")
   178	    assert(
   179	      t.join(
   180	        comps,
   181	        t("window") === comps("window") && t("largestComponentId") === comps("componentId") &&
   182	          t("largestComponentSize") === comps("componentSize")
   183	      ).count() == t.where(col("largestComponentId").isNotNull).count()
   184	    )
   185	    // A rerun is the next attempt with the same values.
   186	    val again = CorpusWindowSummaryJob.run(spark, s, PipelineFixture.cfg)
   187	    assert(again.attempt == 2 && again.runDir != r.runDir)
   188	    def values(dir: String) = spark.read
   189	      .parquet(s"$dir/${CorpusWindowSummaryJob.Table}")
   190	      .drop("provenance")
   191	      .collect()
   192	      .map(_.toSeq)
   193	      .toSet
   194	    assert(values(again.runDir) == values(r.runDir))
   195	  }
```

### modules/storage/src/main/scala/scholarchain/storage/JobQueue.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.storage
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.core.{IdFormat, StableIds}
     4	
     5	import java.time.Instant
     6	import scala.collection.mutable
     7	import scala.util.Try
     8	
     9	/** The body of one job message (Phase 10 ruling 7): the job and query IDs only, never the request.
    10	  * Wire form: {"schemaVersion":"job-message-v1","jobId":...,"queryId":...}.
    11	  */
    12	final case class JobMessage(jobId: String, queryId: String)
    13	
    14	object JobMessage:
    15	  val SchemaVersion = "job-message-v1"
    16	
    17	  /** The message of queryId's job (jobId = StableIds.jobId(queryId)). */
    18	  def of(queryId: String): JobMessage = JobMessage(StableIds.jobId(queryId), queryId)
    19	
    20	  def render(m: JobMessage): String =
    21	    ujson.Obj("schemaVersion" -> SchemaVersion, "jobId" -> m.jobId, "queryId" -> m.queryId).render()
    22	
    23	  /** Left for anything but exactly the three fields, with jobId = StableIds.jobId(queryId). */
    24	  def parse(body: String): Either[String, JobMessage] =
    25	    Try(ujson.read(body)).toOption.flatMap(_.objOpt) match
    26	      case Some(o) if o.keySet == Set("schemaVersion", "jobId", "queryId") =>
    27	        (o("schemaVersion").strOpt, o("jobId").strOpt, o("queryId").strOpt) match
    28	          case (Some(SchemaVersion), Some(j), Some(q))
    29	              if IdFormat.isQueryId(q) && j == StableIds.jobId(q) =>
    30	            Right(JobMessage(j, q))
    31	          case _ => Left("job message fields are malformed")
    32	      case _ => Left("job message is not a job-message-v1 JSON object")
    33	
    34	/** One received message. receiveCount counts this receive (1 on the first). message is Left for a
    35	  * body that is not a valid job message (a poison message: fail or drop it, never run it).
    36	  */
    37	final case class ReceivedJob(
    38	    messageId: String,
    39	    receiptHandle: String,
    40	    receiveCount: Int,
    41	    message: Either[String, JobMessage]
    42	)
    43	
    44	/** SQS bounds (ReceiveMessage, ChangeMessageVisibility; STORAGE.md service limits). */
    45	object JobQueueLimits:
    46	  val MaxMessages = 10
    47	  val MaxWaitSeconds = 20
    48	  val MaxVisibilitySeconds = 43200
    49	
    50	  def check(maxMessages: Int, waitSeconds: Int, visibilitySeconds: Int): Unit =
    51	    require(maxMessages >= 1 && maxMessages <= MaxMessages, s"maxMessages $maxMessages: 1 to 10")
    52	    require(waitSeconds >= 0 && waitSeconds <= MaxWaitSeconds, s"waitSeconds $waitSeconds: 0 to 20")
    53	    visibility(visibilitySeconds)
    54	
    55	  def visibility(s: Int): Unit =
    56	    require(s >= 0 && s <= MaxVisibilitySeconds, s"visibility $s s: 0 to 43200")
    57	
    58	/** The job queue (Phase 10 ruling 7): an SQS standard queue in the cloud (live.SqsJobQueue), an
    59	  * InMemoryJobQueue locally. Delivery is at least once; the job store's conditional transitions
    60	  * make a redelivered message harmless.
    61	  */
    62	trait JobQueuePort:
    63	  /** Sends one message; returns its message ID. */
    64	  def send(message: JobMessage): String
    65	
    66	  /** Long poll: waits up to waitSeconds (0 to 20) for visible messages, returns at most maxMessages
    67	    * (1 to 10), each hidden for visibilitySeconds (0 to 43200; services.worker
    68	    * .visibility-timeout-seconds) from this receive.
    69	    */
    70	  def receive(maxMessages: Int, waitSeconds: Int, visibilitySeconds: Int): Vector[ReceivedJob]
    71	
    72	  /** Deletes the message received with this handle. False when the handle is not the latest one of
    73	    * a message still in the queue (a stale handle: the message may come back).
    74	    */
    75	  def delete(receiptHandle: String): Boolean
    76	
    77	  /** Hides the in-flight message for seconds (0 to 43200) from now. False when the handle is stale
    78	    * or the message is not in flight.
    79	    */
    80	  def changeVisibility(receiptHandle: String, seconds: Int): Boolean
    81	
    82	/** Local fake of the job queue, thread-safe, in memory. It models an SQS standard queue with a
    83	  * redrive policy: a received message is hidden until now + visibilitySeconds; each receive counts
    84	  * and gets a new receipt handle, and only the latest handle deletes or changes the message; a
    85	  * message already received maxReceiveCount times (services.queue.max-receive-count) moves to
    86	  * deadLetters on the next receive attempt instead of being delivered. Messages are delivered in
    87	  * send order (SQS standard queues promise no order). Visibility uses now (inject a clock in
    88	  * tests); the long-poll wait uses real time, rechecked every 50 ms, and a send wakes it. Retention
    89	  * periods and SQS duplicate deliveries are not modelled.
    90	  */
    91	final class InMemoryJobQueue(maxReceiveCount: Int, now: () => Instant = () => Instant.now())
    92	    extends JobQueuePort:
    93	  require(maxReceiveCount >= 1, "maxReceiveCount must be at least 1")
    94	
    95	  private final class Msg(val id: String, val body: String):
    96	    var visibleAtMs = 0L
    97	    var receives = 0
    98	    var handle: Option[String] = None
    99	
   100	  private val messages = mutable.LinkedHashMap.empty[String, Msg]
   101	  private val dead = mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
   102	  private var sent = 0L
   103	
   104	  private def nowMs = now().toEpochMilli
   105	
   106	  def send(message: JobMessage): String = sendRaw(JobMessage.render(message))
   107	
   108	  /** Sends any body, for poison-message tests. */
   109	  def sendRaw(body: String): String = synchronized {
   110	    sent += 1
   111	    val m = Msg(f"msg-$sent%08d", body)
   112	    m.visibleAtMs = nowMs
   113	    messages(m.id) = m
   114	    notifyAll()
   115	    m.id
   116	  }
   117	
   118	  def receive(maxMessages: Int, waitSeconds: Int, visibilitySeconds: Int): Vector[ReceivedJob] =
   119	    JobQueueLimits.check(maxMessages, waitSeconds, visibilitySeconds)
   120	    val deadline = System.nanoTime + waitSeconds * 1000000000L
   121	    synchronized {
   122	      var out = take(maxMessages, visibilitySeconds)
   123	      while out.isEmpty && System.nanoTime < deadline do
   124	        wait(math.max(1L, math.min(50L, (deadline - System.nanoTime) / 1000000L)))
   125	        out = take(maxMessages, visibilitySeconds)
   126	      out
   127	    }
   128	
   129	  private def take(max: Int, visibilitySeconds: Int): Vector[ReceivedJob] =
   130	    val t = nowMs
   131	    val out = Vector.newBuilder[ReceivedJob]
   132	    var n = 0
   133	    messages.values.filter(_.visibleAtMs <= t).toVector.foreach { m =>
   134	      if n < max then
   135	        if m.receives >= maxReceiveCount then
   136	          messages.remove(m.id)
   137	          dead += m.body
   138	        else
   139	          m.receives += 1
   140	          m.handle = Some(s"rh-${m.id}-${m.receives}")
   141	          m.visibleAtMs = t + visibilitySeconds * 1000L
   142	          out += ReceivedJob(m.id, m.handle.get, m.receives, JobMessage.parse(m.body))
   143	          n += 1
   144	    }
   145	    out.result()
   146	
   147	  private def byHandle(h: String) = messages.values.find(_.handle.contains(h))
   148	
   149	  def delete(receiptHandle: String): Boolean = synchronized {
   150	    byHandle(receiptHandle).map(m => messages.remove(m.id)).isDefined
   151	  }
   152	
   153	  def changeVisibility(receiptHandle: String, seconds: Int): Boolean =
   154	    JobQueueLimits.visibility(seconds)
   155	    synchronized {
   156	      val t = nowMs
   157	      byHandle(receiptHandle).filter(_.visibleAtMs > t) match
   158	        case Some(m) =>
   159	          m.visibleAtMs = t + seconds * 1000L
   160	          true
   161	        case None => false
   162	    }
   163	
   164	  /** Bodies moved to the dead-letter queue, in move order. */
   165	  def deadLetters: Vector[String] = synchronized(dead.toVector)
   166	
   167	  /** Messages not deleted and not dead-lettered (visible or in flight). */
   168	  def size: Int = synchronized(messages.size)
```

### modules/storage/src/main/scala/scholarchain/storage/ServiceState.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.storage
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.core.{ErrorCode, IdFormat, JobState, StableIds}
     4	
     5	import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
     6	import java.nio.file.{Files, Path, StandardOpenOption}
     7	import java.time.Instant
     8	import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit
     9	import scala.collection.mutable
    10	import scala.util.Try
    11	
    12	/** Why a job FAILED: a core error code and a short message the service wrote (never user text,
    13	  * prompt text, model output, or keys).
    14	  */
    15	final case class JobError(code: ErrorCode, message: String)
    16	
    17	/** One async query job (Phase 10 ruling 7), item JOB in the ServiceState table. request is the
    18	  * canonical JSON of the accepted POST /v1/query body. stage is a workflow stage name. resultRef is
    19	  * an optional S3 key of the stored answer, set on SUCCEEDED. Timestamps are UTC, in milliseconds.
    20	  */
    21	final case class JobRecord(
    22	    jobId: String,
    23	    queryId: String,
    24	    state: JobState,
    25	    stage: Option[String],
    26	    request: String,
    27	    createdAt: Instant,
    28	    updatedAt: Instant,
    29	    startedAt: Option[Instant],
    30	    finishedAt: Option[Instant],
    31	    error: Option[JobError],
    32	    resultRef: Option[String],
    33	    expiresAt: Long
    34	)
    35	
    36	/** One Idempotency-Key (Phase 10 ruling 7), item IDEMPOTENCY in the ServiceState table. recordKey
    37	  * is idem-<sha256 hex of the key>, so the header value itself is never stored. queryId is the
    38	  * query the first request started: its answer, or job StableIds.jobId(queryId), is the stored
    39	  * response.
    40	  */
    41	final case class IdempotencyRecord(
    42	    recordKey: String,
    43	    requestHash: String,
    44	    queryId: String,
    45	    createdAt: Instant,
    46	    expiresAt: Long
    47	)
    48	
    49	/** A conditional create: Inserted (this call wrote it), Unchanged (the same record was already
    50	  * there), or Conflict (a different record holds the key; nothing was written). record is the one
    51	  * now stored under the key.
    52	  */
    53	final case class Stored[A](outcome: LoadOutcome, record: A)
    54	
    55	enum JobTransition:
    56	  /** The conditional write passed; job is the stored record. */
    57	  case Moved(job: JobRecord)
    58	
    59	  /** The job was not in the expected state (another worker moved it first) or does not exist. */
    60	  case Refused(current: Option[JobRecord])
    61	
    62	/** Item layout and rules of the ServiceState table (STORAGE.md, "Service state"). */
    63	object ServiceStateContract:
    64	  val JobType = "JOB"
    65	  val IdempotencyType = "IDEMPOTENCY"
    66	  val JobSchema = "service-job-v1"
    67	  val IdempotencySchema = "service-idempotency-v1"
    68	
    69	  /** A job holds its request (services.max-body-bytes is 64 KiB), so this table's budget is 96 KB,
    70	    * not the 32 KB of the others; still far under the 400 KB DynamoDB item limit.
    71	    */
    72	  val ItemBudgetBytes = 96 * 1024
    73	  val MaxErrorMessageChars = 500
    74	  private val StageName = "[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,63}".r
    75	  private val IdemKey = "idem-[0-9a-f]{64}".r
    76	
    77	  def idempotencyRecordKey(key: String): String =
    78	    "idem-" + Hashing.sha256(key).stripPrefix("sha256:")
    79	
    80	  import AttributeValue.*
    81	
    82	  private def s(i: Item, k: String) = i.get(k).collect { case S(v) => v }
    83	  private def n(i: Item, k: String) =
    84	    i.get(k).collect { case N(v) => Try(BigDecimal(v).toLongExact).toOption }.flatten
    85	  private def time(i: Item, k: String): Either[String, Option[Instant]] =
    86	    s(i, k) match
    87	      case None    => Right(None)
    88	      case Some(v) =>
    89	        Try(Instant.parse(v)).toEither.left.map(_ => s"$k is not an instant").map(Some(_))
    90	  private def opt(k: String, v: Option[String]) = v.map(x => k -> S(x))
    91	
    92	  def jobItem(j: JobRecord): Item =
    93	    Map(
    94	      "recordKey" -> S(j.jobId),
    95	      "recordType" -> S(JobType),
    96	      "queryId" -> S(j.queryId),
    97	      "state" -> S(j.state.toString),
    98	      "request" -> S(j.request),
    99	      "createdAt" -> S(j.createdAt.toString),
   100	      "updatedAt" -> S(j.updatedAt.toString),
   101	      DynamoContract.TtlAttribute -> AttributeValue.num(j.expiresAt),
   102	      "schemaVersion" -> S(JobSchema)
   103	    ) ++ opt("stage", j.stage) ++ opt("startedAt", j.startedAt.map(_.toString)) ++
   104	      opt("finishedAt", j.finishedAt.map(_.toString)) ++
   105	      opt("errorCode", j.error.map(_.code.toString)) ++
   106	      opt("errorMessage", j.error.map(_.message)) ++ opt("resultRef", j.resultRef)
   107	
   108	  /** Decodes a JOB item and checks every rule; Left names the first broken one. */
   109	  def job(i: Item): Either[String, JobRecord] =
   110	    for
   111	      _ <- Either.cond(s(i, "recordType").contains(JobType), (), "recordType is not JOB")
   112	      queryId <- s(i, "queryId").filter(IdFormat.isQueryId).toRight("queryId is not a query ID")
   113	      jobId <- s(i, "recordKey")
   114	        .filter(_ == StableIds.jobId(queryId))
   115	        .toRight("recordKey is not jobId(queryId)")
   116	      state <- s(i, "state")
   117	        .flatMap(x => JobState.values.find(_.toString == x))
   118	        .toRight("state is not a JobState")
   119	      request <- s(i, "request")
   120	        .filter(r => Try(ujson.read(r)).toOption.exists(_.objOpt.isDefined))
   121	        .toRight("request is not a JSON object")
   122	      stage <- s(i, "stage") match
   123	        case Some(x) if !StageName.matches(x) => Left("stage is not a stage name")
   124	        case other                            => Right(other)
   125	      created <- time(i, "createdAt").flatMap(_.toRight("missing createdAt"))
   126	      updated <- time(i, "updatedAt").flatMap(_.toRight("missing updatedAt"))
   127	      started <- time(i, "startedAt")
   128	      finished <- time(i, "finishedAt")
   129	      error <- (s(i, "errorCode"), s(i, "errorMessage")) match
   130	        case (None, None)       => Right(None)
   131	        case (Some(c), Some(m)) =>
   132	          ErrorCode.values
   133	            .find(_.toString == c)
   134	            .filter(_ => m.nonEmpty && m.length <= MaxErrorMessageChars)
   135	            .map(code => Some(JobError(code, m)))
   136	            .toRight(s"error must be a known code and 1 to $MaxErrorMessageChars characters")
   137	        case _ => Left("errorCode and errorMessage go together")
   138	      resultRef = s(i, "resultRef")
   139	      expiresAt <- n(i, DynamoContract.TtlAttribute).toRight("expiresAt is not epoch seconds")
   140	      _ <- Either.cond(error.isDefined == (state == JobState.FAILED), (), "error iff FAILED")
   141	      _ <- Either.cond(
   142	        finished.isDefined == state.isFinal,
   143	        (),
   144	        "finishedAt iff SUCCEEDED or FAILED"
   145	      )
   146	      _ <- Either.cond(
   147	        started.isDefined || state == JobState.QUEUED || state == JobState.FAILED,
   148	        (),
   149	        "RUNNING and SUCCEEDED need startedAt"
   150	      )
   151	      _ <- Either.cond(
   152	        !(state == JobState.QUEUED && started.isDefined),
   153	        (),
   154	        "QUEUED has no startedAt"
   155	      )
   156	      _ <- resultRef match
   157	        case None                                   => Right(())
   158	        case Some(_) if state != JobState.SUCCEEDED => Left("resultRef only on SUCCEEDED")
   159	        case Some(r) => S3Layout.keyProblems(r).headOption.map(p => s"resultRef: $p").toLeft(())
   160	    yield JobRecord(
   161	      jobId,
   162	      queryId,
   163	      state,
   164	      stage,
   165	      request,
   166	      created,
   167	      updated,
   168	      started,
   169	      finished,
   170	      error,
   171	      resultRef,
   172	      expiresAt
   173	    )
   174	
   175	  def idempotencyItem(r: IdempotencyRecord): Item =
   176	    Map(
   177	      "recordKey" -> S(r.recordKey),
   178	      "recordType" -> S(IdempotencyType),
   179	      "requestHash" -> S(r.requestHash),
   180	      "queryId" -> S(r.queryId),
   181	      "createdAt" -> S(r.createdAt.toString),
   182	      DynamoContract.TtlAttribute -> AttributeValue.num(r.expiresAt),
   183	      "schemaVersion" -> S(IdempotencySchema)
   184	    )
   185	
   186	  def idempotency(i: Item): Either[String, IdempotencyRecord] =
   187	    for
   188	      _ <- Either.cond(s(i, "recordType").contains(IdempotencyType), (), "not IDEMPOTENCY")
   189	      key <- s(i, "recordKey").filter(IdemKey.matches).toRight("recordKey is not idem-<sha256>")
   190	      hash <- s(i, "requestHash").filter(Hashing.Shape.matches).toRight("requestHash")
   191	      queryId <- s(i, "queryId").filter(IdFormat.isQueryId).toRight("queryId is not a query ID")
   192	      created <- time(i, "createdAt").flatMap(_.toRight("missing createdAt"))
   193	      expiresAt <- n(i, DynamoContract.TtlAttribute).toRight("expiresAt is not epoch seconds")
   194	    yield IdempotencyRecord(key, hash, queryId, created, expiresAt)
   195	
   196	  /** The table rule (DynamoContract.ServiceState): the item decodes as its recordType. */
   197	  def problems(i: Item): List[String] =
   198	    (s(i, "recordType") match
   199	      case Some(JobType)         => job(i).map(_ => ())
   200	      case Some(IdempotencyType) => idempotency(i).map(_ => ())
   201	      case _                     => Left("recordType is not JOB or IDEMPOTENCY")
   202	    ).left.toOption.toList
   203	
   204	  /** Every table rule (keys, sizes, TTL, schemaVersion, decoding) of one item. */
   205	  def validate(i: Item): Either[String, Unit] =
   206	    DynamoContract.problems(DynamoContract.ServiceState, i, Map.empty) match
   207	      case Nil => Right(())
   208	      case ps  => Left(ps.mkString("; "))
   209	
   210	/** The ServiceState table as JobStore and IdempotencyStore need it: consistent reads and two
   211	  * conditional puts. LocalServiceStateTable is the fake, live.DynamoServiceStateTable the DynamoDB
   212	  * adapter. Items reaching a put have passed ServiceStateContract.validate.
   213	  */
   214	trait ServiceStateTable:
   215	  /** The unexpired item under the key, or None. */
   216	  def get(recordKey: String, recordType: String): Option[Item]
   217	
   218	  /** Writes item when no unexpired item has its key; else Left(the stored item), nothing written.
   219	    */
   220	  def putIfAbsent(item: Item): Either[Item, Unit]
   221	
   222	  /** Writes item when the stored item's state is expected; else Left(the stored item, if any). */
   223	  def putIfState(item: Item, expected: JobState): Either[Option[Item], Unit]
   224	
   225	/** Local fake of the ServiceState table, thread-safe. With log = None it is in memory only. With
   226	  * log = Some(file) (for example scratch/services/<runId>/ServiceState.jsonl) every accepted write
   227	  * is appended as one item-file line before it is applied, and the log is replayed on open, the
   228	  * last line of a key winning. A partial last line left by a crash was never acknowledged and is
   229	  * cut; any other unreadable line stops the open. Reads hide expired items, as DynamoDB reads must.
   230	  */
   231	final class LocalServiceStateTable(log: Option[Path], now: () => Instant) extends ServiceStateTable:
   232	  private val items = mutable.HashMap.empty[(String, String), Item]
   233	  private val spec = DynamoContract.ServiceState
   234	
   235	  log.filter(Files.exists(_)).foreach { f =>
   236	    val bytes = Files.readAllBytes(f)
   237	    val complete = bytes.lastIndexOf('\n'.toByte) + 1
   238	    if complete < bytes.length then
   239	      scala.util.Using.resource(java.nio.channels.FileChannel.open(f, StandardOpenOption.WRITE))(
   240	        _.truncate(complete.toLong)
   241	      )
   242	    new String(bytes, 0, complete, UTF_8)
   243	      .split('\n')
   244	      .iterator
   245	      .filter(_.nonEmpty)
   246	      .zipWithIndex
   247	      .foreach { (l, k) =>
   248	        DynamoItemFile.parseLine(l).flatMap(i => ServiceStateContract.validate(i).map(_ => i)) match
   249	          case Right(i) => items(DynamoContract.key(spec, i)) = i
   250	          case Left(e)  => throw IllegalStateException(s"$f line ${k + 1}: cannot replay ($e)")
   251	      }
   252	  }
   253	
   254	  private def unexpired(i: Item) = DynamoContract.unexpired(spec, i, now().getEpochSecond)
   255	
   256	  private def write(item: Item): Unit =
   257	    log.foreach { f =>
   258	      Option(f.getParent).foreach(Files.createDirectories(_))
   259	      Files.writeString(
   260	        f,
   261	        DynamoItemFile.renderLine(item) + "\n",
   262	        UTF_8,
   263	        StandardOpenOption.CREATE,
   264	        StandardOpenOption.APPEND
   265	      )
   266	    }
   267	    items(DynamoContract.key(spec, item)) = AttributeValue.canonicalItem(item)
   268	
   269	  def get(recordKey: String, recordType: String): Option[Item] = synchronized(
   270	    items.get((recordKey, recordType)).filter(unexpired)
   271	  )
   272	
   273	  def putIfAbsent(item: Item): Either[Item, Unit] = synchronized {
   274	    items.get(DynamoContract.key(spec, item)).filter(unexpired) match
   275	      case Some(stored) => Left(stored)
   276	      case None         => Right(write(item))
   277	  }
   278	
   279	  def putIfState(item: Item, expected: JobState): Either[Option[Item], Unit] = synchronized {
   280	    val stored = items.get(DynamoContract.key(spec, item))
   281	    if stored.exists(_.get("state").contains(AttributeValue.S(expected.toString))) then
   282	      Right(write(item))
   283	    else Left(stored)
   284	  }
   285	
   286	private def millis(t: Instant): Instant = t.truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.MILLIS)
   287	
   288	/** Async query jobs (Phase 10 ruling 7) over a ServiceStateTable. One job per query: jobId =
   289	  * StableIds.jobId(queryId). Every write is conditional: create only when no unexpired job has the
   290	  * ID, and a transition only from the state the caller names, only along JobState.canMoveTo. The
   291	  * transition graph has no cycle, so the state condition alone means nobody moved the job in
   292	  * between, and a redelivered message can never run a job twice. expiresAt = createdAt + ttlHours
   293	  * (services.ttl.job-hours) and is kept by transitions.
   294	  */
   295	final class JobStore(table: ServiceStateTable, ttlHours: Int, now: () => Instant):
   296	  require(ttlHours >= 1, s"ttlHours $ttlHours must be at least 1")
   297	
   298	  /** A QUEUED job for queryId. Unchanged when the same queryId and request are already queued (a
   299	    * retried POST); Conflict when another request holds the job ID. Left for a malformed queryId or
   300	    * request (nothing written).
   301	    */
   302	  def create(queryId: String, request: String): Either[String, Stored[JobRecord]] =
   303	    if !IdFormat.isQueryId(queryId) then Left("queryId is not a query ID")
   304	    else
   305	      val t = millis(now())
   306	      val job = JobRecord(
   307	        StableIds.jobId(queryId),
   308	        queryId,
   309	        JobState.QUEUED,
   310	        None,
   311	        request,
   312	        t,
   313	        t,
   314	        None,
   315	        None,
   316	        None,
   317	        None,
   318	        t.getEpochSecond + ttlHours * 3600L
   319	      )
   320	      val item = ServiceStateContract.jobItem(job)
   321	      ServiceStateContract.validate(item).flatMap { _ =>
   322	        table.putIfAbsent(item) match
   323	          case Right(())    => Right(Stored(LoadOutcome.Inserted, job))
   324	          case Left(stored) =>
   325	            ServiceStateContract
   326	              .job(stored)
   327	              .map(s =>
   328	                Stored(
   329	                  if s.queryId == queryId && s.request == request then LoadOutcome.Unchanged
   330	                  else LoadOutcome.Conflict,
   331	                  s
   332	                )
   333	              )
   334	      }
   335	
   336	  /** The unexpired job; Right(None) when there is none or jobId is not job-<32 hex>. Left when the
   337	    * stored item does not decode.
   338	    */
   339	  def get(jobId: String): Either[String, Option[JobRecord]] =
   340	    if !IdFormat.isJobId(jobId) then Right(None)
   341	    else
   342	      table.get(jobId, ServiceStateContract.JobType) match
   343	        case None    => Right(None)
   344	        case Some(i) => ServiceStateContract.job(i).map(Some(_))
   345	
   346	  /** Moves the job from `from` to `to` with a conditional write. RUNNING sets startedAt; SUCCEEDED
   347	    * and FAILED set finishedAt; FAILED needs error; resultRef only with SUCCEEDED; stage, when
   348	    * given, replaces the stored one. Refused when the job is not in `from` (for example a second
   349	    * worker on a redelivered message). Left when `from -> to` is not a JobState transition or the
   350	    * new record breaks a rule (nothing written).
   351	    */
   352	  def transition(
   353	      jobId: String,
   354	      from: JobState,
   355	      to: JobState,
   356	      stage: Option[String] = None,
   357	      error: Option[JobError] = None,
   358	      resultRef: Option[String] = None
   359	  ): Either[String, JobTransition] =
   360	    if !from.canMoveTo(to) then Left(s"$from -> $to is not a job transition")
   361	    else
   362	      get(jobId).flatMap {
   363	        case None                           => Right(JobTransition.Refused(None))
   364	        case Some(cur) if cur.state != from => Right(JobTransition.Refused(Some(cur)))
   365	        case Some(cur)                      =>
   366	          val t = millis(now())
   367	          val next = cur.copy(
   368	            state = to,
   369	            stage = stage.orElse(cur.stage),
   370	            updatedAt = t,
   371	            startedAt = if to == JobState.RUNNING then Some(t) else cur.startedAt,
   372	            finishedAt = Option.when(to.isFinal)(t),
   373	            error = error,
   374	            resultRef = resultRef
   375	          )
   376	          val item = ServiceStateContract.jobItem(next)
   377	          ServiceStateContract.validate(item).flatMap { _ =>
   378	            table.putIfState(item, from) match
   379	              case Right(())        => Right(JobTransition.Moved(next))
   380	              case Left(None)       => Right(JobTransition.Refused(None))
   381	              case Left(Some(have)) =>
   382	                ServiceStateContract.job(have).map(j => JobTransition.Refused(Some(j)))
   383	          }
   384	      }
   385	
   386	/** Idempotency-Key records (Phase 10 ruling 7) over a ServiceStateTable. The key is stored only as
   387	  * its SHA-256. expiresAt = createdAt + ttlHours (services.ttl.idempotency-hours); an expired
   388	  * record no longer exists for reads or claims.
   389	  */
   390	final class IdempotencyStore(table: ServiceStateTable, ttlHours: Int, now: () => Instant):
   391	  require(ttlHours >= 1, s"ttlHours $ttlHours must be at least 1")
   392	
   393	  /** Claims key for a request (requestHash: sha256:<hex> of the canonical body) that starts
   394	    * queryId. Inserted: this request owns the key, go on with queryId. Unchanged: the same body
   395	    * claimed it before; replay the stored record's queryId (whose answer may still be running).
   396	    * Conflict: another body holds the key (409). Left for invalid input (nothing written).
   397	    */
   398	  def claim(
   399	      key: String,
   400	      requestHash: String,
   401	      queryId: String
   402	  ): Either[String, Stored[IdempotencyRecord]] =
   403	    if key.isEmpty then Left("empty idempotency key")
   404	    else if !Hashing.Shape.matches(requestHash) then Left("requestHash is not sha256:<64 hex>")
   405	    else if !IdFormat.isQueryId(queryId) then Left("queryId is not a query ID")
   406	    else
   407	      val t = millis(now())
   408	      val r = IdempotencyRecord(
   409	        ServiceStateContract.idempotencyRecordKey(key),
   410	        requestHash,
   411	        queryId,
   412	        t,
   413	        t.getEpochSecond + ttlHours * 3600L
   414	      )
   415	      val item = ServiceStateContract.idempotencyItem(r)
   416	      ServiceStateContract.validate(item).flatMap { _ =>
   417	        table.putIfAbsent(item) match
   418	          case Right(())    => Right(Stored(LoadOutcome.Inserted, r))
   419	          case Left(stored) =>
   420	            ServiceStateContract
   421	              .idempotency(stored)
   422	              .map(s =>
   423	                Stored(
   424	                  if s.requestHash == requestHash then LoadOutcome.Unchanged
   425	                  else LoadOutcome.Conflict,
   426	                  s
   427	                )
   428	              )
   429	      }
   430	
   431	  /** The unexpired record of key, if any. */
   432	  def get(key: String): Either[String, Option[IdempotencyRecord]] =
   433	    if key.isEmpty then Right(None)
   434	    else
   435	      table
   436	        .get(ServiceStateContract.idempotencyRecordKey(key), ServiceStateContract.IdempotencyType)
   437	        .fold[Either[String, Option[IdempotencyRecord]]](Right(None))(i =>
   438	          ServiceStateContract.idempotency(i).map(Some(_))
   439	        )
```

### modules/storage/src/main/scala/scholarchain/storage/WindowSummaries.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.storage
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.core.config.TimeWindow
     4	import scholarchain.core.{IdFormat, Provenance, SchemaVersions, SourceType}
     5	import upickle.default.{ReadWriter, read, write}
     6	
     7	import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
     8	import scala.util.Try
     9	
    10	/** The Spark runs a window summary was computed from (`sourceRuns` of the Spark contract). */
    11	final case class WindowSummarySourceRuns(
    12	    temporalSummaryExportRunId: String,
    13	    communityPathRunId: String,
    14	    researcherAnalyticsRunId: String,
    15	    canonicalGraphRunId: String,
    16	    inputHadoopRunId: String,
    17	    matchPolicyVersion: String
    18	) derives ReadWriter
    19	
    20	/** One row of the Spark `corpus/window-summary` table (`spark-corpus-window-summary-v1`,
    21	  * modules/spark/JOBS.md, "CorpusWindowSummaryJob"), field for field and in column order. A
    22	  * storage-local mirror of that contract; the Spark contract is authoritative. Every value is for
    23	  * `window` only. IDs and fractions are None only when researchers = 0.
    24	  */
    25	final case class WindowSummary(
    26	    window: String,
    27	    windowStartYear: Int,
    28	    windowEndYear: Int,
    29	    researchers: Long,
    30	    coauthorEdges: Long,
    31	    sumJointPublicationCount: Long,
    32	    components: Long,
    33	    largestComponentSize: Long,
    34	    singletonResearchers: Long,
    35	    communities: Long,
    36	    largestCommunitySize: Long,
    37	    singletonCommunities: Long,
    38	    communitiesAtLeastMinSize: Long,
    39	    largestComponentId: Option[String],
    40	    largestCommunityId: Option[String],
    41	    years: Int,
    42	    largestComponentFraction: Option[Double],
    43	    largestCommunityFraction: Option[Double],
    44	    communityMinSize: Int,
    45	    communityConverged: Boolean,
    46	    communityRounds: Int,
    47	    nodeBasis: String,
    48	    sourceRuns: WindowSummarySourceRuns,
    49	    provenance: Provenance,
    50	    schemaVersion: String
    51	) derives ReadWriter
    52	
    53	/** Read side of the corpus window summaries (STORAGE.md, "Window summaries"). */
    54	trait WindowSummaryReadPort:
    55	  /** The stored summary of one window, or None when the store has no row for that name. The caller
    56	    * maps the benchmark alias `all` to the first configured window (`all-years`) first.
    57	    */
    58	  def windowSummary(window: String): Option[WindowSummary]
    59	
    60	object WindowSummaries:
    61	  val SchemaVersion = "spark-corpus-window-summary-v1"
    62	  val KeyPrefix = "curated/window-summary/"
    63	  val SparkStage = "spark-corpus-window-summary"
    64	  val SparkTable = "corpus/window-summary"
    65	
    66	  /** The Spark table schema the store object mirrors, exactly as the Spark run manifest prints it.
    67	    */
    68	  val SparkSchema =
    69	    "window STRING,windowStartYear INT,windowEndYear INT,researchers BIGINT,coauthorEdges BIGINT," +
    70	      "sumJointPublicationCount BIGINT,components BIGINT,largestComponentSize BIGINT," +
    71	      "singletonResearchers BIGINT,communities BIGINT,largestCommunitySize BIGINT," +
    72	      "singletonCommunities BIGINT,communitiesAtLeastMinSize BIGINT,largestComponentId STRING," +
    73	      "largestCommunityId STRING,years INT,largestComponentFraction DOUBLE," +
    74	      "largestCommunityFraction DOUBLE,communityMinSize INT,communityConverged BOOLEAN," +
    75	      "communityRounds INT,nodeBasis STRING,sourceRuns STRUCT<temporalSummaryExportRunId: STRING, " +
    76	      "communityPathRunId: STRING, researcherAnalyticsRunId: STRING, canonicalGraphRunId: STRING, " +
    77	      "inputHadoopRunId: STRING, matchPolicyVersion: STRING>,provenance STRUCT<schemaVersion: " +
    78	      "STRING, sourceDataset: STRING, sourceRecordId: STRING, snapshot: STRING, runId: STRING, " +
    79	      "policyVersion: STRING, modelVersion: STRING>,schemaVersion STRING"
    80	
    81	  /** Top-level fields of a line: exactly the Spark columns, so a new or missing column is refused.
    82	    */
    83	  val Fields: Set[String] = SparkSchema
    84	    .replaceAll("<[^>]*>", "")
    85	    .split(',')
    86	    .map(_.trim.takeWhile(_ != ' '))
    87	    .toSet
    88	
    89	  private val NullableFields = Vector(
    90	    "largestComponentId",
    91	    "largestCommunityId",
    92	    "largestComponentFraction",
    93	    "largestCommunityFraction"
    94	  )
    95	  private val ComponentId = "comp-[0-9a-f]{32}".r
    96	
    97	  /** Rule violations of one row; empty means valid. */
    98	  def problems(s: WindowSummary, windows: Map[String, TimeWindow]): List[String] =
    99	    def check(ok: Boolean, msg: => String) = if ok then Nil else List(msg)
   100	    def fraction(size: Long, f: Option[Double], name: String) =
   101	      check(
   102	        f.exists(x => math.abs(x - size.toDouble / s.researchers.toDouble) <= 1e-12),
   103	        s"$name is not its size / researchers"
   104	      )
   105	    val p = s.provenance
   106	    val r = s.sourceRuns
   107	    val counts = Vector(
   108	      s.researchers,
   109	      s.coauthorEdges,
   110	      s.sumJointPublicationCount,
   111	      s.components,
   112	      s.largestComponentSize,
   113	      s.singletonResearchers,
   114	      s.communities,
   115	      s.largestCommunitySize,
   116	      s.singletonCommunities,
   117	      s.communitiesAtLeastMinSize
   118	    )
   119	    check(s.schemaVersion == SchemaVersion, s"schemaVersion ${s.schemaVersion}") ++
   120	      (windows.get(s.window) match
   121	        case None    => List(s"unknown window ${s.window}")
   122	        case Some(w) =>
   123	          check(
   124	            s.windowStartYear == w.startYear && s.windowEndYear == w.endYear,
   125	            s"window years differ from ${w.name}"
   126	          )) ++
   127	      check(s.years == s.windowEndYear - s.windowStartYear + 1, "years is not end - start + 1") ++
   128	      check(counts.forall(_ >= 0), "a count is negative") ++
   129	      check(
   130	        s.components <= s.researchers && s.communities <= s.researchers &&
   131	          s.singletonResearchers <= s.components && s.singletonCommunities <= s.communities &&
   132	          s.communitiesAtLeastMinSize <= s.communities &&
   133	          s.coauthorEdges <= s.sumJointPublicationCount,
   134	        "counts are inconsistent"
   135	      ) ++
   136	      (if s.researchers == 0 then
   137	         check(
   138	           s.largestComponentId.isEmpty && s.largestCommunityId.isEmpty &&
   139	             s.largestComponentFraction.isEmpty && s.largestCommunityFraction.isEmpty &&
   140	             s.largestComponentSize == 0 && s.largestCommunitySize == 0,
   141	           "an empty window has a largest component or community"
   142	         )
   143	       else
   144	         check(s.largestComponentId.exists(ComponentId.matches), "largestComponentId") ++
   145	           check(s.largestCommunityId.exists(IdFormat.isCommunityId), "largestCommunityId") ++
   146	           check(
   147	             s.largestComponentSize >= 1 && s.largestComponentSize <= s.researchers &&
   148	               s.largestCommunitySize >= 1 && s.largestCommunitySize <= s.researchers,
   149	             "a largest size is outside 1 to researchers"
   150	           ) ++
   151	           fraction(
   152	             s.largestComponentSize,
   153	             s.largestComponentFraction,
   154	             "largestComponentFraction"
   155	           ) ++
   156	           fraction(
   157	             s.largestCommunitySize,
   158	             s.largestCommunityFraction,
   159	             "largestCommunityFraction"
   160	           )) ++
   161	      check(s.communityMinSize >= 1 && s.communityRounds >= 0, "communityMinSize or rounds") ++
   162	      check(s.nodeBasis.trim.nonEmpty, "nodeBasis is empty") ++
   163	      check(
   164	        Vector(
   165	          r.temporalSummaryExportRunId,
   166	          r.communityPathRunId,
   167	          r.researcherAnalyticsRunId,
   168	          r.canonicalGraphRunId,
   169	          r.inputHadoopRunId
   170	        ).forall(IdFormat.isRunId) && IdFormat.Version.matches(r.matchPolicyVersion),
   171	        "sourceRuns"
   172	      ) ++
   173	      check(
   174	        p.schemaVersion == SchemaVersions.Provenance,
   175	        s"provenance.schemaVersion ${p.schemaVersion}"
   176	      ) ++
   177	      check(p.sourceDataset == SourceType.DERIVED, "provenance.sourceDataset is not DERIVED") ++
   178	      check(p.sourceRecordId == s.window, "provenance.sourceRecordId is not the window") ++
   179	      check(IdFormat.isRunId(p.runId), "provenance.runId") ++
   180	      check(ProvenanceRules.Snapshot.matches(p.snapshot), "provenance.snapshot") ++
   181	      check(p.policyVersion.exists(IdFormat.Version.matches), "provenance.policyVersion") ++
   182	      check(p.modelVersion.isEmpty, "provenance.modelVersion must be null")
   183	
   184	  /** The store object: one JSON line per row, ordered by start year, end year, window. */
   185	  def render(rows: Seq[WindowSummary]): Array[Byte] =
   186	    rows
   187	      .sortBy(s => (s.windowStartYear, s.windowEndYear, s.window))
   188	      .map(write(_) + "\n")
   189	      .mkString
   190	      .getBytes(UTF_8)
   191	
   192	  /** Validates store-object lines (`source` names them in violations). Every line must be exactly
   193	    * the Spark columns and pass `problems`; a window appears once; every configured window is
   194	    * present; all rows come from one provenance run and snapshot. Any violation refuses all rows.
   195	    */
   196	  def parseRows(
   197	      source: String,
   198	      bytes: Array[Byte],
   199	      windows: Seq[TimeWindow]
   200	  ): Either[Vector[Violation], Vector[WindowSummary]] =
   201	    val byWindow = ProvenanceRules.windowsByName(windows)
   202	    val out = Vector.newBuilder[Violation]
   203	    val rows = Vector.newBuilder[WindowSummary]
   204	    new String(bytes, UTF_8).split('\n').iterator.zipWithIndex.foreach { (l, i) =>
   205	      if l.trim.nonEmpty then
   206	        Try(ujson.read(l)).toEither.left.map(e => s"not JSON: ${e.getMessage}").flatMap {
   207	          case o: ujson.Obj if o.value.keySet == Fields =>
   208	            Try(read[WindowSummary](o)).toEither.left.map(e => s"bad value: ${e.getMessage}")
   209	          case o: ujson.Obj =>
   210	            val extra = (o.value.keySet.toSet -- Fields).toVector.sorted.mkString(" ")
   211	            val missing = (Fields -- o.value.keySet.toSet).toVector.sorted.mkString(" ")
   212	            Left(s"fields differ from $SchemaVersion: extra [$extra], missing [$missing]")
   213	          case _ => Left("line is not a JSON object")
   214	        } match
   215	          case Left(e)  => out += Violation(source, i + 1L, "MALFORMED", e.take(200))
   216	          case Right(s) =>
   217	            problems(s, byWindow).foreach(p => out += Violation(source, i + 1L, "BAD_ROW", p))
   218	            rows += s
   219	    }
   220	    val got = rows.result()
   221	    got.groupBy(_.window).toVector.sortBy(_._1).foreach { (w, xs) =>
   222	      if xs.size > 1 then out += Violation(source, 0, "DUPLICATE_WINDOW", w)
   223	    }
   224	    windows.map(_.name).filterNot(got.map(_.window).toSet).foreach { w =>
   225	      out += Violation(source, 0, "MISSING_WINDOW", w)
   226	    }
   227	    val runs = got.map(s => (s.provenance.runId, s.provenance.snapshot)).distinct
   228	    if runs.size > 1 then out += Violation(source, 0, "PROVENANCE", s"rows from ${runs.size} runs")
   229	    val found = out.result()
   230	    Either.cond(found.isEmpty, got, found)
   231	
   232	  /** parseRows for a store object, plus the key rules: a valid key under KeyPrefix whose snapshot
   233	    * is the rows' snapshot.
   234	    */
   235	  def parse(
   236	      key: String,
   237	      bytes: Array[Byte],
   238	      windows: Seq[TimeWindow]
   239	  ): Either[Vector[Violation], Vector[WindowSummary]] =
   240	    val keyIssues = (S3Layout.keyProblems(key) ++
   241	      Option.when(!key.startsWith(KeyPrefix))(s"key is not under $KeyPrefix"))
   242	      .map(Violation(key, 0, "BAD_KEY", _))
   243	      .toVector
   244	    parseRows(key, bytes, windows) match
   245	      case Left(vs)   => Left(keyIssues ++ vs)
   246	      case Right(got) =>
   247	        val snapshotIssue = got.headOption
   248	          .map(_.provenance.snapshot)
   249	          .filterNot(snap => key.contains(s"/snapshot=$snap/"))
   250	          .map(snap =>
   251	            Violation(key, 0, "PROVENANCE", s"key snapshot differs from the rows' $snap")
   252	          )
   253	        val found = keyIssues ++ snapshotIssue
   254	        Either.cond(found.isEmpty, got, found)
   255	
   256	  /** Reads and validates the store object once, then answers from memory. The fake is a
   257	    * FileObjectStore reader and the live adapter S3ObjectStore's reader (one GetObject). Both
   258	    * stores are write-once, so the copy never goes stale.
   259	    */
   260	  def open(
   261	      objects: ObjectReadPort,
   262	      key: String,
   263	      windows: Seq[TimeWindow]
   264	  ): Either[Vector[Violation], WindowSummaryReadPort] =
   265	    objects
   266	      .get(key)
   267	      .toRight(Vector(Violation(key, 0, "OBJECT_MISSING", "no window-summary object")))
   268	      .flatMap(parse(key, _, windows))
   269	      .map { rows =>
   270	        val byName = rows.map(s => s.window -> s).toMap
   271	        (window: String) => byName.get(window)
   272	      }
   273	
   274	  val SparkManifestSchema = "spark-run-manifest-v1"
   275	
   276	  /** Store-object rows from a Spark CorpusWindowSummaryJob run manifest (`_manifest.json`). Its
   277	    * `counts.windowSummary` holds every column except nodeBasis, sourceRuns and provenance. Those
   278	    * come from the manifest's `settings.nodeBasis` and `sourceRuns`, and provenance is rebuilt as
   279	    * the job writes it (spark Canonical.provenance: provenance-v1, DERIVED, the window, the run's
   280	    * snapshot and runId, `settings.graphPolicy`, no model). The manifest must list the table with
   281	    * SparkSchema, SchemaVersion, and its row count, and report no integrity violation.
   282	    */
   283	  // shortcut: storage has no Parquet reader, so it reads the manifest copy of the 3 rows; drop
   284	  // this once spark-engineer writes the store object directly (RUNTIME.md F2).
   285	  def fromSparkManifest(
   286	      source: String,
   287	      m: ujson.Value,
   288	      windows: Seq[TimeWindow]
   289	  ): Either[Vector[Violation], Vector[WindowSummary]] =
   290	    def bad(code: String, e: String) = Vector(Violation(source, 0, code, e))
   291	    Try {
   292	      val rows = m("counts")("windowSummary").arr.toVector
   293	      val tableOk = m("tables").arr.exists(t =>
   294	        t("name").str == SparkTable && t("schemaVersion").str == SchemaVersion &&
   295	          t("schema").str == SparkSchema && t("rowCount").num.toLong == rows.size
   296	      )
   297	      val header = Vector(
   298	        Option.when(m("schemaVersion").str != SparkManifestSchema)("manifest schemaVersion"),
   299	        Option.when(m("stage").str != SparkStage)(s"stage is not $SparkStage"),
   300	        Option.when(!tableOk)(s"no $SparkTable table with $SchemaVersion, its schema and row count")
   301	      ).flatten
   302	      val violations = m("counts")("integrityViolations").arr.size
   303	      if header.nonEmpty then Left(header.flatMap(bad("BAD_MANIFEST", _)))
   304	      else if violations > 0 then Left(bad("INTEGRITY_VIOLATIONS", s"the run reports $violations"))
   305	      else
   306	        val runId = m("runId").str
   307	        val snapshot = m("snapshot").str
   308	        Right(rows.map { r =>
   309	          val o = ujson.Obj.from(r.obj)
   310	          NullableFields.foreach(k => if !o.value.contains(k) then o(k) = ujson.Null)
   311	          o("nodeBasis") = m("settings")("nodeBasis").str
   312	          o("sourceRuns") = m("sourceRuns")
   313	          o("provenance") = ujson.Obj(
   314	            "schemaVersion" -> SchemaVersions.Provenance,
   315	            "sourceDataset" -> SourceType.DERIVED.toString,
   316	            "sourceRecordId" -> o.value.getOrElse("window", ujson.Null),
   317	            "snapshot" -> snapshot,
   318	            "runId" -> runId,
   319	            "policyVersion" -> m("settings")("graphPolicy").str,
   320	            "modelVersion" -> ujson.Null
   321	          )
   322	          o.render()
   323	        })
   324	    }.toEither.left
   325	      .map(e => bad("BAD_MANIFEST", s"unreadable manifest: ${e.getMessage}"))
   326	      .flatMap(identity)
   327	      .flatMap(lines => parseRows(source, lines.mkString("\n").getBytes(UTF_8), windows))
```

### modules/storage/src/main/scala/scholarchain/storage/cli/LoadWindowSummaryCli.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.storage.cli
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.core.StableIds
     4	import scholarchain.storage.*
     5	
     6	import java.nio.file.{Files, Paths}
     7	import scala.util.Try
     8	
     9	/** Converts one Spark CorpusWindowSummaryJob run into the window-summary store object in the file
    10	  * object store `<out>/s3` (STORAGE.md, "Window summaries"), write-once, with a run manifest, then
    11	  * reopens it through the read port. Reads the Spark run only. No live mode: LoadS3Cli copies the
    12	  * manifest's objects to the bucket.
    13	  */
    14	object LoadWindowSummaryCli:
    15	  val Stage = "load-window-summary"
    16	  val CodeVersion = "load-window-summary-v1"
    17	  val UsageText =
    18	    "storage/runMain scholarchain.storage.cli.LoadWindowSummaryCli " +
    19	      "--spark-run <out>/snapshot=<d>/run=<spark run> --out <new root, e.g. scratch/runtime/wo8>"
    20	
    21	  def main(args: Array[String]): Unit = Cli.main("LoadWindowSummaryCli", args)(run(_, _))
    22	
    23	  def run(args: Seq[String], out: String => Unit, env: Map[String, String] = sys.env): Int =
    24	    Cli.withArgs(args, Set.empty, Set("spark-run", "out"), UsageText, out, env) { (a, cfg) =>
    25	      for
    26	        dir <- a.req("spark-run").map(Paths.get(_))
    27	        root <- a.req("out").map(Paths.get(_))
    28	        segs <- Cli.runSegments(dir).toRight(s"$dir has no snapshot=/run= segments")
    29	        snapshot = segs._1
    30	        sparkRun = segs._2
    31	        manifestBytes <- Try(Files.readAllBytes(dir.resolve("_manifest.json"))).toEither.left
    32	          .map(e => s"cannot read $dir/_manifest.json: ${e.getMessage}")
    33	        manifest <- Try(ujson.read(manifestBytes)).toEither.left.map(e => s"not JSON: $e")
    34	        _ <- Either.cond(
    35	          Try((manifest("snapshot").str, manifest("runId").str)).toOption
    36	            .contains((snapshot, sparkRun)),
    37	          (),
    38	          s"the manifest's snapshot and runId differ from the path segments of $dir"
    39	        )
    40	        _ <- Cli.freeDiskProblem(root).toLeft(())
    41	      yield
    42	        val manifestHash = Hashing.sha256(manifestBytes)
    43	        WindowSummaries.fromSparkManifest(
    44	          dir.resolve("_manifest.json").toString,
    45	          manifest,
    46	          cfg.graph.windows
    47	        ) match
    48	          case Left(vs) =>
    49	            Cli.report(vs, 5, out)
    50	            1
    51	          case Right(rows) =>
    52	            val runId = StableIds.runId(
    53	              Stage,
    54	              CodeVersion,
    55	              cfg.configVersion,
    56	              Seq(s"source=$sparkRun", manifestHash),
    57	              1
    58	            )
    59	            out(s"load run $runId from spark run $sparkRun snapshot $snapshot")
    60	            val store = FileObjectStore(root.resolve("s3"))
    61	            val key = S3Layout.windowSummary(snapshot, runId)
    62	            val bytes = WindowSummaries.render(rows)
    63	            val runManifest = RunManifest(
    64	              RunManifest.SchemaVersion,
    65	              Stage,
    66	              runId,
    67	              snapshot,
    68	              Vector(
    69	                ManifestEntry(
    70	                  key,
    71	                  Hashing.sha256(bytes),
    72	                  bytes.length.toLong,
    73	                  s"spark ${WindowSummaries.SparkTable} run=$sparkRun _manifest.json $manifestHash"
    74	                )
    75	              )
    76	            )
    77	            val manifestKey = S3Layout.runManifest(Stage, snapshot, runId)
    78	            val outcomes = Vector(
    79	              store.loader.putNew(key, bytes),
    80	              store.loader.putNew(manifestKey, RunManifest.toJson(runManifest))
    81	            )
    82	            def shown(o: Either[List[String], PutObjectOutcome]) =
    83	              o.fold(e => s"Invalid ${e.mkString("; ")}", _.toString)
    84	            out(s"object $key ${shown(outcomes(0))}")
    85	            out(s"manifest $manifestKey ${shown(outcomes(1))}")
    86	            val reopened = WindowSummaries.open(store.reader, key, cfg.graph.windows)
    87	            val problems =
    88	              RunManifest.verify(runManifest, store.reader) ++ reopened.left.getOrElse(Vector.empty)
    89	            reopened.foreach { port =>
    90	              cfg.graph.windows.flatMap(w => port.windowSummary(w.name)).foreach { s =>
    91	                out(
    92	                  s"window ${s.window} researchers ${s.researchers} components ${s.components} " +
    93	                    s"largestComponentFraction ${s.largestComponentFraction.getOrElse("null")} " +
    94	                    s"singletonResearchers ${s.singletonResearchers} communitiesAtLeastMinSize " +
    95	                    s"${s.communitiesAtLeastMinSize} (min ${s.communityMinSize}) runId ${s.provenance.runId}"
    96	                )
    97	              }
    98	            }
    99	            Cli.report(problems, 5, out)
   100	            if problems.isEmpty && outcomes.forall(
   101	                _.exists(!_.isInstanceOf[PutObjectOutcome.Conflict])
   102	              )
   103	            then 0
   104	            else 1
   105	    }
```

### modules/storage/src/main/scala/scholarchain/storage/live/ServiceLive.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.storage.live
     2	
     3	import scholarchain.core.{IdFormat, JobState}
     4	import scholarchain.storage.*
     5	import software.amazon.awssdk.awscore.AwsRequestOverrideConfiguration
     6	import software.amazon.awssdk.services.dynamodb.DynamoDbClient
     7	import software.amazon.awssdk.services.dynamodb.model.{
     8	  AttributeValue as Sdk,
     9	  ConditionalCheckFailedException,
    10	  PutItemRequest,
    11	  QueryRequest,
    12	  ReturnValuesOnConditionCheckFailure
    13	}
    14	import software.amazon.awssdk.services.sqs.SqsClient
    15	import software.amazon.awssdk.services.sqs.model.{
    16	  ChangeMessageVisibilityRequest,
    17	  DeleteMessageRequest,
    18	  Message,
    19	  MessageNotInflightException,
    20	  MessageSystemAttributeName,
    21	  ReceiptHandleIsInvalidException,
    22	  ReceiveMessageRequest,
    23	  SendMessageRequest
    24	}
    25	
    26	import java.time.{Duration, Instant}
    27	import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*
    28	
    29	private def jitter(ms: Long): Unit = Thread.sleep(ms / 2 + scala.util.Random.nextLong(ms / 2 + 1))
    30	
    31	/** DynamoDB requests of the ServiceState table and the EvidenceBundle evidenceById index. */
    32	object ServiceStateRequests:
    33	  private def str(s: String) = Sdk.builder().s(s).build()
    34	  private def num(n: Long) = Sdk.builder().n(n.toString).build()
    35	
    36	  /** Put when no item has the key or the stored one has expired (expiresAt <= now); on failure
    37	    * DynamoDB returns the stored item.
    38	    */
    39	  def putIfAbsent(table: String, item: Item, nowEpochSeconds: Long): PutItemRequest =
    40	    PutItemRequest
    41	      .builder()
    42	      .tableName(table)
    43	      .item(DynamoCodec.toSdkItem(item))
    44	      .conditionExpression("attribute_not_exists(#pk) OR #e <= :now")
    45	      .expressionAttributeNames(
    46	        Map(
    47	          "#pk" -> DynamoContract.ServiceState.partitionKey,
    48	          "#e" -> DynamoContract.TtlAttribute
    49	        ).asJava
    50	      )
    51	      .expressionAttributeValues(Map(":now" -> num(nowEpochSeconds)).asJava)
    52	      .returnValuesOnConditionCheckFailure(ReturnValuesOnConditionCheckFailure.ALL_OLD)
    53	      .build()
    54	
    55	  /** Put only when the stored job is in state expected; on failure the stored item comes back. */
    56	  def putIfState(table: String, item: Item, expected: JobState): PutItemRequest =
    57	    PutItemRequest
    58	      .builder()
    59	      .tableName(table)
    60	      .item(DynamoCodec.toSdkItem(item))
    61	      .conditionExpression("#s = :from")
    62	      .expressionAttributeNames(Map("#s" -> "state").asJava)
    63	      .expressionAttributeValues(Map(":from" -> str(expected.toString)).asJava)
    64	      .returnValuesOnConditionCheckFailure(ReturnValuesOnConditionCheckFailure.ALL_OLD)
    65	      .build()
    66	
    67	  /** Items evaluated per evidence lookup page (shortcut: one page; enough unless that many expired
    68	    * bundles, not yet deleted by TTL, hold the same evidence ID).
    69	    */
    70	  val EvidenceLookupLimit = 100
    71	
    72	  /** evidenceById: itemKey = EV#<evidenceId>, unexpired, smallest queryId first. A GSI read is
    73	    * eventually consistent.
    74	    */
    75	  def evidenceById(table: String, evidenceId: String, nowEpochSeconds: Long): QueryRequest =
    76	    QueryRequest
    77	      .builder()
    78	      .tableName(table)
    79	      .indexName(DynamoContract.EvidenceByIdIndex)
    80	      .keyConditionExpression("#k = :k")
    81	      .filterExpression("#e > :now")
    82	      .expressionAttributeNames(Map("#k" -> "itemKey", "#e" -> DynamoContract.TtlAttribute).asJava)
    83	      .expressionAttributeValues(
    84	        Map(":k" -> str(s"EV#$evidenceId"), ":now" -> num(nowEpochSeconds)).asJava
    85	      )
    86	      .scanIndexForward(true)
    87	      .limit(EvidenceLookupLimit)
    88	      .build()
    89	
    90	/** Live ServiceState table (physical name tablePrefix + "ServiceState"). get is a consistent
    91	  * GetItem, retried by policy (services.resilience; reads only, ruling 9). The two conditional puts
    92	  * are single attempts: a TransientStoreException from one leaves the outcome unknown, so read the
    93	  * record back before deciding.
    94	  */
    95	final class DynamoServiceStateTable(
    96	    client: DynamoDbClient,
    97	    tablePrefix: String,
    98	    now: () => Instant,
    99	    policy: RetryPolicy,
   100	    sleep: Long => Unit = jitter
   101	) extends ServiceStateTable:
   102	  private val spec = DynamoContract.ServiceState
   103	  private val table = tablePrefix + TableName.ServiceState.toString
   104	  private def decode(m: java.util.Map[String, Sdk]): Item =
   105	    DynamoCodec.fromSdkItem(m).fold(e => throw IllegalStateException(e), identity)
   106	
   107	  def get(recordKey: String, recordType: String): Option[Item] =
   108	    val r = Retry
   109	      .run(policy, sleep)(
   110	        LiveAws.guarded(
   111	          client.getItem(DynamoRequests.get(table, spec, recordKey, recordType))
   112	        )
   113	      )
   114	      ._1
   115	    Option
   116	      .when(r.hasItem && !r.item.isEmpty)(decode(r.item))
   117	      .filter(DynamoContract.unexpired(spec, _, now().getEpochSecond))
   118	
   119	  def putIfAbsent(item: Item): Either[Item, Unit] =
   120	    try
   121	      LiveAws.guarded(
   122	        client.putItem(
   123	          ServiceStateRequests.putIfAbsent(
   124	            table,
   125	            AttributeValue.canonicalItem(item),
   126	            now().getEpochSecond
   127	          )
   128	        )
   129	      )
   130	      Right(())
   131	    catch case e: ConditionalCheckFailedException if e.hasItem => Left(decode(e.item))
   132	
   133	  def putIfState(item: Item, expected: JobState): Either[Option[Item], Unit] =
   134	    try
   135	      LiveAws.guarded(
   136	        client.putItem(
   137	          ServiceStateRequests.putIfState(table, AttributeValue.canonicalItem(item), expected)
   138	        )
   139	      )
   140	      Right(())
   141	    catch
   142	      case e: ConditionalCheckFailedException =>
   143	        Left(Option.when(e.hasItem && !e.item.isEmpty)(decode(e.item)))
   144	
   145	/** Live EvidenceLookupPort over the evidenceById GSI of tablePrefix + "EvidenceBundle". Retried by
   146	  * policy (a read).
   147	  */
   148	final class DynamoEvidenceLookup(
   149	    client: DynamoDbClient,
   150	    tablePrefix: String,
   151	    now: () => Instant,
   152	    policy: RetryPolicy,
   153	    sleep: Long => Unit = jitter
   154	) extends EvidenceLookupPort:
   155	  private val table = tablePrefix + TableName.EvidenceBundle.toString
   156	
   157	  def evidenceItem(evidenceId: String): Option[Item] =
   158	    if !IdFormat.isEvidenceId(evidenceId) then None
   159	    else
   160	      val t = now().getEpochSecond
   161	      Retry
   162	        .run(policy, sleep)(
   163	          LiveAws.guarded(client.query(ServiceStateRequests.evidenceById(table, evidenceId, t)))
   164	        )
   165	        ._1
   166	        .items
   167	        .asScala
   168	        .iterator
   169	        .map(m => DynamoCodec.fromSdkItem(m).fold(e => throw IllegalStateException(e), identity))
   170	        .find(DynamoContract.unexpired(DynamoContract.EvidenceBundle, _, t))
   171	
   172	/** SQS requests. Every request carries apiCallTimeout = timeoutMs (services.timeouts.sqs-ms); a
   173	  * receive adds its long-poll wait, so the HTTP call outlives WaitTimeSeconds.
   174	  */
   175	object SqsRequests:
   176	  private def timeout(ms: Long) =
   177	    AwsRequestOverrideConfiguration.builder().apiCallTimeout(Duration.ofMillis(ms)).build()
   178	
   179	  def send(queueUrl: String, body: String, timeoutMs: Long): SendMessageRequest =
   180	    SendMessageRequest
   181	      .builder()
   182	      .queueUrl(queueUrl)
   183	      .messageBody(body)
   184	      .overrideConfiguration(timeout(timeoutMs))
   185	      .build()
   186	
   187	  def receive(
   188	      queueUrl: String,
   189	      maxMessages: Int,
   190	      waitSeconds: Int,
   191	      visibilitySeconds: Int,
   192	      timeoutMs: Long
   193	  ): ReceiveMessageRequest =
   194	    JobQueueLimits.check(maxMessages, waitSeconds, visibilitySeconds)
   195	    ReceiveMessageRequest
   196	      .builder()
   197	      .queueUrl(queueUrl)
   198	      .maxNumberOfMessages(maxMessages)
   199	      .waitTimeSeconds(waitSeconds)
   200	      .visibilityTimeout(visibilitySeconds)
   201	      .messageSystemAttributeNames(MessageSystemAttributeName.APPROXIMATE_RECEIVE_COUNT)
   202	      .overrideConfiguration(timeout(timeoutMs + waitSeconds * 1000L))
   203	      .build()
   204	
   205	  def delete(queueUrl: String, handle: String, timeoutMs: Long): DeleteMessageRequest =
   206	    DeleteMessageRequest
   207	      .builder()
   208	      .queueUrl(queueUrl)
   209	      .receiptHandle(handle)
   210	      .overrideConfiguration(timeout(timeoutMs))
   211	      .build()
   212	
   213	  def changeVisibility(
   214	      queueUrl: String,
   215	      handle: String,
   216	      seconds: Int,
   217	      timeoutMs: Long
   218	  ): ChangeMessageVisibilityRequest =
   219	    JobQueueLimits.visibility(seconds)
   220	    ChangeMessageVisibilityRequest
   221	      .builder()
   222	      .queueUrl(queueUrl)
   223	      .receiptHandle(handle)
   224	      .visibilityTimeout(seconds)
   225	      .overrideConfiguration(timeout(timeoutMs))
   226	      .build()
   227	
   228	  /** ApproximateReceiveCount, or 1 when SQS left it out. */
   229	  def received(m: Message): ReceivedJob =
   230	    val count = Option(m.attributes.get(MessageSystemAttributeName.APPROXIMATE_RECEIVE_COUNT))
   231	      .flatMap(_.toIntOption)
   232	      .getOrElse(1)
   233	    ReceivedJob(m.messageId, m.receiptHandle, count, JobMessage.parse(m.body))
   234	
   235	/** Live JobQueuePort over one SQS standard queue (services.queue.url). The client comes from
   236	  * LiveAws.sqs (the shared RefreshingCredentials of the profile, SDK retries off). No call is
   237	  * retried here (ruling 9 retries reads only, and none of these is a pure read): the worker's poll
   238	  * loop is the retry, and the job store makes a duplicate send or delivery harmless. The redrive
   239	  * policy (max-receive-count, dead-letter queue) belongs to the queue, created by Terraform.
   240	  */
   241	final class SqsJobQueue(client: SqsClient, queueUrl: String, timeoutMs: Long) extends JobQueuePort:
   242	  require(timeoutMs >= 1, s"timeout $timeoutMs ms")
   243	
   244	  def send(message: JobMessage): String =
   245	    LiveAws
   246	      .guarded(
   247	        client.sendMessage(SqsRequests.send(queueUrl, JobMessage.render(message), timeoutMs))
   248	      )
   249	      .messageId
   250	
   251	  def receive(maxMessages: Int, waitSeconds: Int, visibilitySeconds: Int): Vector[ReceivedJob] =
   252	    val r = SqsRequests.receive(queueUrl, maxMessages, waitSeconds, visibilitySeconds, timeoutMs)
   253	    LiveAws.guarded(client.receiveMessage(r)).messages.asScala.map(SqsRequests.received).toVector
   254	
   255	  /** SQS answers success for an old handle without deleting (DeleteMessage); only an invalid handle
   256	    * gives false.
   257	    */
   258	  def delete(receiptHandle: String): Boolean =
   259	    try
   260	      LiveAws.guarded(client.deleteMessage(SqsRequests.delete(queueUrl, receiptHandle, timeoutMs)))
   261	      true
   262	    catch case _: ReceiptHandleIsInvalidException => false
   263	
   264	  def changeVisibility(receiptHandle: String, seconds: Int): Boolean =
   265	    try
   266	      LiveAws.guarded(
   267	        client.changeMessageVisibility(
   268	          SqsRequests.changeVisibility(queueUrl, receiptHandle, seconds, timeoutMs)
   269	        )
   270	      )
   271	      true
   272	    catch case _: ReceiptHandleIsInvalidException | _: MessageNotInflightException => false
```

### modules/storage/src/test/scala/scholarchain/storage/JobQueueSpec.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.storage
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.StableIds
     5	import scholarchain.storage.live.*
     6	import software.amazon.awssdk.services.sqs.SqsClient
     7	import software.amazon.awssdk.services.sqs.model.{
     8	  ChangeMessageVisibilityRequest,
     9	  ChangeMessageVisibilityResponse,
    10	  DeleteMessageRequest,
    11	  DeleteMessageResponse,
    12	  Message,
    13	  MessageNotInflightException,
    14	  MessageSystemAttributeName,
    15	  ReceiptHandleIsInvalidException,
    16	  ReceiveMessageRequest,
    17	  ReceiveMessageResponse,
    18	  SendMessageRequest,
    19	  SendMessageResponse
    20	}
    21	
    22	import java.time.{Duration, Instant}
    23	import scala.jdk.CollectionConverters.*
    24	
    25	/** Phase 10 work order 4: the job queue port, its in-memory fake, and the live SQS adapter against
    26	  * a stub client (no network).
    27	  */
    28	class JobQueueSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
    29	  private val q1 = "qry-" + "1" * 32
    30	  private val q2 = "qry-" + "2" * 32
    31	  private var t = Instant.parse("2026-11-12T00:00:00Z")
    32	  private val now = () => t
    33	
    34	  test("job message: only the job and query IDs; anything else is a poison message") {
    35	    val m = JobMessage.of(q1)
    36	    assert(m.jobId == StableIds.jobId(q1))
    37	    val body = JobMessage.render(m)
    38	    assert(ujson.read(body).obj.keySet == Set("schemaVersion", "jobId", "queryId"))
    39	    assert(JobMessage.parse(body) == Right(m))
    40	    val bad = Seq(
    41	      "not json",
    42	      "[]",
    43	      ujson.Obj("schemaVersion" -> "job-message-v0", "jobId" -> m.jobId, "queryId" -> q1).render(),
    44	      ujson
    45	        .Obj("schemaVersion" -> "job-message-v1", "jobId" -> StableIds.jobId(q2), "queryId" -> q1)
    46	        .render(),
    47	      ujson
    48	        .Obj("schemaVersion" -> "job-message-v1", "jobId" -> m.jobId, "queryId" -> q1, "x" -> 1)
    49	        .render()
    50	    )
    51	    bad.foreach(b => assert(JobMessage.parse(b).isLeft, b))
    52	  }
    53	
    54	  test("fake queue: a receive hides the message for the visibility timeout; receives are counted") {
    55	    val queue = InMemoryJobQueue(maxReceiveCount = 3, now)
    56	    val id = queue.send(JobMessage.of(q1))
    57	    val Vector(first) = queue.receive(10, 0, 300): @unchecked
    58	    assert(first.messageId == id && first.receiveCount == 1)
    59	    assert(first.message == Right(JobMessage.of(q1)))
    60	    assert(queue.receive(10, 0, 300).isEmpty) // in flight
    61	    t = t.plusSeconds(299)
    62	    assert(queue.receive(10, 0, 300).isEmpty)
    63	    t = t.plusSeconds(1) // visibility timeout passed: redelivered with a new handle
    64	    val Vector(second) = queue.receive(10, 0, 300): @unchecked
    65	    assert(second.messageId == id && second.receiveCount == 2)
    66	    assert(second.receiptHandle != first.receiptHandle)
    67	    assert(!queue.delete(first.receiptHandle)) // a stale handle deletes nothing
    68	    assert(queue.size == 1)
    69	    assert(queue.delete(second.receiptHandle) && queue.size == 0)
    70	    assert(!queue.delete(second.receiptHandle))
    71	  }
    72	
    73	  test("fake queue: after max-receive-count receives the message moves to the dead-letter queue") {
    74	    val queue = InMemoryJobQueue(maxReceiveCount = 2, now)
    75	    queue.sendRaw("poison")
    76	    queue.send(JobMessage.of(q2))
    77	    val firstRound = queue.receive(10, 0, 30)
    78	    assert(firstRound.map(_.receiveCount) == Vector(1, 1))
    79	    assert(firstRound.head.message.isLeft && firstRound(1).message.isRight)
    80	    queue.delete(firstRound(1).receiptHandle)
    81	    t = t.plusSeconds(30)
    82	    assert(queue.receive(10, 0, 30).map(_.receiveCount) == Vector(2))
    83	    t = t.plusSeconds(30)
    84	    assert(queue.receive(10, 0, 30).isEmpty) // the third receive attempt redrives it
    85	    assert(queue.deadLetters == Vector("poison") && queue.size == 0)
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  test("fake queue: change visibility, bounds, order, and a long poll woken by a send") {
    89	    val queue = InMemoryJobQueue(maxReceiveCount = 3, now)
    90	    queue.send(JobMessage.of(q1))
    91	    queue.send(JobMessage.of(q2))
    92	    val Vector(a) = queue.receive(1, 0, 60): @unchecked
    93	    assert(a.message.map(_.queryId) == Right(q1)) // send order
    94	    assert(queue.changeVisibility(a.receiptHandle, 0)) // visible again now
    95	    val Vector(again) = queue.receive(1, 0, 60): @unchecked
    96	    assert(again.message.map(_.queryId) == Right(q1) && again.receiveCount == 2)
    97	    assert(!queue.changeVisibility(a.receiptHandle, 10)) // stale handle
    98	    t = t.plusSeconds(60)
    99	    assert(!queue.changeVisibility(again.receiptHandle, 10)) // no longer in flight
   100	    assertThrows[IllegalArgumentException](queue.receive(11, 0, 30))
   101	    assertThrows[IllegalArgumentException](queue.receive(1, 21, 30))
   102	    assertThrows[IllegalArgumentException](queue.receive(1, 0, 43201))
   103	    assertThrows[IllegalArgumentException](queue.changeVisibility(again.receiptHandle, -1))
   104	    val empty = InMemoryJobQueue(maxReceiveCount = 3) // real clock for the long poll
   105	    val sender = Thread(() => { Thread.sleep(200); empty.send(JobMessage.of(q1)); () })
   106	    val started = System.nanoTime
   107	    sender.start()
   108	    val got = empty.receive(1, 5, 30)
   109	    val waitedMs = (System.nanoTime - started) / 1000000L
   110	    sender.join()
   111	    assert(got.size == 1 && waitedMs >= 150 && waitedMs < 4000, s"waited $waitedMs ms")
   112	    val polled = System.nanoTime
   113	    assert(empty.receive(1, 1, 30).isEmpty) // the received message is in flight
   114	    assert((System.nanoTime - polled) / 1000000L >= 900)
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  // ---- live SQS against a stub client ----------------------------------------------------------
   118	
   119	  private final class StubSqs extends SqsClient:
   120	    var sent = Vector.empty[SendMessageRequest]
   121	    var received = Vector.empty[ReceiveMessageRequest]
   122	    var deleteError: Option[Exception] = None
   123	    var visibilityError: Option[Exception] = None
   124	    def serviceName(): String = "sqs"
   125	    def close(): Unit = ()
   126	    override def sendMessage(r: SendMessageRequest): SendMessageResponse =
   127	      sent :+= r
   128	      SendMessageResponse.builder().messageId("m-1").build()
   129	    override def receiveMessage(r: ReceiveMessageRequest): ReceiveMessageResponse =
   130	      received :+= r
   131	      val m = Message
   132	        .builder()
   133	        .messageId("m-1")
   134	        .receiptHandle("rh-1")
   135	        .body(JobMessage.render(JobMessage.of(q1)))
   136	        .attributes(Map(MessageSystemAttributeName.APPROXIMATE_RECEIVE_COUNT -> "3").asJava)
   137	        .build()
   138	      val poison = Message.builder().messageId("m-2").receiptHandle("rh-2").body("x").build()
   139	      ReceiveMessageResponse.builder().messages(m, poison).build()
   140	    override def deleteMessage(r: DeleteMessageRequest): DeleteMessageResponse =
   141	      deleteError.foreach(e => throw e)
   142	      DeleteMessageResponse.builder().build()
   143	    override def changeMessageVisibility(
   144	        r: ChangeMessageVisibilityRequest
   145	    ): ChangeMessageVisibilityResponse =
   146	      visibilityError.foreach(e => throw e)
   147	      ChangeMessageVisibilityResponse.builder().build()
   148	
   149	  test("live SQS: requests carry the timeout (plus the long poll), receive counts are parsed") {
   150	    val url = "https://sqs.us-east-2.amazonaws.com/000000000000/example-scholarchain-jobs"
   151	    val r = SqsRequests.receive(url, 1, 20, 300, 5000L)
   152	    assert(r.queueUrl == url && r.maxNumberOfMessages == 1 && r.waitTimeSeconds == 20)
   153	    assert(r.visibilityTimeout == 300)
   154	    assert(
   155	      r.messageSystemAttributeNames.asScala == Seq(
   156	        MessageSystemAttributeName.APPROXIMATE_RECEIVE_COUNT
   157	      )
   158	    )
   159	    assert(r.overrideConfiguration.get.apiCallTimeout.get == Duration.ofMillis(25000L))
   160	    val s = SqsRequests.send(url, "b", 5000L)
   161	    assert(s.overrideConfiguration.get.apiCallTimeout.get == Duration.ofMillis(5000L))
   162	    assert(LiveAws.overridesWithTimeout(2000L).apiCallTimeout.get == Duration.ofMillis(2000L))
   163	    assertThrows[IllegalArgumentException](SqsRequests.receive(url, 1, 21, 300, 5000L))
   164	    assertThrows[IllegalArgumentException](SqsRequests.changeVisibility(url, "h", 43201, 5000L))
   165	
   166	    val stub = StubSqs()
   167	    val queue = SqsJobQueue(stub, url, 5000L)
   168	    assert(queue.send(JobMessage.of(q1)) == "m-1")
   169	    assert(JobMessage.parse(stub.sent.head.messageBody) == Right(JobMessage.of(q1)))
   170	    val Vector(ok, poison) = queue.receive(10, 20, 300): @unchecked
   171	    assert(ok.receiveCount == 3 && ok.message == Right(JobMessage.of(q1)))
   172	    assert(poison.receiveCount == 1 && poison.message.isLeft)
   173	    assert(queue.delete("rh-1") && queue.changeVisibility("rh-1", 0))
   174	    stub.deleteError = Some(ReceiptHandleIsInvalidException.builder().message("bad").build())
   175	    assert(!queue.delete("old"))
   176	    stub.visibilityError = Some(MessageNotInflightException.builder().message("no").build())
   177	    assert(!queue.changeVisibility("rh-1", 10))
   178	  }
```

### modules/storage/src/test/scala/scholarchain/storage/ServiceStateSpec.scala
```
     1	package scholarchain.storage
     2	
     3	import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
     4	import scholarchain.core.{ErrorCode, JobState, StableIds}
     5	import scholarchain.storage.live.*
     6	import software.amazon.awssdk.core.exception.SdkClientException
     7	import software.amazon.awssdk.services.dynamodb.DynamoDbClient
     8	import software.amazon.awssdk.services.dynamodb.model.{
     9	  AttributeValue as Sdk,
    10	  ConditionalCheckFailedException,

    14	  PutItemResponse,
    15	  QueryRequest,

    17	  ReturnValuesOnConditionCheckFailure
    18	}
